Method: CHIS包含两个阶段：1) 结构初始化——将先验掩码的相位信息与高斯噪声的幅度在频域融合，得到结构感知的初始噪声状态；2) 纹理调制——在反向扩散过程中，在不同小波分解层级上自适应调节粗粒度和细粒度纹理，使仅用无标签图像预训练的扩散模型生成与结构掩码对齐、风格匹配的输出。

Result: 大量实验表明CHIS在生成保真度上具有优越性，并对下游分割任务有显著提升效果。

Conclusion: CHIS作为即插即用框架，无需标注数据即可引导预训练扩散模型生成高质量、结构可控的组织病理学图像，有效缓解数据稀缺和标注负担问题。

Abstract: Deep learning has demonstrated remarkable success in high-throughput histopathology image analysis. However, the performance of learning-based models critically depends on the quality and size of annotations by expert pathologists, which is a resource-intensive and time-consuming process. To address the limitations of data scarcity and annotation burden, several methods have been proposed to synthesize paired histopathology data. Nevertheless, these frameworks typically still require annotation data, albeit in reduced quantities, to impose structural constraints during training.
  In this work, we present CHIS, a plug-in framework that guides the sampling trajectory of a pretrained diffusion model through two key stages: structural initialization at the start and textural modulation during generation. The initial noise state is refined by fusing the phase information from a prior mask with the amplitude of Gaussian noise in the frequency domain, yielding a structurally informed starting point. During the reverse diffusion process, we adaptively modulate both coarse-grained and fine-grained textures at different wavelet decomposition levels. This enables a diffusion model pretrained solely on unlabeled images to generate outputs that align with prior structural masks while preserving the reference tissue style.
  We conducted extensive experiments demonstrating the superiority of CHIS in generation fidelity and its substantial benefits for downstream segmentation tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/IBIL-Code/CHIS.

</details>


### [93] [Understanding How MLLMs Describe Artworks Using Token Activation Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27947)
*Nicola Fanelli,Pasquale De Marinis,Raffaele Scaringi,Eva Cetinic,Gennaro Vessio,Giovanna Castellano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Token Activation Map (TAM)方法，用于分析多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在描述艺术作品时的视觉 grounding 机制，发现不同语义类别token的视觉证据定位存在显著差异，并揭示了模型在艺术家归属和标题预测中的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs虽然能流畅描述艺术作品，但其视觉推理过程不透明——模型在命名风格、识别主题或识别图像符号时，究竟是依据画作的相关区域、依赖未区分的视觉信号，还是主要借助文本先验知识，这一问题尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 使用Token Activation Map (TAM)方法，为每个生成的token生成隔离特定视觉证据的热力图；对跨越多个时期和流派的绘画数据集，分析五种语义类别token（常见视觉对象、风格描述符、元数据、图像符号、情感表达）的grounding模式，并与SAM~3开放词汇分割进行对比。

Result: 视觉grounding程度随token语义类别显著变化；模型在艺术家归属任务中准确率高于标题预测，后者幻觉更频繁。

Conclusion: MLLMs的视觉grounding具有语义依赖性，不同类别token依赖不同的视觉证据来源；模型在艺术识别任务中表现不均衡，且TAM可作为分析视觉推理透明度的有效工具。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) describe artworks with remarkable fluency, yet the visual reasoning behind their outputs remains opaque. When an MLLM names a style, identifies a subject, or recognizes an iconographic symbol, does it ground each claim in the relevant region of the canvas, draw on an undifferentiated visual signal, or rely primarily on textual priors? We study this using the Token Activation Map (TAM), which produces, for each generated token, a heatmap isolating the visual evidence specific to that token from prior-context interference. Applying TAM to a curated set of paintings spanning multiple periods and genres, we analyze grounding patterns across five semantically distinct token categories: common visual objects, style descriptors, metadata, iconographic tokens, and affective expressions. We find that visual grounding varies substantially with token semantics. We further show that MLLMs attempt to identify artworks and artists, achieving higher accuracy in artist attribution than in title prediction, where hallucinations are more frequent. Finally, we compare TAM with SAM~3 open-vocabulary segmentation. To ensure reproducibility, we release our code, experimental configurations, prompts, and qualitative results on the project page at https://nicolafan.github.io/tamart/.

</details>


### [94] [Directing the World: Fast Autoregressive Video Generation with Compositional Human-Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27964)
*Haoyuan Wang,Yabo Chen,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"Directing the World"框架，通过解耦控制学习与统一自回归视频先验，结合Fast-Slow Memory训练策略、t引导动态投影和运动CFG策略，实现长程可控的人体运动与相机轨迹视频生成，在保持高视觉质量的同时实现精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成在长程推演中存在误差累积和时间退化问题，且在人体运动与相机轨迹等异构控制下，控制信号可能相互干扰并破坏预训练视频先验，现有方法往往在可控性和视觉质量之间妥协。

Method: 1) Fast-Slow Memory训练策略稳定长程推演学习；2) t引导动态投影机制和精细化Motion-CFG策略实现平滑准确的人体运动控制；3) 两阶段训练：先学习运动先验，再引入相机轨迹控制模块组合人体动态与视角变化；4) 构建大规模多模态标注数据集用于解耦训练。

Result: 实验表明该方法实现了稳定的长程视频生成、精确的可控性和高视觉质量，支持多人控制与视角变化下的连贯世界探索。

Conclusion: 通过解耦控制学习并保留统一的自回归视频先验，可以有效地解决异构控制信号间的干扰问题，实现长程、可控、高质量的世界模型视频生成。

Abstract: Building interactive world models requires generating realistic videos while maintaining controllable dynamics over long horizons. Autoregressive video generation offers a scalable foundation, but suffers from error accumulation and temporal degradation during extended rollouts. This issue is further amplified under heterogeneous controls such as human motion and camera trajectories, which may interfere and destabilize a pretrained video prior, while existing methods often trade off controllability and visual quality. We propose "Directing the World", a fast autoregressive framework for controllable world-model video generation with compositional human-motion and camera-trajectory control. Our key idea is to decouple control learning while preserving a unified autoregressive video prior. We introduce a Fast-Slow Memory training strategy to stabilize long-horizon rollout learning and improve convergence. For human motion control, we design a t-guided Dynamic Projection mechanism and a refined Motion-CFG strategy, enabling temporally smooth and accurate motion alignment without degrading visual fidelity, and supporting multi-person control.After learning a robust motion prior, we introduce a second-stage camera-trajectory control module to compose human dynamics with viewpoint changes for coherent world exploration. We further construct a large-scale dataset with synchronized video, text, human-motion, and camera-trajectory annotations, organized into motion-centric and camera-centric subsets for decoupled training. Extensive experiments show stable long-horizon generation with precise controllability and high visual quality. See more at https://whydahuzi.github.io/Directing-the-World.github.io/.

</details>


### [95] [Latent Visual Diffusion Reasoning with Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27988)
*Xirui Teng,Nan Xi,Junsong Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LVDR框架，结合关键点引导的MCTS，实现动作质量评估中的可解释视觉推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作质量评估模型虽性能出色，但都是黑箱模型，缺乏揭示判断背后推理过程的能力。

Method: 提出Latent Visual Diffusion Reasoning (LVDR)框架，集成关键点引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)来建模和可视化潜在视觉推理过程。

Result: 在涵盖体育和手术领域的四个数据集上，LVDR取得了有竞争力的量化性能，同时提供了可解释的视觉推理轨迹。

Conclusion: LVDR不仅提升了技能评估的准确性，还能揭示导致最终评估结果的关键视觉推理序列，实现了模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Analyzing fine-grained skill activities (e.g., sports, surgery) requires not only recognizing visual patterns but also performing step-by-step visual reasoning that leads to the final judgment. While recent advances in action quality assessment have achieved remarkable progress in evaluating performance, existing models remain black boxes, where they lack the ability to explicitly reveal the reasoning processes underlying their judgments. To address this limitation, we propose Latent Visual Diffusion Reasoning (LVDR), a novel framework that integrates keypoint-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to model and visualize the latent visual reasoning process. LVDR not only produces more accurate skill assessments but also uncovers the critical visual reasoning sequences that contribute to the final evaluation. Extensive experiments across four datasets spanning diverse sports and surgical domains demonstrate that LVDR achieves competitive quantitative performance while providing interpretable visual reasoning trajectories leading to the final predictions. Source codes and models can be found through the following link: https://github.com/XiruiTeng/LVDR_Official.git.

</details>


### [96] [HumanMoveVQA: Can Video MLLMs reason about human movement in videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27999)
*Pulkit Gera,Faegheh Sardari,Asmar Nadeem,Valentina Bono,Padraig Boulton,Adrian Hilton,Armin Mustafa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanMoveVQA，首个从外部视角评估全局人体运动轨迹和方向推理的综合基准。通过将2D视频提升到世界一致3D运动轨迹，生成超1万结构化QA对，覆盖7个推理类别。评估显示现有顶级模型存在能力差距，但通过针对性微调可显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在高层视频理解中，将复杂人体运动简化为粗粒度语义标签；现有基准聚焦场景级事件或局部关节运动，缺乏对全局时空人体运动（轨迹和朝向变化）的评估能力。

Method: 构建以首帧锚定的世界坐标系，保持相对于固定起点的平移和旋转；提出可扩展的多阶段流水线，将2D视频观测提升为世界一致的3D运动轨迹，生成7个推理类别的超1万结构化问答对。

Result: 最先进的闭源模型在深度人体运动理解上存在关键能力差距；通过世界一致的监督信号对开源基线进行微调，取得了显著性能提升。

Conclusion: HumanMoveVQA为开发下一代运动感知视频理解模型建立了严格的几何基础，证明了人体运动推理是一个可学习的问题。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advance of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in high-level video understanding, a fundamental bottleneck remains: these models collapse complex human motion into coarse semantic labels. Existing benchmarks mostly focus on scene-centric events or local joint articulations, failing to probe global human motion in space over time (trajectory and orientation changes). We introduce HumanMoveVQA, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate global trajectory and orientation reasoning from an exocentric perspective. Our benchmark utilizes a first-frame anchored world coordinate system, preserving translation and rotation relative to a fixed starting point. We propose a scalable, multi-stage pipeline that lifts 2D video observations into world-consistent 3D motion tracks to generate over 10K structured question-answer pairs across seven reasoning categories, including motion aggregation, sequential ordering, and trajectory-level inference. Our extensive evaluation reveals a critical capability gap in state-of-the-art proprietary models on deep human motion understanding. However, we demonstrate that this is a learnable problem; by fine-tuning an open-source baseline with our targeted, world-consistent supervision, we achieve a significant improvement.HumanMoveVQA establishes a rigorous geometric foundation for developing next-generation, movement-aware video understanding models.

</details>


### [97] [Curriculum-guided Change Detection Training: Toward Accurate Serac Fall Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28012)
*Arthur Dérédel,Carlos Crispim-Junior,Pierre Lemaire,Johan Berthet,Laure Tougne Rodet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对变化检测的课程学习框架，利用太阳角度差（SAG）和结构相似性（SSIM）两种困难度量，从易到难地组织训练样本，在SeracFallDet基准上一致优于均匀采样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度变化检测方法大多采用均匀采样训练，忽略了不同训练样本对优化的差异性贡献，导致噪声梯度和鲁棒表示学习受限；而半监督学习以外的训练范式尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出课程学习框架，定义两种互补的困难度量：太阳角度差（SAG）作为采集条件变异性的物理代理，SSIM评估图像对的外观相似性；基于这些准则逐步引入困难样本，实现从粗到细的表示学习。

Result: 在SeracFallDet基准上，该方法在基于像素和基于对象的方法上均一致优于标准均匀采样策略。

Conclusion: 课程学习能够提升深度变化检测的鲁棒性，且该框架与现有CD架构正交，可广泛适用。

Abstract: Change Detection (CD) aims to identify semantic or structural changes from nearly registered multi-temporal images. While recent advances in training methodologies have largely focused on semi-supervised learning and consistency regularization, alternative training paradigms remain underexplored. In particular, most deep CD methods rely on uniform sampling during training, implicitly assuming that all training samples contribute equally to the optimization process. However, such naive sampling can introduce noisy gradients and hinder robust representation learning. To address this limitation, we propose a curriculum learning framework tailored for change detection. Our approach investigates two complementary difficulty measures: the Solar Angular Gap (SAG), a physically grounded proxy for acquisition-condition variability, and the Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM), which evaluates appearance similarity between image pairs. Based on these criteria, the framework progressively introduces challenging samples during training, enabling models to learn robust representations in a coarse-to-fine manner. We evaluate our method on the challenging SeracFallDet benchmark, where results demonstrate consistent improvements of the proposed approach over standard uniform-sampling strategies for both pixel-based and object-based approaches. These results highlight the potential of curriculum learning to improve robustness in deep change detection. Importantly, our training framework is orthogonal to existing CD architectures, making it readily applicable to a broad range of methods.

</details>


### [98] [TempAct: Advancing Temporal Plausibility in Autoregressive Video Generation via Planner-Executor RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28016)
*Jing Wang,Xiangxin Zhou,Jiajun Liang,Kaiqi Liu,Wanyun Pang,Zhenyu Xie,Tianyu Pang,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TempAct是一个将大型语言模型作为规划器、自回归视频扩散模型作为执行器的强化学习框架，通过层级化组探索和分层奖励设计，解决自回归视频扩散模型在分块生成时的时间指令遵循问题，包括延迟反应、语义混淆和错误传播等缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型以分块方式生成视频，单个全局提示无法指定每个分块应实现的子事件，而逐步骤提示又会导致延迟反应、步骤语义混淆和提示切换时的错误传播。SFT存在暴露偏差，蒸馏方法优化的是底层去噪或教师分布匹配，而非直接强制执行动作排序和提示转换的正确性。

Method: 提出TempAct规划器-执行器强化学习框架：1) LLM规划器探索可被视频模型执行的span感知步骤提示；2) AR扩散执行器在自身生成的上下文中学习遵循这些提示；3) 层级化组探索——候选计划形成规划组，每个计划在共享视觉上下文中生成多个延续的执行组，实现计划层面和执行器层面的信用分配；4) 分层奖励设计结合了计划质量、全视频时间反馈、局部过渡级步骤跟随奖励、美学正则化和KL约束。

Result: 在Self-Forcing和LongLive上的实验表明，TempAct在不牺牲整体视觉质量的前提下，提升了时间一致性。

Conclusion: 规划器-执行器的强化学习范式能够有效解决自回归视频生成中的时间指令遵循问题，层级化组探索和分层奖励设计是实现长程时间连贯性的关键机制。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models enable low-latency streaming generation by synthesizing videos chunk by chunk with cached visual context, but this chunk-wise formulation makes temporal instruction following ambiguous. A single global prompt does not specify which sub-event should be realized in each chunk, while naively switching to step-wise prompts often leads to delayed reactions, blended step semantics, and error propagation across prompt transitions. These failures are difficult to address with supervised fine-tuning or distillation alone: SFT suffers from exposure bias, while rollout-based distillation still optimizes low-level denoising or teacher-distribution matching rather than directly enforcing action ordering and prompt-transition correctness. We address these challenges with TempAct, a planner--executor reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes temporal decomposition and step-conditioned execution for temporally plausible AR video generation. TempAct uses an LLM planner to explore span-aware step prompts that are executable by the video model, and trains an AR diffusion executor to follow these prompts under its own generated histories. Its key mechanism is hierarchical group exploration: candidate plans form planning groups, and each plan induces an execution group of multiple continuations from a shared visual context, enabling plan-level credit assignment for long-horizon temporal outcomes and executor-level credit assignment for prompt-switch behavior. We further design hierarchical rewards that combine plan-quality and full-video temporal feedback for the planner with local transition-level step-following rewards, aesthetic regularization, and KL constraints for the executor. Experiments on Self-Forcing and LongLive show that TempAct improves temporal consistency while preserving overall visual quality.

</details>


### [99] [EMOSH: Expressive Motion and Shape Disentanglement for Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28026)
*Dongbin Zhang,Hao Liu,Binquan Dai,Kangjie Chen,Chuming Wang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EMOSH提出了一种基于表达性人体模型(EHM)的可控人体视频生成框架，通过解耦形状与姿态参数、粗到细的混合运动注入以及空间对齐条件机制，在保持形状解耦的同时实现了高保真、富有表情的人体动画生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在表现力与解耦性之间存在矛盾：基于2D姿态的方法存在"运动-形状纠缠"，导致驱动主体的体形泄露；而基于3D先验(SMPL)的方法虽能实现几何解耦，但难以捕捉面部表情和复杂手势，生成结果僵硬。

Method: 1) 提出表达性人体模型(EHM)作为核心控制表示，显式解耦形状和姿态参数，并设计鲁棒运动追踪器从视频中估计EHM参数；2) 提出粗到细的混合运动注入策略，实现对表情和手势的细粒度控制；3) 引入空间对齐条件机制，弥合训练与推理之间的域差距，提升身份一致性。

Result: 在自驱动和跨驱动场景下，EMOSH均优于先前方法，生成高保真视频，具有生动的表情，同时保持形状解耦。

Conclusion: EMOSH通过表达性人体模型和混合运动注入策略，有效解决了人体动画生成中表现力与解耦性的权衡问题。

Abstract: High-fidelity and expressive controllable human animation is essential for content creation and digital avatar applications. However, existing methods face a dilemma between expressiveness and disentanglement. Mainstream 2D pose-conditioned approaches suffer from "motion-shape entanglement", leading to the leakage of the driving subject's body shape. Conversely, methods relying on 3D priors (e.g., SMPL) achieve geometric disentanglement but struggle to capture facial expressions and complex gestures, resulting in rigid animations. To this end, we propose EMOSH, a novel framework for high-fidelity controllable human video generation. First, an Expressive Human Model (EHM) is introduced as the core control representation. By explicitly disentangling shape and pose parameters, we fundamentally resolve the body shape leakage issue. Alongside this, a robust motion tracker is designed to accurately estimate EHM parameters from video. Second, we propose a Coarse-to-Fine Hybrid Motion Injection strategy, enabling more fine-grained control over expressions and gestures. Furthermore, we introduce a Spatially-Aligned Conditioning mechanism to bridge the domain gap between training and inference, improving identity consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EMOSH outperforms previous methods in both self-driven and cross-driven scenarios, producing high-fidelity videos with vivid expressions while maintaining shape disentanglement.

</details>


### [100] [AirGroundBench: Probing Spatial Intelligence in Multimodal Large Models under Heterogeneous Multi-View Embodied Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28049)
*Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Leyuan Wang,Jinshan Lai,Keyang Wang,Zonghao Guo,Qiang Ma,Liuyu Xiang,Jianwei Hu,Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AirGroundBench基准，用于评估异构无人机-无人车协作中的多视图空间智能。包含11个高保真环境、约6.2万个双视图问答实例和115个VLN任务，覆盖空间感知、跨视图对齐、空间变换推理和具身决策四个维度。评估13个MLLM发现其在空间感知上表现尚可，但在跨视图对齐和变换推理上存在瓶颈，且缺陷会传播至序列决策，双视图输入虽有提升但仍显著落后于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM基准大多聚焦单智能体、单视图感知，缺乏对异构空地协作中多尺度观测的系统评估，尤其是在尺度不匹配、非对称遮挡和参考系不一致等问题上的几何一致性评估。

Method: 构建11个高保真仿真环境，采集1021对同步空-地观测数据，生成约6.2万双视图四选一VQA实例和115个闭环VLN任务，提供结构化空间标注（跨视图物体身份、度量2D/3D边界框），在13个MLLM上评估三种输入设置（仅无人机、仅无人车、双视图）。

Result: 模型在空间感知维度表现较好，但在跨视图对齐和变换推理维度存在明显瓶颈，且缺陷会传播至下游VLN决策。双视图输入相比单视图有提升，但距离人类表现仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: 几何一致性是当前具身MLLM的关键局限性，双视图协作虽然有益但远不足以弥合与人类的空间智能差距。

Abstract: In recent years, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for embodied intelligence, yet their ability to maintain geometrically consistent spatial understanding across heterogeneous views remains under-evaluated. Existing benchmarks largely focus on single-agent, single-view perception, leaving a gap in the systematic assessment of collaborative air-ground settings, where multi-scale observations are complementary but introduce scale mismatch, asymmetric occlusion, and reference-frame inconsistencies. We present AirGroundBench, a diagnostic benchmark for evaluating multi-view spatial intelligence in heterogeneous UAV-UGV collaboration. AirGroundBench is built from 11 high-fidelity simulated environments with 1,021 synchronized air-ground observation pairs, yielding approximately 62,000 dual-view, four-option single-choice visual question answering instances and 115 closed-loop vision-language navigation episodes. It covers 10 task types organized into four progressively demanding capability dimensions: spatial perception, cross-view alignment, spatial transformation and reasoning, and embodied decision-making. To support geometry-grounded evaluation and analysis, we provide structured spatial annotations, including cross-view object identities and metric 2D and 3D bounding boxes. Evaluations of 13 representative MLLMs under UAV-only, UGV-only, and dual-view input settings reveal consistent bottlenecks: models perform relatively well on spatial perception but struggle with cross-view alignment and transformation-intensive reasoning, and these deficits propagate to sequential decision-making in vision-language navigation. Although dual-view inputs provide measurable gains over single-view variants, a persistent gap from human performance remains, highlighting geometric consistency as a key limitation of current embodied MLLMs.

</details>


### [101] [ReScene: Structured Indoor Scene Reconstruction from Multi-View Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28060)
*Haoran Xu,Lechao Zhang,Daoguo Dong,Yan Gao,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReScene 提出了一种从多视角图像构建物理一致的3D场景的框架，通过层次化视图选择（HierView）和关系感知组装（Relation-Aware Assembly），在几何、渲染和感知质量上达到SOTA，Chamfer距离降低17%，LPIPS降低26%，速度提升10倍，并基于重建场景生成了具身VQA数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角捕获构建仿真就绪3D场景是具身智能的关键瓶颈，现有方法要么依赖专用硬件，要么存在单视角重建偏差，或者生成的布局几何合理但物理不一致。本文指出问题不在于单物体重建，而在于跨视角关系融合和物理合理的场景组装。

Method: ReScene框架包含两个核心组件：1) HierView——基于语义一致性和3D覆盖完整性对重建视图进行优先级排序，取代了将图像占用误认为物体覆盖的最大掩码启发式方法；2) Relation-Aware Assembly——将视觉-语言模型的多帧关系预测与几何和房间外壳先验融合为置信度加权场景图，实现物理一致的场景组装。

Result: 在ScanNet场景上，Chamfer距离降低17%，LPIPS降低26%，运行速度比多视角方法快10倍；基于重建场景生成的具身VQA数据集上，微调后的Qwen-VL在多个空间推理任务上接近强闭源模型性能。

Conclusion: 通过将多视角几何作为统一先验贯穿整个流水线，ReScene有效解决了跨视角关系融合和物理一致场景组装问题，在多个指标上达到SOTA，并展示了重建场景在下游具身任务中的应用价值。

Abstract: Constructing simulation-ready 3D scenes from multi-view captures is a key bottleneck for Embodied Artificial Intelligence, as downstream tasks require object-level structure, explicit inter-object relations, and physical plausibility. Existing approaches either rely on specialized capture hardware, suffer from single-view bias in object reconstruction, or yield layouts that are geometrically reasonable but physically inconsistent. We identify that the problem is not single-object reconstruction but cross-view relation fusion and physically plausible scene assembly. To address this challenge, we present ReScene, a framework that threads multi-view geometry throughout the pipeline as a unifying prior. Our method consists of two main components: HierView prioritizes reconstruction views based on semantic consistency and 3D coverage completeness, replacing the largest-mask heuristic that conflates image occupancy with object coverage; and Relation-Aware Assembly fuses multi-frame relation predictions from a vision-language model with geometric and room-shell priors into a confidence-weighted scene graph, enabling physically consistent scene assembly. ReScene sets a new state of the art across geometry, rendering, and perceptual quality on a set of ScanNet scenes, achieving a 17% reduction in Chamfer Distance and 26% in LPIPS over the strongest prior baseline, while running up to 10x faster than prior multi-view methods. Based on the reconstructed scenes, we also generate an embodied visual question answering dataset, on which fine-tuned Qwen-VL approaches the performance of strong closed-source models on several spatial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [102] [TextDS: Parameter-Efficient Representation Alignment for Scene Text Detection under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28077)
*Boyuan Chen,Zichen Dang,Chuang Yang,Lap-pui Chau,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextDS提出了一种高效的场景文本检测框架，通过双编码器设计（结合视觉基础模型）、步进式LoRA自适应（SWLoRA）和公共子空间融合（CSF），仅用490万可训练参数即可在分布偏移和成像退化条件下实现鲁棒的场景文本检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景文本检测方法在真实部署中面临训练分布之外的分布偏移问题，且跨域变化和成像退化条件下的评估有限，大多依赖大规模场景文本预训练。

Method: 1) 数据高效的双编码器设计，结合视觉基础模型，无需大规模场景文本预训练；2) 步进式LoRA自适应（SWLoRA），通过动态早停机制进行渐进式低秩优化；3) 公共子空间融合（CSF），在对齐的共享子空间中融合两分支特征，保留互补的偏移鲁棒信息；4) 构建了退化条件下的场景文本检测数据集。

Result: 仅用490万可训练参数，在场景文本检测任务中达到竞争性能，在跨域和不利成像条件下均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TextDS是一个高效且鲁棒的场景文本检测框架，在极少量可训练参数下实现了对分布偏移和成像退化的有效应对。

Abstract: In real-world deployments, scene text detectors inevitably face distribution shifts beyond the training distribution. Prior work often depends on large-scale scene-text pretraining, yet evaluation under cross-domain changes and real-world imaging degradations remains limited. We propose TextDS, an efficient framework for scene text detection under distribution shifts. First, we propose a data-efficient dual-encoder design with visual foundation models, eliminating the reliance on large-scale scene-text pretraining. Second, we introduce Step-wise LoRA adaptation (SWLoRA), which performs progressive low-rank refinement with a dynamic early-exit mechanism for effective feature adaptation. Third, we propose Common Subspace Fusion (CSF) to align and fuse the two branches in a shared subspace while retaining complementary, shift-robust information. Finally, we construct adverse-condition scene text detection datasets to address the gap in evaluating under imaging degradation. Experiments show that TextDS achieves competitive performance in scene text detection, demonstrating robustness across domains and adverse imaging conditions with only 4.9M trainable parameters.

</details>


### [103] [RPM-Distill: Physiology-guided Adaptive Cross-modal Distillation for Robust Remote Physiological Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28089)
*Jiyao Wang,Qingyong Hu,Duoxun Tang,Xiao Yang,Kaishun Wu,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RPM-Distill，一种生理引导的跨模态蒸馏框架，训练时用雷达信号辅助RGB视频进行远程生理测量，推理时仅需视频，通过频域生理结构蒸馏和元学习策略网络，在困难条件下MAE降低81%、相关性提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频远程生理测量(RPM)在光照变化、肤色差异和运动干扰下脆弱；雷达能提供互补的心肺微动信号但对推理阶段部署不实用。希望利用雷达在训练阶段辅助视频模型，同时保持推理时仅需视频的轻量部署。

Method: 提出生理引导的跨模态蒸馏框架：(1)频域生理结构蒸馏，包含基频锚定、离峰背景分布匹配、频谱形态与锐度保持三个损失；(2)频谱策略网络，从师生频谱关系图预测样本级蒸馏门控和组件权重，通过元双层目标在少量标注验证集上学习，避免教师质量不确定时的负迁移。

Result: 在困难条件和跨数据集设置下，RPM-Distill相比单模态基线MAE降低81%，相关性提升21%。

Conclusion: RGB与RF雷达虽然在时域形态不同，但在频域共享相似的潜在周期性节律，通过频域生理蒸馏可有效将雷达知识迁移至视频模型，提升RPM鲁棒性。

Abstract: Video-based remote physiological measurement (RPM) is highly accessible but remains fragile under varying illumination, skin tones, and motion. Radio frequency (RF) radar is largely invariant to illumination and appearance, providing complementary cardio-respiratory micro-motion cues; however, requiring radar at inference is often impractical due to its limited ubiquity and deployment overhead. We propose RPM-Distill, a physiology-guided cross-modal distillation framework that leverages synchronized radar only during training while retaining video-only inference. Our key observation is that although RGB and RF waveforms differ in sensing physics and time-domain morphology, they share similar latent periodic rhythm in the frequency domain. We thus distill physiology-structured spectral evidence to improve robustness, via losses that (i) anchor the fundamental peak, (ii) match the off-peak background distribution, and (iii) preserve spectral morphology and sharpness. To avoid negative transfer under sample-level teacher quality and alignment uncertainty, a spectral policy network predicts sample-level distillation gates and component weights from the student--teacher spectral relation map, learned with a meta bilevel objective on a small labeled validation split. Through extensive experiments in challenging conditions and cross-dataset settings, RPM-Distill brings 81\% MAE and 21\% correlation improvement over unimodal baselines. Code is at https://github.com/WJULYW/RPM-Distill.

</details>


### [104] [Diffusion Model Attribution via Spectral Coupling of Denoiser Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28092)
*Pragati Shuddhodhan Meshram,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SDS提出了一种基于谱几何的非侵入式扩散模型归属方法，通过探测去噪过程中的频率能量重分布来生成稳定签名，在8个扩散模型上达到99.9%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的归属问题面临两大挑战：不同数据集训练出的模型可能收敛到相似得分函数，导致生成图像本身不可靠；现有非侵入式方法无法区分架构相似的变体，或在模型共享同一自编码器时失效。亟需一种不依赖生成图像、无需模型侵入的稳健归属方法。

Method: 提出SDS方法，核心是对每个候选模型的去噪行为进行指纹提取。利用频域控制扰动探测模型在去噪过程中如何重新分配空间频带间的能量，提取反映模型去噪得分函数独特谱几何的稳定签名。仅需标准前向传播，无需反演、优化或生成时注册。

Result: 在8个多样化扩散模型上达到约99.9%准确率；跨域提示偏移下达到96.2%准确率；在训练数据、架构、训练过程变化下均优于非侵入式基线方法。

Conclusion: 证明了谱几何可作为扩散模型归属的原则性且实用的基础，SDS方法有效解决了现有非侵入式方法的局限性。

Abstract: Attributing a generated image to its source diffusion model is a fundamental challenge in provenance verification and intellectual property protection. This problem is particularly difficult because diffusion models trained on different datasets can converge to similar score functions and thus similar output distributions, making the generated images themselves unreliable as attribution evidence. Existing non-invasive methods either fail on architecturally similar variants or rely on signals that vanish when models share the same autoencoder. We propose Spectral Denoising Signatures (SDS), a non-invasive attribution method that identifies the source model by fingerprinting each candidate model's denoising behavior. Our key insight is that a model's denoising score function exhibits a distinctive spectral geometry, reflected in how it redistributes energy across spatial frequency bands during denoising. By probing this behavior with frequency-controlled perturbations, SDS extracts a stable signature that is intrinsic to the model, requiring only standard forward passes with no inversion, optimization, or generation-time enrollment. Our results demonstrate that SDS achieves approximately 99.9% accuracy across eight diverse diffusion models and 96.2% under cross-domain prompt shift, outperforming non-invasive baselines across variations in training data, architecture, and training procedure, establishing spectral geometry as a principled and practical basis for diffusion model attribution. Code is available at: https://github.com/Pragati-Meshram/SGS

</details>


### [105] [Cross-view Multimodal Vision-Based Assessment Framework for Traditional Chinese Medicine Rehabilitation Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28104)
*Francis Xiatian Zhang,Hao Yao,Shengxuan Chen,Hong Zhu,Hongxiao Jia,Sisi Zheng,Hubert P. H. Shum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CME-AQA框架，通过跨视角多模态融合（第一人称+第三人称视频与视觉-姿态融合）提升中医康复训练中动作质量评估的准确性，尤其针对针刺和推拿中手部自遮挡和手物交互复杂场景，在自建双视角数据集上相比基线方法在加权F1上提升超10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单视角骨骼数据的AQA框架无法有效处理中医康复技术（如针刺、推拿）中密集的手部自遮挡和复杂的手物交互问题，需要更鲁棒的评估方法。

Method: 提出CME-AQA框架，采用跨视角（第一人称+第三人称）多模态融合方案，结合视觉-姿态融合增强环境上下文理解，训练时利用双视角视频提升推理鲁棒性；并构建了两个包含61名受试者的双视角数据集TCM-AQA61-A（针刺）和TCM-AQA61-T（推拿）。

Result: 在关键评分任务（如针刺深度、快速进针）上加权F1相对最优基线提升超10%，在插入时间、操作频率等定量指标上降低平均绝对误差；在CPR数据集上也展现出可比性能。

Conclusion: CME-AQA提升了结构化中医康复训练的评估准确性，为训练导向的技能评估提供了更便捷有效的方案，并展现出向相关结构化临床技能评估推广的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-based assessment can provide convenient and cost-effective evaluation in Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) rehabilitation training, where action quality assessment (AQA) from computer vision offers a promising solution. Existing automatic AQA frameworks for physical therapy typically rely on skeletal data captured from a single viewpoint, which is inefficient for TCM techniques such as acupuncture or Tuina that involve dense hand self-occlusion and complex hand-object interactions. To address these challenges, we propose CME-AQA, a cross-view, multimodal vision-based assessment framework that integrates visual-pose fusion to enhance understanding of environmental context and leverages both first-person and third-person videos during training to improve inference robustness. We collected two dual-view datasets, TCM-AQA61-A (Acupuncture) and TCM-AQA61-T (Tuina), each containing synchronized first-person and third-person recordings of 61 subjects with expert annotations. Experimental results show that our approach achieves superior or comparable mean performance against competitive baselines, achieving over 10% relative improvement in weighted F1 over the best competing method on key rating tasks such as Needle Depth and Quick Needle Insertion, while also reducing mean absolute error in quantitative measures such as insertion time and manipulation frequency. Testing on a CPR dataset further demonstrates comparable performance on several posture-based criteria, suggesting applicability to related structured simulated clinical skill assessments where participant motion is central to evaluation. Overall, CME-AQA enhances assessment accuracy for structured TCM rehabilitation training and facilitates more convenient and effective training-oriented skill evaluation.

</details>


### [106] [Monocular Avatar Reconstruction via Cascaded Diffusion Priors and UV-Space Differentiable Shading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28144)
*Hong Li,Minqi Meng,Yanjun Liang,Chongjie Ye,Houyuan Chen,Weiqing Xiao,Xianda Guo,Guojun Lei,Xuhui Liu,Chaojie Yang,Yanlun Peng,Hao Zhao,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据高效的框架，利用统一预训练扩散模型的稳健先验，通过级联LoRA在UV空间中依次完成纹理修补、光照去除和材质分解，仅用不到100个真实3D扫描数据即可生成高质量的4K PBR 3D头像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张野外图像重建高保真、可重新光照的3D头像是一个病态问题，主要受限于高质量PBR数据的稀缺性以及从内在材质中解耦光照的复杂性。

Method: 利用统一预训练扩散模型，通过级联LoRA（Inpainting LoRA、Light-Homogenization LoRA）在UV空间依次处理纹理补全、去光照和材质分解；引入Cross-Intrinsic Attention机制协同合成像素对齐的PBR贴图；施加UV空间可微BRDF着色损失以保证物理合理性。

Result: 在不到100个真实3D扫描数据上训练，可生成4K分辨率的综合PBR资产，在真实感和泛化能力上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过级联LoRA策略，高效利用扩散模型的生成先验，以极少的训练数据实现了高质量、可重新光照的3D头像重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity, relightable 3D avatars from a single in-the-wild image is a challenging ill-posed problem, primarily hindered by the scarcity of high-quality PBR data and the complexity of disentangling illumination from intrinsic materials. In this paper, we present a data-efficient framework that leverages the robust priors of a unified pre-trained diffusion backbone to sequentially address texture completion, delighting, and material decomposition. Unlike existing methods that rely on fragmented pipelines or extensive proprietary datasets, we utilize cascaded Low-Rank Adaptations (LoRAs) to adapt the strong generative prior of the diffusion model for each sub-task in UV space. Specifically, we first employ an Inpainting LoRA to complete missing UV textures caused by occlusion, leveraging the model's semantic understanding to generate semantically and photometrically coherent details. Subsequently, a Light-Homogenization LoRA and a novel Cross-Intrinsic Attention mechanism are introduced to remove baked-in lighting and collaboratively synthesize pixel-aligned PBR maps (Albedo, Normal, Roughness, Specular, and Displacement). To ensure physical plausibility, we impose a UV-space differentiable BRDF shading loss during the decomposition stage, forcing the generative process to adhere to the rendering equation without the artifacts typical of rasterization-based supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method, trained on fewer than 100 real 3D scans, generates comprehensive, 4K-resolution PBR assets with superior realism and generalization compared to state-of-the-art methods, and all training code and model weights will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [107] [EchoSonar-R: A Multi-View Reasoning-Enabled Model for Disease Classification and Report Generation in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28164)
*Darya Taratynova,Ahmed Aly,Numan Saeed,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoSonar-R 是一个多视图推理增强的视觉-语言模型，联合实现超声心动图的多标签疾病分类和报告生成。它结合时空视频编码器与结构感知心脏检测器，提供空间锚定的解剖学线索以提升可解释性。模型采用两阶段训练：监督微调（SFT）后接基于组相对策略优化（GRPO）的统一强化学习框架，联合对齐分类和报告生成。在私有数据集和两个公开基准上，EchoSonar-R在宏观平衡准确率上分别提升17.1%和6.1%，GREEN临床忠实度得分达0.800，并生成基于多视图视觉证据的可解释推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超声心动图分类模型缺乏显式诊断推理能力和空间锚定的解剖学证据，限制了临床医生的信任度。

Method: 提出EchoSonar-R，包含时空视频编码器和结构感知心脏检测器；两阶段训练：SFT + GRPO强化学习，联合优化分类与报告生成。

Result: 私有数据集上宏观平衡准确率提升17.1%，MIMICEchoQA上提升6.1%；GREEN临床忠实度得分0.800。

Conclusion: EchoSonar-R通过多视图推理和空间锚定解剖学线索显著提升了分类性能与可解释性，增强了临床可信度。

Abstract: Echocardiography is the most widely used non-invasive cardiac imaging modality, providing essential information for cardiovascular diagnosis. Interpreting an echocardiogram requires synthesizing complementary evidence across multiple heart views to identify abnormalities and produce structured clinical reports. While recent efforts focus on improving classification performance, most models lack explicit diagnostic reasoning and spatially grounded anatomical evidence, limiting clinician trust. We present EchoSonar-R, a multi-view reasoning-enabled vision-language model that jointly performs multi-label disease classification and report generation from echocardiography studies. EchoSonar-R combines a spatiotemporal video encoder with a structure-aware cardiac detector that provides spatially grounded anatomical cues to improve interpretability and clinician trust during cross-view reasoning. EchoSonar-R is trained in two stages: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on reasoning-annotated targets, followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-specific rewards that jointly align classification and report generation within a unified reinforcement-learning framework. Across a private multi-view dataset and two public benchmarks, EchoSonar-R improves macro balanced accuracy by 17.1% on the private set and 6.1% on MIMICEchoQA over the strongest baseline, achieves a GREEN clinical faithfulness score of 0.800, and produces interpretable reasoning traces grounded in multi-view visual evidence.

</details>


### [108] [RSICCLLM: A Multimodal Large Language Model for Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28266)
*Yelin Wang,Zijia Song,Shuo Ye,Chuanguang Yang,Miaoyu Wang,Yong Xu,Zhulin An,Yongjun Xu,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSICCLLM，首个面向遥感图像变化描述的大型视觉-语言模型后训练框架。通过设计数据生成范式（RSICI指令数据集）、差异感知监督微调（提取变化表征）和双负样本偏好优化（DNPO，构建RSICP偏好数据集），仅用7B参数即超越更大规模模型，解决了传统方法模型容量不足及大模型直接迁移面临的数匮乏与细粒度变化理解难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感图像变化描述方法依赖传统深度学习架构，模型容量有限制约性能；大模型后训练技术虽在通用领域成功，但因数据稀缺和需要细粒度变化理解，直接迁移至RSICC面临挑战。

Method: 1) 设计数据生成范式并发布指令数据集RSICI，建立任务专属基准；2) 提出差异感知监督微调（Difference-aware SFT），显式提取变化表征并引导模型感知时序差异；3) 提出双负样本偏好优化（DNPO），通过两种互补负样本构建策略生成偏好数据集RSICP，进一步优化模型性能。

Result: RSICCLLM以仅7B参数取得卓越结果，显著超越更大规模模型，验证了该后训练框架在遥感图像变化描述任务上的优越能力。

Conclusion: 提出首个面向RSICC的大视觉-语言模型后训练框架，通过数据生成、差异感知微调和双负样本偏好优化三大核心设计，有效解决数据稀缺与细粒度变化理解问题，实现用较小模型参数超越大规模模型的性能突破。

Abstract: Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning (RSICC) aims to describe changes between bi-temporal remote sensing images and holds significant research and application value. However, most existing methods rely on conventional deep learning architectures, and the limited model capacity constrains performance. Although large-model post-training techniques have achieved great success in general domains, their direct transfer to RSICC remains challenging due to data scarcity and the need for fine-grained change understanding. To address this, we propose RSICCLLM, the first post-training framework for large vision-language models in RSICC. Specifically, we design a data generation paradigm, release the instruction dataset RSICI, and establish a task-specific RSICC benchmark. We further introduce Difference-aware Supervised Fine-tuning to explicitly extract change representations and guide the model in perceiving and understanding temporal differences. In addition, we propose Dual-Negative Preference Optimization (DNPO), which employs two complementary negative-sample construction strategies to construct the preference dataset RSICP and further refine model performance. Extensive experiments validate the superior capability of RSICCLLM, which achieves outstanding results with only 7B parameters, surpassing models of substantially larger scales. The code and dataset will be made publicly available at https://github.com/keaill/RSICCLLM.

</details>


### [109] [StructSplat: Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting from Uncalibrated Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28321)
*Jia-Chen Zhao,Beiqi Chen,Xinyang Chen,Guangcong Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StructSplat 是一种前馈式、可泛化的3D高斯重建框架，无需相机参数即可直接处理未校准图像。通过结构化表示分离几何、语义和纹理信息，引入像素对齐特征注入、语义感知先验和相机对齐策略，在DL3DV数据集上PSNR达到28.045，大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖逐场景优化，要么需要已知相机位姿，且通常将几何与外观耦合在同一骨干网络中，限制了重建保真度和泛化能力。本文旨在设计一种无需相机参数、可直接泛化的前馈式3D高斯重建方法。

Method: 提出结构化表示，将几何、语义和纹理线索在重建过程中赋予明确角色；引入像素对齐特征注入机制实现从2D观测的精确纹理建模；融入语义感知先验提升全局一致性；设计相机对齐策略防止信息泄露并提升泛化能力。

Result: 在DL3DV上PSNR达28.045，超越AnySplat（22.377）+5.67 dB；跨数据集评测中，在ACID上比AnySplat高+1.94 dB，在RealEstate10K上高+1.72 dB。

Conclusion: StructSplat通过结构化表示和多种解耦设计，在无需相机参数的条件下显著提升了3D高斯重建的保真度和跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: We present StructSplat, a feed-forward and generalizable 3D Gaussian reconstruction framework that operates directly on uncalibrated images without requiring camera parameters. Existing methods either rely on per-scene optimization or assume known camera poses, and often entangle geometry and appearance within a unified backbone, limiting reconstruction fidelity and generalization. Our key idea is to adopt a structured representation that organizes geometry, semantic, and texture cues with explicit roles in the reconstruction process. Specifically, we introduce a pixel-aligned feature injection mechanism to enable accurate texture modeling from 2D observations, incorporate semantic-aware priors to improve global consistency, and design a camera alignment strategy to prevent information leakage and improve generalization. Experiments show that our method significantly outperforms prior approaches on challenging benchmarks. On DL3DV, our method achieves 28.045 PSNR, surpassing AnySplat (22.377) by +5.67 dB. In cross-dataset evaluation, our method achieves +1.94 dB over AnySplat on ACID and +1.72 dB on RealEstate10K. Project page: https://structsplat.github.io Code: https://github.com/J-C-Zhao/StructSplat

</details>


### [110] [PerceptionRubrics: Calibrating Multimodal Evaluation to Human Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28322)
*Yana Wei,Hongbo Peng,Yanlin Lai,Liang Zhao,Kangheng Lin,En Yu,Keyu Lv,Han Zhou,Yin Tang,Haodong Li,Mitt Huang,Hangyu Guo,Jianjian Sun,Zheng Ge,Xiangyu Zhang,Daxin Jiang,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerceptionRubrics是一个基于评分细则的评估框架，通过1,038张信息密集型图像和12,000多条实例级评分细则，采用门控评分机制替代线性平均，揭示模型在密集域中的可靠性差距、开源与闭源模型间8%的感知缺陷，并验证了严格感知保真度是可靠生成的前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试分数饱和但模型在真实场景中仍表现脆弱，亟需一种能从整体语义匹配转向严格原子级审计的评估框架，以捕捉模型在密集视觉域中的真实感知能力。

Method: 构建1,038张信息密集型图像与12,000多条实例级评分细则；通过循环同行评审共识流水线生成黄金描述，并蒸馏为双流系统（Must-Right基本事实 + Easy-Wrong细粒度细节）；采用门控评分机制，对强制性视觉事实失败施加二元惩罚。

Result: (1) 可靠性差距：模型能正确验证碎片化元素，但难以满足严格的合取约束；(2) 开源-闭源分层：开源与闭源模型间存在8%的感知差距；(3) 人类对齐严格性：门控指标优于常规基准，验证了严格感知保真度的必要性。

Conclusion: 严格的原子级感知评估比整体语义匹配更能揭示模型真实能力，门控评分机制和双流评分细则是评估密集视觉域中模型感知缺陷的有效工具。

Abstract: We introduce PerceptionRubrics, a rubric-based evaluation framework that addresses the gap between saturated benchmark scores and real-world brittleness. Shifting evaluation from holistic semantic matching to rigorous atomic auditing, PerceptionRubrics pairs 1,038 information-dense images with over 12,000 instance-specific rubrics. These criteria are derived from golden captions constructed via a novel Circular Peer-Review consensus pipeline and then distilled into a dual-stream system of Must-Right (essential facts) and Easy-Wrong (fine-grained details) rubrics. Crucially, PerceptionRubrics implements a Gated Scoring mechanism: unlike linear averages, failure on mandatory visual facts triggers sharp binary penalties. Extensive evaluation yields critical insights: (1) The Reliability Gap: models often verify fragmented elements correctly yet fail strict conjunctive constraints, exposing brittleness in dense domains; (2) Open-Closed Stratification: contrary to reasoning trends, we reveal a persistent 8% perception deficit between open-source and proprietary frontiers; and (3) Human-Aligned Rigor: our gated metrics substantially out-align conventional benchmarks, validating that strict perceptual fidelity is the prerequisite for reliable generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [When the Aggregator Cheats: Data-Free Backdoors in Federated LLM-based QA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27511)
*Chenqing Zhu,Yanbo Dai,Yulong Tian,Qingming Li,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习框架下基于LLM的问答系统中，恶意聚合器（可能与第三方供应商合谋）如何在不访问客户端数据的情况下，通过利用客户端上传的梯度恢复代表性训练样本，构建中毒数据集并植入广告型后门，实现近乎100%的攻击成功率且几乎不损害模型清洁性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的LLM问答系统在敏感领域（如医疗、心理健康咨询、法律咨询）日益普及，而恶意聚合器可能植入后门广告，现有后门注入方法若缺乏客户端数据知识可能导致清洁性能下降或攻击失败，因此需要一种数据无痕、隐蔽的投毒方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 从客户端梯度中恢复代表性训练样本；(2) 利用恢复的样本和触发短语构建中毒数据集，将后门注入全局模型。在完整微调和LoRA设置下进行实验。

Result: 在多个代表性QA数据集和LLM家族上，该方法实现了近100%的攻击成功率，且对清洁任务性能的退化可忽略不计。仅需重建5-20%的梯度就足以发动可靠攻击。

Conclusion: 暴露了联邦训练QA LLMs流程中的一个实际盲点：恶意聚合器可通过梯度恢复实现数据无关的隐蔽后门攻击，对联邦学习的安全性构成严重威胁。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based question-answering (QA) systems are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare, mental health counseling, and legal consultation. Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training without sharing raw client data, for which locally trained models are aggregated at a central server (i.e., a cloud service provider) to obtain a global model. In this paper, we explore the potential vulnerability where a malicious aggregator, who may collude with a third-party vendor, stealthily implants advertisement-type backdoors into federated QA models, without ever accessing client data. The attacker's goals are twofold: (1) preserve clean QA fidelity (i.e., the poisoned model behaves like a clean model on non-triggered queries); and (2) generate highly natural, contextually relevant responses with target advertisements when a trigger appears. Achieving these two goals simultaneously is highly challenging, as naive backdoor injection without knowledge about private data may degrade model's clean performance or fail to inject the target. Motivated by this, we propose to leverage clients' uploaded gradients during training, and develop a two-stage framework for data-free and stealthy poisoning: (1) recover representative training samples from client gradients, and (2) construct poisoning datasets utilizing recovered samples and trigger phrases to inject backdoors into the global model. Experiments across representative QA datasets and LLM families under full fine-tuning and LoRA settings demonstrate that, our method achieves nearly 100% Attack Success Rate (ASR) while incurring negligible degradation on clean tasks. Crucially, reconstructing only 5-20% of gradients suffices to mount a reliable attack, exposing a practical blind spot in the pipeline of federated training of QA LLMs.

</details>


### [112] [On the Inseparability of Instructions and Data in Shared-Embedding Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27567)
*Dewank Pant,Shruti Lohani,Avijit Kumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明了在共享嵌入架构中，完美防御提示注入在数学上是不可能的，因为缺乏强制性的控制-数据分离。作者形式化了受提示系统为Prompted Action Models，定义了语义忠实控制（SFC）属性，并通过三个不可能性定理（证据恢复不可能性、控制路径暴露、有限覆盖不变性差距）证明SFC无法在共享管道中实现。结论是提示注入无法通过更好的管道内分类或对齐消除，需要架构性的指令与数据通道分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM集成应用中提示注入攻击的防御方案均已被攻破，作者试图证明这种失败并非偶然，而是共享嵌入架构中缺乏强制控制-数据分离所导致的根本性结构问题。

Method: 作者形式化定义了Prompted Action Models和语义忠实控制（SFC）属性，然后通过三个数学证明（证据恢复不可能性、控制路径暴露、有限覆盖不变性差距）论证SFC在共享管道中不可实现，并在生产级分词器和模型上进行了量化验证。

Result: 证明了三个核心定理：1）共享表示使可信与不可信内容在统计上不可分离；2）不可信令牌通过注意力值聚合进入控制相关计算；3）有限训练无法保证无限语义等价类上的不变性。这些结果本质上是结构性的，而非当前防御的缺陷。

Conclusion: 提示注入无法通过更好的管道内分类或对齐来消除，需要从架构层面实现指令与数据通道的分离，类似于Von Neumann架构中通过DEP、W^X、ASLR、栈金丝雀和内存安全语言等分层防御最终解决缓冲区溢出问题的方式。

Abstract: Prompt injection is the top security risk for LLM-integrated applications, yet every defense proposed so far has been broken. We prove this is not a coincidence: in shared-embedding architectures that lack enforced control-data separation, perfect prompt-injection prevention is mathematically impossible. We formalize prompted systems as Prompted Action Models whose outputs include control-authoritative actions: refusal decisions, tool authorization, policy routing, and memory writes. We define Semantic-Faithful Control (SFC), the property that such behavior depends only on the meaning of untrusted input, not on how it is encoded. We then prove SFC is unachievable within the shared pipeline, via three results: a provenance-recovery impossibility (shared representations make trusted and untrusted content statistically inseparable, bounded by total variation distance); control-path exposure (untrusted tokens enter control-relevant computation through the same attention value-aggregation that determines outputs); and a finite-coverage invariance gap (finite training cannot certify invariance over infinite semantic-equivalence classes). We ground each quantity in measurements on production tokenizers and models.
  The result is structural, not a gap in current defenses. It mirrors the code-data confusion in Von Neumann machines that gives rise to buffer overflows, a vulnerability class that took decades of layered defenses (DEP, Write-XOR-Execute, ASLR, stack canaries, and ultimately memory-safe languages) to contain, because no single mechanism sufficed. The implication is the same: prompt injection cannot be eliminated by better in-pipeline classification or alignment alone. It requires architectural separation of instruction and data channels. We identify the root cause and the class of solution it demands.

</details>


### [113] [AdvScan: Black-Box Adversarial Example Detection at Runtime through Power Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27704)
*Robi Paul,Michael Zuzak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AdvScan，一种基于运行时功耗分析的对抗样本检测方法，在黑盒场景下以极低延迟实现高精度检测。该方法利用对抗样本会产生异常神经元激活从而产生独特功耗签名这一观察，通过构建良性输入的功耗签名基线分布，并在运行时使用单样本t检验检测异常。在318,400个测试样本上，AdvScan检测率达99.984%，仅40个假阴性，零假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗样本检测方法存在两大局限：(1)需要白盒模型访问权限，这在授权黑盒部署场景中不可行；(2)依赖输入预处理阶段，引入不可忽视的延迟和资源开销，超出任务关键型应用在推理路径上可承受的预算。

Method: AdvScan基于对抗样本产生异常神经元激活进而产生独特功耗签名的观察。算法首先从已知良性输入构建功耗签名基线分布；运行时，对测试输入的功耗签名应用单样本t检验，判断是否显著偏离基线分布，从而检测对抗样本。

Result: 在STM32F303RC (ARM Cortex-M4)和STM32L562RE (ARM Cortex-M33)两款微控制器上，使用FGSM、PGD和C&W三种对抗样本生成算法，对三个MLPerf Tiny基准模型进行测试。在总计318,400个测试输入中，AdvScan检测出99.984%的对抗样本，仅产生40个假阴性和零假阳性。

Conclusion: AdvScan证明了基于功耗的对抗样本检测在安全关键型TinyML黑盒部署中的可行性，为资源受限的边缘设备提供了兼顾延迟和检测精度的可行方案。

Abstract: TinyML models deployed on edge devices are increasingly adopted in safety/security-critical applications, making them a prime target for adversarial example (AE) attacks where inputs are modified to cause misclassifications. However, existing AE detection methods either require white-box model access, which is often unavailable in licensed black-box deployments, or rely on input pre-processing stages that add non-trivial latency and resource overhead, often exceeding what mission-critical applications can afford on their inference path. To address these challenges, we propose AdvScan, a runtime power analysis-based methodology for AE detection that operates in a black-box scenario while inducing minimal latency. AdvScan is based on the observation that AEs produce anomalous neuron activations, which in turn generate distinctive power-consumption signatures. The algorithm initially constructs a baseline distribution of power signatures from known benign inputs; then, at runtime, it applies a one-sample t-test to determine whether a test input's power signature significantly deviates from this baseline, thereby detecting AEs. We evaluated AdvScan using three adversarial example generation algorithms: Fast Gradient Sign Method (FGSM), Projected Gradient Descent (PGD), and Carlini-Wagner (C&W), on three MLPerf Tiny benchmark models implemented on two target devices: the STM32F303RC (ARM Cortex-M4) and STM32L562RE (ARM Cortex-M33) microcontrollers. Across 318,400 total test inputs, AdvScan detects 99.984% of AEs with only 40 false negatives and zero false positives. These results demonstrate the viability of power-based AE detection for secure, accuracy-critical TinyML deployments in black-box environments.

</details>


### [114] [Reliable Homomorphic Matching for Fuzzy Labeled PSI at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27803)
*Erkam Uzun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现模糊标签隐私集合交集（FLPSI）中基于阈值的同态内核存在组合漏洞：单次试验的误接受概率会随数据库规模累积为实现的可靠度误差（RSE）。提出CSTPSI内核，通过独立令牌轮次将误接受概率提升至匹配强度，证明其在半诚实模型下的安全性。评估表明：百万级数据库基线内核RSE达100%，而CSTPSI保持为零；中小规模下CSTPSI比基线快20倍以上，通信量减少93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FLPSI构造采用集合-阈值归约达到实用性能，但其同态内核存在组合漏洞：单次试验的误接受概率随数据库规模线性累积，导致实现的可靠度误差（RSE）不可忽略。该RSE是密码匹配层的可靠性属性，而非匹配器自身的精度问题，需要形式化定义并消除这一漏洞。

Method: 形式化定义了组合安全属性，给出接收方优势的闭式界限；提出CSTPSI内核，通过运行独立令牌轮次将单次试验误接受概率提升至匹配强度（即与正确匹配概率同阶）；基于半诚实模型证明安全性；轮次数由界限确定：百万级需2轮，十亿级需3轮（工程阈值10^{-6}）。

Result: 百万记录规模下基线内核RSE达100%，CSTPSI在所有配置下保持零RSE；中小规模下CSTPSI比基线快20倍以上，通信量降低93%；仅在大规模（百万级）时性能趋近基线。

Conclusion: CSTPSI成功消除了FLPSI同态内核的组合漏洞，提供了可组合的安全保障，并在中小规模场景下显著优于现有方案。实现代码及可复现性工具已公开。

Abstract: Fuzzy Labeled Private Set Intersection (FLPSI) lets a receiver learn the labels of enrolled records similar to its query, and nothing else. Constructions based on a set-threshold reduction reach practical performance: a query matches a record when the two agree on a threshold number of components, and the private matching is delegated to an inner set-threshold kernel. We study its homomorphic form, which combines leveled-BFV homomorphic encryption (HE), a garbled circuit, and secret sharing to decide the match under encryption and release the record's label. We identify a composition gap in this kernel: efficiency is bought with a per-trial false-accept probability, but one query runs a trial for every record, so the error compounds with the database size into the kernel's realization soundness error (RSE), the rate at which it accepts a query the plaintext matcher would reject. The RSE is a reliability property of the cryptographic matching layer, not the matcher's accuracy, and a sound kernel must contribute zero or negligible RSE of its own. We formalize this as a composable security property, give a closed-form bound on the receiver's advantage, and close the gap with CSTPSI, a kernel that runs independent token rounds and raises the per-trial bound to a matching power. We prove CSTPSI secure in the semi-honest model. The bound sets the round count: two token rounds suffice for million-scale databases and three for billion-scale at the $10^{-6}$ engineering threshold. Our evaluation confirms this: at a million records the baseline kernel's RSE reaches 100% while CSTPSI holds it at 0 in every measured configuration. For large labels at small to moderate scale CSTPSI is more than 20x faster than the baseline, with up to 93% less communication, converging to the baseline only at million-scale. Our implementation, with a one-command reproducibility harness, is publicly available.

</details>


### [115] [Exploring and Exploiting Synchrony Limitations of Time-Triggered Network-Agnostic Guardians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27819)
*Shreya Vithal Kulhalli,Mohammad Ibrahim Alkoudsi,Gerhard Fohler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了网络无关型守护节点依赖被控节点进行时钟同步的安全漏洞，理论上界定了可达的时钟同步精度下限，并提出一种利用不完美时钟同步的定时攻击，使恶意节点能够与守护节点去同步并干扰目标节点的关键消息。在FlexRay总线网络上的仿真验证表明，该攻击能以100%的成功率保持隐蔽并引发碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络无关型守护节点在时间和成本上具有优势，但其依赖被控节点进行时钟同步的设计引入了安全漏洞：恶意节点可利用此依赖关系发动定时攻击，干扰网络中其他节点的消息传输。

Method: 首先从理论上推导节点与其网络无关型守护节点之间时钟同步精度的下界；基于该下界，提出一种利用不可避免的时钟不同步来引发受控且未被检测到的去同步的定时攻击；最后在OMNeT++仿真框架中实现FlexRay现场总线网络模型进行攻击有效性评估。

Result: 该攻击能以100%的成功率保持未被检测到，并能以100%的成功率通过碰撞破坏目标节点关键消息的传输。

Conclusion: 网络无关型守护节点存在由时钟同步依赖引入的安全漏洞，所提出的定时攻击能够在实际网络场景中高效隐蔽地破坏关键消息通信，需在设计上加以防范。

Abstract: Time-triggered communication protocols rely on trusted components known as guardians to enforce adherence to predetermined network schedules. Network-agnostic guardians offer an efficient and scalable distributed solution with reduced implementation cost and complexity compared to network-aware alternatives. However, this efficiency is based on the guardian's dependence on the controlled node for clock synchronization, which introduces a vulnerability: a malicious node can exploit this dependency to launch timing attacks against its guardian and eventually interfere with messages from other nodes on the network. In this paper, we establish a theoretical lower bound on the attainable clock synchronization precision between a node and its network-agnostic guardian. Building on this result, we introduce a timing attack that leverages the unavoidably imperfect clock synchrony to cause controlled and undetected de-synchronization of the guardian. The attack enables a malicious node to cause collisions with targeted critical network messages. We evaluate the effectiveness of the attack using a FlexRay field bus network model implemented in the OMNeT++ simulation framework. Our results show that the attack is able to remain undetected with 100% success and disrupts the transmission of the critical messages of the target node by causing collisions with them with 100% success.

</details>


### [116] [Self-Verifying Measurement Records: Hash-Linked Evidence Graphs for Hardware Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27934)
*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于透明度日志的硬件测量可验证框架，通过内容哈希绑定、概率性矩阵乘积验证（Freivalds算法）和代数校验和，使硬件性能报告成为防篡改、可独立审计的记录，并在Blackwell和Hopper GPU上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件性能数据通常被“信任接受”，但读者无法复现、设备已不存在，且硅片本身可能静默出错（约千分之一核心返回错误算术结果而不报错）。因此需要一种使测量结果可独立验证、防篡改的方法。

Method: 1) 将所有测量数据（文本、表格、图形）通过内容哈希绑定到观测和验证过程；2) 构建哈希链接的仅追加结构（透明度日志），验证者可离线审计而不需信任生产者；3) 矩阵乘积采用Freivalds概率恒等式在O(k n²)代价下验证，基于浮点误差分析推导容差并校准到设备自身实测残差底噪；4) 无此类恒等式的量附带代数校验和及可复现性分类；5) 将验证本身作为安全对象处理：针对探测种子提交离线复现的攻擊面，提出Fiat-Shamir挑战（从声称输出推导）以抵御探针感知的对抗者；6) 通过di/dt功率病毒和热浸透测试物理故障威胁。

Result: 在Blackwell和Hopper GPU上验证了该构造，报告了不同精度、规模和设备下的残差底噪与可复现性图谱。功率病毒和热浸透测试未使校准容差偏移或产生静默错误，说明物理故障威胁主要来自罕见缺陷部件或特权攻击者。

Conclusion: 该框架使得硬件测量结果可独立验证、防篡改，明确了需与硬件信任根（root of trust）组合使用的边界，为可信硬件性能报告提供了系统性的安全方案。

Abstract: Performance numbers reported for hardware are accepted on trust: the reader cannot recompute them, the apparatus is gone, and the silicon itself can be silently wrong, with fleet studies reporting on the order of one core in a thousand returning incorrect arithmetic with no error raised. We make a reported hardware measurement a tamper-evident, independently checkable record. Every quantity in the text, a table, or a figure is bound, by its content hash, to the observation and the verification behind it; the whole is a hash-linked, append-only structure (a transparency log for measurement) that a verifier audits offline without trusting its producer. Matrix products are verified by a probabilistic identity (Freivalds) at O(k n^2) cost under a tolerance we derive from floating-point error analysis and calibrate to the device's own measured residual floor, so a wrong product is rejected with probability 1 - 2^(-k); quantities with no such identity carry an algebraic checksum and a measured reproducibility class. We then treat the check itself as a security object: a probe seed committed for offline reproducibility is an attack surface, and a probe-aware adversary can hide a corruption in the probe's null space, fooling even a quorum of bit-identical witnesses, while a Fiat-Shamir challenge derived from the claimed output closes this. Driving the device from an unprivileged tenant's reach, with a di/dt power virus and a thermal soak, neither moves the calibrated tolerance nor produces a silent error, placing the physical-fault threat at the rare defective part or the privileged attacker and marking the boundary at which the record must compose with a hardware root of trust. We demonstrate the construction across Blackwell and Hopper GPUs and report a residual-floor and reproducibility map by precision, size, and device.

</details>


### [117] [Agentic AI-Powered Re-Identification: An Emerging, Scalable Threat to Mobility Microdata Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27936)
*Oscar Thees,Roman Müller,Matthias Templ*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究表明，基于智能体AI的自动化流水线能够仅凭时空坐标数据和公开网络信息，在无需人工干预的情况下成功重识别个体身份（72%的可识别个体被成功识别），从根本上改变了传统去匿名化的威胁模型，对统计披露控制实践和GDPR标准下的匿名性认定构成严峻挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业数据经纪商广泛收集细粒度位置数据，但公众对此带来的重识别风险认识不足。先前研究虽已证明移动轨迹具有高度唯一性，但重识别攻击需要大量人工分析，限制了实际规模化应用。本文旨在探索智能体AI是否能够从根本上改变这一威胁模型。

Method: 构建端到端全自动流水线：大语言模型智能体自主搜索开放网络、交叉参考公开记录和社交媒体、将原始坐标序列解析为候选身份。使用包含真实家庭和工作地址附近模拟定位点的时空数据集进行评估，聚焦高风险披露场景。

Result: 在25个可重识别个体中成功识别18个（72%），在全部43个案例中成功重识别18个（41.9%），仅依赖时空数据和公开来源信息，无需人工干预。

Conclusion: 事实上的匿名性——统计披露控制实践的基础——正在发生转变。智能体AI强化了在GDPR Recital-26标准下重识别"合理可能"的论证，且成本仅为每分钟数美元/每目标。数据保管者和监管机构必须预见并应对这一即将升级的威胁。

Abstract: The widespread collection of fine-grained location data by commercial data brokers creates a re-identification risk that is not widely recognised by the public. While prior research has established that mobility traces are highly unique and that individuals can, in principle, be identified from a handful of spatio-temporal points, such attacks have historically required significant manual effort from skilled analysts, limiting their practical scale.
  In this feasibility study, we demonstrate in a real world setting that agentic AI fundamentally changes this threat model. We present an end-to-end pipeline in which large language model agents autonomously search the open web, cross-reference public records and social media, and resolve raw coordinate sequences to candidate identities - without human intervention. We evaluate the pipeline on a spatio-temporal dataset containing simulated location points anchored at and around true home and work addresses, focusing on a high-risk disclosure scenario. Our results demonstrate that, from spatio-temporal data and public sources alone, our agentic AI successfully re-identified 18 of the 25 re-identifiable individuals (72%) and 18 of 43 cases overall (41.9%).
  We discuss implications for Statistical Disclosure Control (SDC) practice and outline the near-future escalation that data custodians and regulators must anticipate. De facto anonymity - an implicit foundation of SDC practice - is shifting. Agentic AI strengthens the case that re-identification is reasonably likely by any means under the GDPR Recital-26 standard, at costs of minutes-and-dollars per target.

</details>


### [118] [Decoys Cannot Go Everywhere: Mapping the Deception Surface in MITRE ATT&CK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27966)
*Veronica Valeros,Carlos Catania,Viliam Lisý,Harm Griffioen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了MITRE ATT&CK v18.1中250种技术对基础设施欺骗的适用性，发现仅80种（32%）技术允许部署诱饵，并归纳出Sweep和Seek两种部署模式，表明欺骗手段不能假定适用于所有攻击者行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络欺骗研究通常默认诱饵可以放置在存在攻击者行为的任何位置，但这一假设未经系统检验。本文旨在通过严格的评估框架验证该假设的有效性。

Method: 提出四维度评估标准（诱饵可部署性、攻击者交互可能性、交互情报价值、交互的恶意指示可靠性），对ATT&CK v18.1全部250种技术进行逐项评估。

Result: 仅80种技术（32%）允许部署诱饵且攻击者可能触及；170种技术无法放置任何防御者控制的诱饵。诱饵部署呈现两种模式：Sweep（攻击者在范围内广泛移动时遇到诱饵）和Seek（攻击者寻找特定资产时与伪造版本交互）。

Conclusion: 基础设施诱饵不能假定适用于所有攻击行为；诱饵的有效性在交互可能性和恶意指示可靠性上存在显著差异；研究发布了可审计的评估基线和决策规则。

Abstract: Cyber deception research often assumes that a decoy can be placed wherever there is attacker behavior. This work tests that assumption across MITRE ATT&CK v18.1. We introduce a four-criterion rubric for infrastructure deception and apply it to all 250 ATT&CK techniques. The rubric evaluates whether a defender-controlled decoy can be placed, whether an attacker is likely to interact with it, what intelligence that interaction can yield, and whether the interaction reliably indicates malice. The resulting deception surface is sparse: only 80 techniques (32%) admit a decoy the attacker could plausibly reach. For the remaining 170 techniques, there is no defender-controlled asset in the attacker's path that can be fabricated as a decoy. Decoy placement across those 80 techniques falls into two patterns we call Sweep and Seek. In Sweep, the attacker moves broadly through assets in range and encounters the decoy as part of that activity. In Seek, the attacker looks for a specific kind of asset and interacts with a fabricated version of it. These patterns give a simple placement rule: a decoy must either sit on a sweep path or imitate a sought asset. We also show that decoys usually have useful intelligence potential, but whether an attacker interacts with them at all, and whether that interaction reliably indicates malice, both vary. We release the rubric, decision rules, and per-technique assessment as an auditable baseline for future deception research and deployment planning, and show that infrastructure decoys cannot be assumed to apply to all attacker behavior.

</details>


### [119] [SHARD: cell-keyed residual splitting for alignment-resistant private dense retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27976)
*Sergey Kurilenko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Shard是一种针对稠密嵌入向量泄露攻击的防御方法，通过将嵌入拆分为公共前缀和私有分片残差，在保持检索性能的同时大幅提升攻击者恢复原始文本所需的锚点数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密嵌入被广泛用于语义搜索和RAG，但泄露的向量存储库会暴露大量原始文本。现有的攻击方法（少样本对齐、零样本反转、无监督跨空间翻译）都利用了一个弱点：受保护的存储库具有单一的全局几何结构，可以被对齐到已知空间。即使是秘密全局旋转这种轻量级防御，攻击者也能通过正交Procrustes方法恢复。因此需要一种能消除这一弱点的防御方法。

Method: 将中心化嵌入拆分为短公共前缀（用于第一阶段检索）和私有残差。私有残差被分片为C个单元，每个单元使用独立的秘密密钥加密，在CKKS同态加密下进行重排序，密钥相互抵消后得到精确的内积。参数C控制设计从全局线性基线（C=1）到每文档微密钥（C=N）的连续谱系。

Result: Shard返回与原始空间相同的nDCG@10（而半SVD截断会损失精度）；在已知明文泄露场景下，将残差映射回公共坐标系所需的锚点数量约为C倍（C=256时中位数从200增至102,400）。短公共前缀泄露的邻居结构更少，微密钥极限下残差图归零。Shard能抵御学习型、非线性和无监督对齐攻击；在匹配效用的噪声防御几乎去匿名化每个探针时，Shard的去匿名化数量为零。

Conclusion: Shard是一种攻击感知的几何防御方法，而非加密保证。其局限包括：单元内密钥相互抵消、目标攻击者仅需约d_priv个锚点、重叠参考语料仍会通过前缀泄露信息。

Abstract: Dense embeddings underpin semantic search and RAG, yet a leaked vector store hands much of the underlying text back to whoever holds it. The attacks that make this possible (few-shot alignment, zero-shot inversion, unsupervised cross-space translation) share one weakness: the protected store is a single global geometry that can be aligned to a known one. A secret global rotation, the usual lightweight defence, is no exception: orthogonal Procrustes recovers it once the attacker has about the subspace dimension in known pairs.
  We introduce Shard, a retrieval-preserving embedding transform that removes this weak axis. The centred embedding is split into a short public prefix (for stage-1 retrieval) and a private residual sharded into C cells under separate secret keys; the residual is reranked under CKKS, where the keys cancel and leave the inner product exact. A single parameter C runs the design from the global-linear baseline it replaces (C=1) to per-document micro-keys (C=N). Because the rerank is full-dimensional, Shard returns the raw-space nDCG@10 that half-SVD truncation gives up; and because the residual is keyed cell-locally, mapping it back to a common frame under a diffuse known-plaintext leak costs roughly C times more anchors (median 200 to 102,400 at C=256), for a few encrypted queries. The short public prefix leaks far less neighbour structure, and a micro-key limit drives the residual graph to zero with an unlinkable, renewable template. The barrier holds against learned, non-linear and unsupervised aligners, and where a matched-utility noise defence de-anonymises almost every probe, Shard de-anonymises none. We are plain about the limits: within a cell the keys cancel, a targeted attacker needs only about d_priv anchors, and an overlapping reference corpus still leaks through the prefix. Shard is an attack-aware geometric defence, not a cryptographic guarantee.

</details>


### [120] [AdvancedShelLM: A Stateful Multi-Agent LLM Honeypot for SSH Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27990)
*Muris Sladić,Eman Alibalić,Veronica Valeros,Carlos Catania,Sebastian Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多智能体、多LLM架构的高级SSH蜜罐AdvancedShelLM，通过Manager和Worker两个LLM代理协同工作，并引入永久文件系统，在单元测试中达到99.02%的通过率，能够欺骗真实攻击者并影响其行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM蜜罐（如shelLM）虽然能生成逼真的交互，但经验丰富的攻击者仍可识别，需要更好的框架支撑。

Method: 采用Manager和Worker双LLM代理架构，Manager负责理解命令意图，Worker负责生成响应，减少错误回复并提升欺骗性；实现高级永久文件系统，允许多个并发攻击者看到相同的动态变化文件。

Result: 单元测试通过率最高达99.02%；AI攻击者ARACNE对是否为蜜罐的判断存在困难，但对真实Ubuntu shell也有轻微偏向蜜罐的判断；对人类攻击者的欺骗能力优于Cowrie，与shelLM相当；互联网部署证明AdvancedShelLM的输出能影响真实攻击者的行为。

Conclusion: 多智能体多LLM架构能有效提升蜜罐的欺骗能力，永久文件系统是多并发场景下的重要改进，但仍需进一步降低被AI识别为蜜罐的风险。

Abstract: LLM-based SSH honeypots can generate believable interactions, but evaluations indicate they remain somewhat identifiable to determined attackers, indicating the need for a better scaffolding. We present a new LLM-based honeypot design that uses a multi-agent, multi-LLM architecture to address the limitations of the previous shelLM LLM honeypot. Our honeypot, called AdvancedShelLM, uses two LLM agents, a Manager and a Worker, that better understand the commands while reducing incorrect responses and increasing deception. It implements an advanced permanent filesystem, allowing many simultaneous attackers to see the same changing files for the first time. It was evaluated with: (i) unit tests for generative capabilities, (ii) an AI attacker (ARACNE) to assess realism and deception, (iii) human attackers to assess its deceptive capability, and (iv) an Internet deployment to evaluate deception in real-world attacks. In unit test results, AdvancedShelLM achieved a pass rate of up to 99.02%. The AI attacker ARACNE had issues making a decision if the system is honeypot or not, but showed slight bias towards saying honeypot, even for a real Ubuntu shell. With human attackers, AdvancedShelLM deceived more humans than Cowrie, but had similar results as shelLM. The Internet deployment showed concrete evidence that the output of AdvancedShelLM can influence the behaviour of real-life attackers.

</details>


### [121] [Ghost Without Shell: Measuring Non-Interactive SSH Attacks on Honeypots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28006)
*Veronica Valeros,Muris Sladić,Sebastian Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过在15天内部署11个SSH蜜罐并分析177,622次认证会话，发现99.23%的会话是非交互式的，交互式会话仅占0.10%，这一模式在第三方Cowrie数据集中也得到验证。这表明依赖交互式shell或会话长度评估攻击的蜜罐会错过绝大多数认证后攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证现有SSH蜜罐的核心假设——攻击者登录后会打开shell并输入命令——是否仍然成立，因为蜜罐的欺骗能力和情报收集效率依赖于这一假设。

Method: 部署11个同时支持交互式和非交互式会话请求的SSH蜜罐，收集15天的认证会话数据（共177,622个），并使用独立的Cowrie数据集在同一时间窗口内进行交叉验证。

Result: 99.23%的会话是非交互式的，交互式会话仅占0.10%，第三方数据集验证了相同的模式。

Conclusion: 专注于交互式shell或以会话长度和命令数量评估成功的蜜罐会遗漏绝大多数认证后攻击，导致对攻击者行为的错误结论，需要重新设计蜜罐评估方法和欺骗策略。

Abstract: Cyber deception research has focused on improving honeypot deception capabilities to increase attacker engagement and extend their interactions to collect more and better intelligence. For SSH honeypots, this relies on the assumption that attackers log in, open a shell, and type. We tested whether this still held by deploying eleven SSH honeypots that served both interactive and non-interactive session requests for fifteen days. We collected 177,622 authenticated sessions and validated our results against an independent Cowrie dataset over the same time window. We found that 99.23% of sessions were non-interactive. Interactive sessions account for only 0.10%. The same pattern held in the comparative third-party dataset used for evaluation. This finding is important because a honeypot that focuses on interactive shells or evaluates success based on session length and the number of commands can miss most authenticated attacks and draw the wrong conclusions about what attackers do after login.

</details>


### [122] [ToolPrivacyBench: Benchmarking Purpose-Bound Privacy in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28061)
*Shijing Hu,Liang Liu,Zhu Meng,Zhicheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ToolPrivacyBench是一个评估多工具代理在任务执行过程中隐私过度披露的基准，包含2,150个案例，覆盖9种主流代理。研究发现，任务成功执行并不等同于隐私合规，代理可能在中间工具调用中传输不必要的私有信息。该基准通过轨迹级审计来识别目的约束下的隐私泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数调用基准主要评估任务完成度和API正确性，隐私评估基准则聚焦最终响应或隐私判断，两者均无法捕捉多工具轨迹中的目的约束信息流。当前缺乏一个能够同时评估任务完成和工具使用过程中隐私过度披露的评估框架。

Method: 构建包含2,150个案例的基准（1,150个全合成隐私敏感业务工作流+1,000个改编自现有基准的案例），每个案例由策略知识库表示。代理针对模拟业务后端执行后，评估器将记录的工具参数和后端审计日志与策略知识库进行比对，覆盖9种主流代理进行目的约束隐私过度披露评估。

Result: 成功的工具执行并不等同于适当的隐私披露：代理可能在完成任务的同时，通过中间工具调用传输不必要的私有信息。

Conclusion: ToolPrivacyBench形式化了“需要知道”的披露边界，即每个工具只应接收完成其声明目的所需的信息，并通过轨迹级审计识别多工具工作流中的隐私过度披露。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly moved from standalone text generation systems to agents that invoke external tools, access environments, and execute multi-step tasks. However, conventional function-calling benchmarks mainly evaluate task completion and API correctness, while privacy evaluation benchmarks typically focus on final responses or privacy judgments. Neither perspective captures purpose-bound information flow across an executed multi-tool trajectory. Motivated by this limitation in current agent evaluation, ToolPrivacyBench audits whether task-private atoms are routed only to authorized tools and downstream sinks, thereby evaluating both task completion and privacy over-disclosure during tool use. The benchmark contains 2,150 cases, including 1,150 fully synthetic privacy-sensitive business workflows and 1,000 cases adapted from existing multi-tool and function-calling benchmarks. Each case is represented by a policy knowledge base. After an agent executes against mock business backends, the evaluator compares recorded tool arguments and backend audit logs with this policy knowledge base. The evaluation covers nine widely used agents to characterize purpose-bound privacy over-disclosure. The results show that successful tool execution does not imply appropriate privacy disclosure: an agent may complete a task while transmitting unnecessary private information through intermediate tool calls. ToolPrivacyBench therefore formalizes a need-to-know disclosure boundary, under which each tool should receive only the information necessary for its stated purpose, and uses trajectory-level auditing to identify privacy over-disclosure in multi-tool workflows.

</details>


### [123] [GTI-mSEMP Framework : A Proposed Framework to Stimulate Malware Propagation with Inclusion of Attacker-Defender Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28079)
*Shadeeb Hossain,Kristopher Wilson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成博弈论的改进型多无线传感器流行病恶意软件传播（GTI-mSEMP）框架，用于分析异构、资源受限的网络中攻防双方的策略性恶意软件传播动态，并通过三种不同操作 regime 下的数值仿真验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流行病学模型将安全防御视为静态参数，无法捕捉攻防双方之间的策略性不对称博弈，而自动化多向量恶意软件威胁对异构、资源受限的网络造成了重大风险。

Method: 提出GTI-mSEMP框架，将博弈论与改进的多无线传感器流行病模型相结合，分析易感（S）和恢复（R）节点种群在三种操作 regime（平衡对抗、利用激增、强化防御）下的运行轨迹。

Result: 数值仿真展示了网络状态变量的实时瞬态动力学，揭示了当防御或攻击规模向量具有效率优势时流行病曲线的变化特征。

Conclusion: 该数学与数值框架为高度对抗性的网络环境提供了评估动态恶意软件传播和预测局部节点种群状态的严格基础。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of automated, multi-vector malware threats poses a significant risk to heterogeneous, resource constrained cyber-physical networks. Conventional epidemiological models often treat security defenses as static parameters, failing to capture the strategic, asymmetric maneuvers between an attacker and a defender. To address the gap, this paper proposes a Game-Theory-Integrated Modified Multi- Wireless Sensor Epidemic Malware Propagation (GTI-mSEMP) framework. This paper analyzed and compared the operational trajectories of Susceptible (S) and Recovered (R) node populations across three different operational regimes: Balanced Matchup, Exploit Surge and Hardened Defense. Numerical simulation results capture the real-time transient dynamics of the network state variables, demonstrating how the epidemic curve shifts when either the defensive or offensive scaling vectors hold an efficiency advantage. The proposed mathematical and numerical framework provides a rigorous foundation that can be deployed in highly adversarial network environments to evaluate dynamic malware propagation and predict localized node population states.

</details>


### [124] [Robust Harmful Features Under Jailbreak Attacks: Mechanistic Evidence from Attention Head Specialization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28153)
*Yanchen Yin,Dongqi Han,Linghui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了越狱攻击并非全面消除LLM的安全对齐特征，而是选择性地抑制早期层中的特定注意力头（ACHs），而中期层的安全对齐头（SAHs）仍保持鲁棒激活。通过消融实验验证了两者的因果作用，并发现这种鲁棒有害特征可以用于无训练的高效越狱检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对越狱攻击如何绕过LLM安全对齐的底层机制理解不足，缺乏对注意力头层面功能分化的深入分析。

Method: 识别两种功能分化的注意力头类型：早期层的攻击妥协头（ACHs）被攻击抑制，中期层的安全对齐头（SAHs）保持鲁棒激活；通过消融实验和token级归因分析验证其因果机制。

Result: 抑制少量ACHs即可在正常拒绝输入上诱导越狱行为；移除SAHs会削弱中层安全激活；攻击模板token特异性驱动ACH抑制；SAHs的鲁棒激活可直接用于无训练的高效越狱检测。

Conclusion: 越狱攻击通过选择性抑制早期ACHs而非全面消除安全特征来实现绕过，同时SAHs维持的鲁棒有害特征可用于对抗性鲁棒的越狱检测。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks bypass LLM safety alignment, yet their mechanisms remain poorly understood. We provide evidence that attacks do not comprehensively eliminate safety features, but instead selectively suppress specific attention heads. We identify two functionally differentiated types: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs) concentrated in early layers, which are suppressed under attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs) in mid-layers, which maintain robust activations even when attacks succeed. Ablation studies support the causal role of ACHs and the contribution of SAHs to robust activations: suppressing a small number of ACHs is sufficient to induce jailbreak-like behavior on normally refused inputs, while removing SAHs substantially weakens mid-layer safety activations. Token-level attribution further shows that ACH suppression is driven specifically by attack-template tokens, providing a mechanistic account of why attacks can bypass refusal decisions through ACH suppression while leaving internal safety signals sustained by SAHs -- a phenomenon we term Robust Harmful Features. To validate the practical significance of this robustness, we show that simply reading these persistent activations -- without any training -- yields competitive aggregate detection performance with strong adversarial robustness.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Surprises in Proper Positive-Only Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28309)
*Shai Ben-David,Farnam Mansouri,Anay Mehrotra,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文解决了仅从正样本中进行二分类的恰当学习（proper learning）的完整刻画问题，发现其需要有限VC维加上一种新的组合条件——均匀外部可分性（uniform exterior separability）。结果揭示了该模型与标准PAC学习存在显著差异：恰当与不恰当学习可分离、随机化与确定化恰当学习可分离、存在ERM无法学习的概念类，且有限VC维不足以保证非一致学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从正样本中学习的问题自Natarajan [1987]提出以来，不恰当学习（improper learning）的刻画已成熟，但恰当学习（proper learning）的完全刻画长期悬而未决。本文旨在解决这一开放问题，并深入理解该模型下的学习能力边界。

Method: 本文引入了一个新的组合条件——均匀外部可分性（uniform exterior separability），结合有限VC维，给出了概念类可恰当学习的充要条件。同时通过构造分离性结果，展示了该模型与标准PAC学习的诸多差异。

Result: 1) 概念类可恰当学习的充要条件：有限VC维 + 均匀外部可分性。2) 发现多个分离结果：恰当学习与不恰当学习分离、随机化与确定化恰当学习分离、存在无ERM学习器的概念类、有限VC维不足以保证非一致学习。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了正样本二分类的恰当学习刻画问题，揭示了该模型丰富且独特的学习景观，并引入了新的组合维度，有望对更广泛的学习理论产生价值。

Abstract: Binary classification from positive-only samples is a variant of PAC learning in which the learner receives i.i.d. samples from the positive region of an unknown target concept, but is evaluated under the original distribution (which places mass on both positive and negative regions). This model dates back to Natarajan [1987, STOC], and the characterization of improper learning is well-known -- it even appears in textbooks. The characterization of proper positive-only learning, however, has long remained open. In this work, we revisit and settle this question: a concept class is properly learnable from positive-only samples if and only if it has finite VC dimension and satisfies a new combinatorial condition, which we call uniform exterior separability. Together with several separation results, this characterization reveals a surprisingly rich landscape that differs sharply from standard PAC learning: proper and improper learning are separated, randomized and deterministic proper learning are separated, there are classes for which no ERM is a learner, and finite VC dimension does not suffice even for non-uniform learning.
