Along the way, we introduce new combinatorial dimensions that we believe can be of broader interest in learning theory.

</details>


### [126] [Directed Graph Topology Inference via Graph Filter Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27455)
*Rasoul Shafipour,Andrei Buciulea,Santiago Segarra,Antonio G. Marques,Gonzalo Mateos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从节点测量数据中推断有向网络拓扑的方法，该方法基于线性扩散动力学，考虑了图卷积滤波器（未知系数）和任意相关输入信号，通过系统辨识和拓扑推断两阶段算法（含Stiefel流形优化）实现网络恢复，并在合成数据和真实案例中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多假设无向图和白噪声激励，导致图移位算子与观测协方差矩阵可同时对角化，限制了适用性。本文旨在解决有向图场景下，当信号激励具有任意相关节点分量时，如何从线性扩散动力学的节点观测中推断潜在网络拓扑的问题。

Method: 首先利用输出信号测量和输入信号的先验统计信息进行扩散滤波器系统辨识，求解二次矩阵方程组（在输入协方差谱多样性假设下可辨识），并将其重铸为带Stiefel流形约束的光滑二次最小化问题。随后，根据滤波器估计值识别网络拓扑，即寻找与给定滤波器可交换的稀疏且结构可接受的移位算子。最后提出联合图滤波器和拓扑辨识的交替算法，相互强化以改善样本复杂度。

Result: 数值实验证实了所提算法在合成有向图恢复中的有效性，并在真实数据案例研究中展示了其在城市流动性分析和投资组合优化中的潜在应用价值。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法突破了传统方法对无向图和白噪声假设的限制，能够有效从扩散动力学观测中推断有向网络拓扑，且联合优化策略降低了样本复杂度，在多个实际场景中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We address the problem of inferring a directed network from nodal measurements generated by linear diffusion dynamics on the sought graph. Observations are modeled as the outputs of a graph convolutional filter, i.e., a polynomial (with unknown coefficients) of a local diffusion graph-shift operator encoding the latent graph topology, excited with an ensemble of independent graph signals with arbitrarily-correlated nodal components. Unlike prior efforts that considered undirected graphs and white signal excitations, here the graph-shift operator and the observations' covariance matrix are not simultaneously diagonalizable. In this challenging context, we first rely on measurements of the output signals along with prior statistical information on the inputs to identify the diffusion filter. Such system identification problem involves solving a system of quadratic matrix equations, which we show is identifiable under spectral-diversity assumptions on the input covariances. For algorithmic purposes we recast it as a smooth quadratic minimization subject to Stiefel manifold constraints. Subsequent identification of the network topology given the graph filter estimate boils down to finding a sparse and structurally admissible shift that commutes with the given filter, thus, forcing the latter to be a polynomial in the sought graph-shift operator. A joint graph filter and topology identification algorithm is also proposed, which alternates between the aforementioned steps in a mutually reinforcing fashion to offer improved sample complexity. Numerical tests corroborate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms in recovering synthetic digraphs and real-data case studies, and illustrate their potential utility on urban mobility analyses as well as portfolio optimization.

</details>


### [127] [The Decision Geometry of Covariance Estimation for the Global Minimum-Variance Portfolio under Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27462)
*Xavier Fonseca*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了全局最小方差组合(GMVP)决策中协方差矩阵估计误差如何映射为组合次优性，证明了精确的遗憾恒等式和非渐近界，表明决策遗憾仅通过组合权重、组合集中度和真实协方差条件数受估计误差影响。据此推导出决策几何：GMVP遗憾对(p-1)维投影不变。应用于厚尾收益时，建立了收敛速率，并指出决策聚焦优势在于更优常数和集中度折扣而非更快速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协方差矩阵估计方法普遍使用矩阵范数损失进行评估，但这并非GMVP决策所依赖的目标。需要精确刻画协方差估计误差如何转化为GMVP的决策次优性，建立决策导向的评估框架。

Method: 推导精确的遗憾恒等式和非渐近界；从决策几何出发分析误差投影；应用于厚尾分布(tail index kappa∈(2,4))并用偏斜-t/t-copula模拟验证。

Result: 遗憾依赖于误差在组合权重方向上的作用、组合集中度和真实协方差条件数；遗憾对(p-1)维投影不变；厚尾下收敛速率由中心化算子范数速率决定；决策聚焦优势是更优常数和集中度折扣。

Conclusion: 为决策聚焦学习提供了精确的估计几何和一致性理论，补充了现有方法缺乏的理论基础。

Abstract: The global minimum-variance portfolio (GMVP) is the canonical decision built from an estimated covariance matrix, yet covariance estimators are universally evaluated by matrix-norm loss, which is not the object the decision depends on. We characterise exactly how covariance-estimation error maps into GMVP suboptimality. We prove an exact regret identity and a non-asymptotic bound showing decision regret depends on the estimation error only through its action on the portfolio weights, scaled by portfolio concentration and the conditioning of the true covariance. From this we derive the decision geometry: GMVP regret is invariant to a (p-1)-dimensional projection of the p^2-dimensional error matrix, with invariance to the covariance-scale direction as an exact special case. We then apply the framework to heavy-tailed returns (tail index kappa in (2,4)), establishing the regret convergence rate implied by the centred operator-norm rate, and confirm the theory on a skew-t/t-copula simulation design with pre-registered analysis. The decision-focused advantage is a sharper constant and a concentration discount rather than a faster rate; we report an honest high-conditioning boundary of the rate prediction. The results complement recent decision-focused learning approaches by supplying the exact estimation geometry and consistency theory they lack.

</details>


### [128] [Adversarial Contamination Meets Hard Thresholding: An Iterative Algorithm with Signal Adaptivity and Minimax Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27685)
*Shixiang Liu,Hanming Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段抗污染迭代硬阈值（AC-IHT）算法，用于存在污染的高维回归问题。该非凸算法通过以不同阈值尺度迭代更新系数向量和污染向量，实现了极小极大近优估计，具有信号自适应性和强预言性质，数值实验验证了其优越性能，并讨论了向广义线性模型和重尾噪声的理论扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据污染（测量误差、异常值或对抗性破坏）普遍存在，促使需要开发稳健的统计方法来解决高维回归中的污染问题。

Method: 提出两阶段抗污染迭代硬阈值（AC-IHT）算法，通过以不同阈值尺度迭代更新系数向量和污染向量，是一种非凸优化算法。

Result: 该算法实现了极小极大近优估计（对数项内），具有信号自适应性，在适当信号条件下可自适应地获得更优的估计率和更精确的支持恢复；具备强预言性质；数值实验验证了其优越的有限样本性能。

Conclusion: AC-IHT算法能有效应对高维回归中的数据污染问题，理论上具有强预言性质为渐近推断奠定基础，并可扩展到广义线性模型和重尾噪声场景。

Abstract: Pervasive data contamination -- stemming from measurement errors, outliers, or adversarial corruption -- has motivated the development of robust statistical methods. In this context, we propose a two-stage Adversarial Contamination-resistant Iterative Hard Thresholding (AC-IHT) algorithm for high-dimensional regression with contamination. Our nonconvex algorithm achieves minimax near-optimal (up to logarithmic terms) estimation by iteratively updating the coefficient vector and the contamination vector with different thresholding scales. We further demonstrate that our AC-IHT estimator is signal-adaptive: under proper signal conditions, it adaptively attains a sharper estimation rate and more accurate support recovery. Moreover, it enjoys the strong oracle property, laying a theoretical foundation for asymptotic inference. Numerical experiments confirm its superior finite-sample performance. Finally, we discuss theoretical extensions of the proposed procedure to generalized linear models and to heavy-tailed noise settings.

</details>


### [129] [Local Fokker--Planck Geometry for Score Estimation: Heat-Ball Mean-Value Representations and Exact High-Dimensional Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27954)
*Jiayao Bai,Lang Deng,Yi Du,Yifei Jia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性漂移下分数函数难以估计的问题，提出局部Fokker–Planck几何框架，通过时间变换和热球均值表示精确计算分数函数，并引入κ-测度实现高维蒙特卡洛采样，在2D结构数据和256维MNIST上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分数匹配方法（如去噪分数匹配、全局Fokker–Planck残差惩罚）在处理非线性漂移时依赖全局平均，在低密度区域估计误差大，而这正是精度最关键的区域。作者旨在解决这一精度瓶颈，提供局部精确的分数估计方法。

Method: 1) 通过时间变换将变系数Fokker–Planck方程化为标准非齐次热方程，扩展Evans热球单调性方法导出分数、密度、对数密度和熵密度的精确局部均值表示，并在显式维度相关漂移预算下建立局部适定性。2) 针对高维蒙特卡洛评估引入κ-测度，推导出其精确因子化采样器（单位样本权重）及χ²₂径向集中性质。3) 证明r→0极限下热球残差恢复点态Fokker–Planck残差，局部框架是全局FP残差方法的单参数推广。

Result: 在2D结构数据和256维MNIST数据集上验证了框架有效性，并通过专用采样器研究确认了浓度定律。

Conclusion: 局部Fokker–Planck几何框架通过局部抛物平均替代全局平均，有效提升低密度区域分数估计精度，是全局FP残差方法的有益推广，为非线性漂移下的分数估计提供了新路径。

Abstract: Score-based generative models and Langevin samplers rely on estimating the score function $\nabla_x\log p_t(x)$ of a forward diffusion. Classically this is tractable when the drift is linear: the marginal density is Gaussian and the score is a global conditional expectation. For a general nonlinear, state-dependent drift the marginal density has no closed form, and existing methods--denoising score matching and global Fokker--Planck residual penalties--resort to global averaging that inflates estimation error in low-density regions precisely where accuracy is most critical. We address this by developing a local Fokker--Planck geometric framework that replaces global conditioning with local parabolic averaging. Our approach rests on three contributions. First, a time change to the cumulative-variance coordinate reduces the variable-coefficient Fokker--Planck equation to a standard inhomogeneous heat equation, on which we extend Evans' classical heat-ball monotonicity method to derive exact local mean-value representations for the score $\nabla_x\log p$ together with the density, log-density, and entropy density; local well-posedness is established under an explicit dimension-dependent drift budget. Second, for high-dimensional Monte Carlo evaluation of the resulting heat-ball integrals, we introduce the $κ$-measure and derive its exact factorized sampler with unit per-sample weight, $χ^2_2$ radial concentration. Third, the $r\to0$ limit of the heat-ball residual recovers the pointwise Fokker--Planck residual, showing that the local framework is a one-parameter generalization of global FP-residual methods, and that the DSM population minimizer is feasible for the heat-ball constraint at every scale. We validate the framework on 2D structured data on 256-dimensional MNIST, and on a dedicated sampler study confirming the concentration laws.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Fast-Mixing Markov Chains without Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27564)
*Robert Kutri,Robert Scheichl*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DART的MCMC加速框架，通过正则化和退火技术，利用替代密度实现梯度级几何信息的高效利用，在强对数凹目标上达到了与MALA相当或更优的混合时间保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MCMC加速方法要么依赖昂贵的几何信息（如梯度），要么通过替代密度降低每步采样成本，但难以兼顾效率与精度。本文旨在提出一种无需显式计算目标或替代梯度，却能利用梯度级几何信息的替代转移MCMC方法。

Method: 提出延迟接受结合正则化与退火（DART）框架。通过正则化和退火技术调整替代提议测度，使得期望提议位移在受控误差下与朗之万漂移一致，从而在不计算梯度的前提下利用几何信息。

Result: 在强对数凹目标上，DART从热启动出发的混合时间为O(κ·max{κ, d})，当d ≥ κ时与MALA的O(κd)率匹配，当d < κ时尺度为O(κ²)且与维度无关。这是首个具有混合时间保证的基于替代转移的MCMC方法。

Conclusion: DART在层次空间广义线性混合模型上进行了验证，其中Dirichlet-Neumann平均参数化被重新用于提供替代，其线性内存和拟线性算术复杂度得以继承到推断中，展示了方法的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Most approaches for accelerating Markov chain mixing either rely on incorporating expensive geometric information in the proposals, or reduce the per-step cost of sampling via surrogate densities. We propose a localisation principle that allows a surrogate-based Metropolis-Hastings proposal to exploit gradient-level geometric information of the target density, without evaluating either the target gradient or the surrogate gradient. The construction relies on regularisation and tempering of the proposal measure. We show that the expected proposal displacement coincides with the Langevin drift up to controlled error. The resulting framework, Delayed Acceptance with Regularisation and Tempering (DART), achieves an $O(κ\max\{κ, d\})$ mixing time from warm start for strongly log-concave targets with condition number $κ$ in $d$ dimensions. This matches the known $O(κd)$ rate for MALA when $d \ge κ$, and scales as $O(κ^2)$, independent of dimension, otherwise. This is, to our knowledge, the first mixing time guarantee for a surrogate-transition-based MCMC method. We demonstrate DART on a hierarchical spatial generalised linear mixed model. In this setting, the Dirichlet-Neumann averaging parametrisation, originally introduced for the efficient simulation of Gaussian processes, is repurposed to supply the surrogate, and its linear memory and log-linear arithmetic scaling in the number of observation sites carry over to inference.

</details>


### [131] [Optimal Estimators for Heavy-Tailed Mean Estimation via Convex Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27899)
*Bart P. G. van Parys,Bert Zwart*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 针对仅已知位于对称矩类中的位置参数，提出了最优估计方法，给出了大偏差精确刻画，并通过凸规划构造了单调M估计器，取得了非渐近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非参数框架下，当分布仅属于对称矩类（均值为零、有界矩）时，如何对位置参数进行最优估计。重点研究固定误差 margin 和固定置信度两种 regime 下的 minimax 最优性。

Method: 采用大偏差方法刻画最小最坏情况错误概率的指数率，将其表示为两点 Hellinger 指数；通过拉格朗日对偶将无限维搜索化简为两个乘子，构造由两参数凸规划合成的单调 M-估计器；利用夹层形状（sandwich shape）的包络线刻画最优估计函数。

Result: 固定 margin 下，错误概率指数率等于平移后分布类的两点 Hellinger 指数，且非渐近地可达（β_n(Δ) ≤ e^{-nr(Δ)}）；对于有界方差类，指数为 r(Δ)=½log(1+Δ²/σ²)。固定置信度下，该估计器在多个具体类上达到 leading-order 最优，包括 Catoni 的 √2 常数和 Lee/Bhatt 等人的 L(α) 常数。

Conclusion: 该合成方法在两类 regime 下均达到最优，最不利分布结构简单（至多三个原子支撑），且夹层形状包络线自然导出，而非先验假设。

Abstract: We study optimal estimation of the location parameter of a distribution known only to lie in a symmetric moment class $\mathcal C_0$: the mean-zero distributions with bounded moment $\intφ\, d\mathbb P\le B$ for a fixed even $φ$. Our main result concerns the fixed-margin regime, where the error margin $Δ$ is fixed as $n\to\infty$: we give an exact large-deviation characterization of the smallest worst-case probability $β_n(Δ)$ of an error exceeding $Δ$ that any measurable estimator can guarantee with $n$ observations. Its exponential rate is exactly a two-point Hellinger exponent over the class shifted to means $\pmΔ$, $r(Δ)=-\log\sup_{\mathbb P_{\pmΔ}\in\mathcal C_{\pmΔ}}\int\sqrt{d\mathbb P_{-Δ}\, d\mathbb P_Δ}$, achieved non-asymptotically, $β_n(Δ)\le e^{-nr(Δ)}$, by a monotone $M$-estimator synthesized from a two-parameter convex program. Lagrangian duality collapses the infinite-dimensional search over estimating functions to two multipliers, which determine a pair of envelopes characterizing the optimal estimating functions; the sandwich shape posited ad hoc in prior constructions emerges naturally. For bounded variance ($φ(x)=x^2$, $B=σ^2$) the exponent is $r(Δ)=\tfrac12\log(1+Δ^2/σ^2)$. In the fixed-confidence regime, holding $β$ fixed and letting the optimal margin $Δ_n(β)$ shrink with $n$, the same synthesis stays optimal to leading order for several concrete classes. As $β\downarrow0$ it attains the sharp constant $\sqrt2$ of Catoni for bounded variance and the constant $L(α)$ of Lee and Bhatt et al. for bounded $α$-moments, $α\in(1,2)$, thereby shown tight; for slowly varying $φ$ it is leading-order minimax at every fixed $β$. The least-favorable distributions are simple, supported on at most three atoms.

</details>


### [132] [Global convergence analysis of mixtures of Exponential densities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28053)
*Rajita Chandak,Kathryn Dullerud*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文首次证明EM算法在混合指数分布（非高斯混合模型）中能以次指数收敛速率在O(log n)次迭代内达到最优估计，表明EM算法的统计性能不受限于高斯假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EM算法的理论基础通常建立在高斯混合模型上，但其实际应用远超高斯模型。本文旨在探索EM算法在非高斯混合密度（如指数分布混合）下的理论行为，填补该领域的理论空白。

Method: 理论分析：将高斯混合模型中的典型假设（如最小均值分离、信噪比）推广到次指数分布场景；通过数学推导证明混合指数分布的EM算法具有次指数收敛速率，且最多需O(log n)次迭代。仿真实验验证理论结果。

Result: EM算法在混合指数分布下保持次指数收敛速率，迭代复杂度为O(log n)；非高斯场景不损害EM算法的统计性能；推广的假设条件在仿真中表现良好。

Conclusion: EM算法的优良统计性能可拓展至非高斯混合模型（如指数分布混合），为更大类别混合模型的理论扩展奠定了基础。

Abstract: The theoretical foundations of the EM algorithm are often thought of in the context of Gaussian mixture models, However, the practical use cases of the EM algorithm span beyond Gaussian models. This paper establishes the first step towards understanding the behavior of the EM algorithm under mixtures of non-Gaussian densities. We show that a mixture of two Exponential distributions can be approximated by the EM algorithm at the sub-Exponential rate of convergence in at most $\log(n)$ iterations. The results here show that extending away from Gaussian mixture models does not affect the statistical performance of the EM algorithm. Furthermore, we present generalizations of typical assumptions in the Gaussian setting like minimum mean-separation and signal-to-noise ratio to the sub-Exponential setting. A simulation study is used to highlight the empirical performance of EM for mixtures of exponentials with promising results for the extension of existing theory to a larger class of mixture models.

</details>


### [133] [The multiply iterated law of the iterated logarithm: game-theoretic foundations of sequential detection boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28324)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文揭示了任意时点有效的置信序列和e过程构建中的混合先验本质上是两玩家博弈中的均衡策略，证明了重对数律是该序贯检测博弈的极小极大边界，并推导出最优先验（均衡器）的封闭形式——其第一迭代对数校正系数为3/2，在log-log尺度下等价于尺度之尺度上的Jeffreys先验。Erdős-Kolmogorov积分检验是选择该先验的准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任意时点有效置信序列和e过程的构建几乎都依赖于对倾斜尺度上的先验进行平均，再通过Ville不等式得到非负上鞅。但混合先验的宽度通常由人工经验选择，缺乏理论指导。本文旨在揭示先验选择背后的博弈论本质，建立统一的路径wise Gibbs变分恒等式，并导出最优先验的封闭形式。

Method: 将混合过程重新诠释为两玩家博弈：学习者选择先验，自然自适应地产生均值为零的得分过程，其难度由累积生成函数定价。推导出沿每条实现路径成立的路径wise Gibbs变分恒等式，该恒等式统一了Ville不等式、均衡条件、GROW表征和鞍点公式。通过博弈论分析证明重对数律是序贯检测的极小极大边界，并求解均衡器先验得到第一迭代对数校正系数的封闭形式3/2。

Result: (1) 重对数律是序贯检测博弈的极小极大边界；(2) 最优先验不是设计选择而是被迫的均衡器策略，其第一迭代对数校正系数为3/2（Erdős基线为1，Laplace鞍点包络为1/2）；(3) 在log-log尺度下均衡器恰好是尺度之尺度上的Jeffreys先验；(4) Erdős-Kolmogorov积分检验是选择该先验的准则；(5) 两阶段有限时间重对数律证明、Howard-Ramdas混合与拼接构造、对赌置信序列均可视为该均衡器原理的实例。

Conclusion: 本文建立了任意时点有效推断中先验选择的博弈论基础，揭示了重对数律与最优先验之间的深层联系，为置信序列和e过程的自动化最优设计提供了理论指导。伴随的实证评估验证了核心恒等式，并确认Erdős阈值在预测值处。

Abstract: Anytime-valid confidence sequences and e-processes are built almost universally from one recipe: average exponential test statistics over a prior on the tilting scale, then invoke Ville's inequality on the resulting nonnegative supermartingale. The mixing prior sets the width of the detection boundary and is usually chosen by hand. We recast the recipe as a two-player game with information as currency. A Learner commits to the prior; Nature adaptively produces a mean-zero score process whose difficulty is priced by a cumulant-generating-function charge. The Learner's mixture wealth obeys a single pathwise Gibbs-variational identity that holds along every realized path with no expectation operator; Ville's inequality, the equalizer condition, the GROW characterization, and the saddlepoint formula are all specializations of it. Three messages organize the rest. First, the law of the iterated logarithm (LIL) is the minimax boundary of this sequential-detection game, not arbitrary combinatorial slack. Second, the optimal prior is not a design choice but the forced equalizer strategy -- the unique law that makes every boundary-crossing time equally costly for Nature -- and it yields the sharp first iterated-log correction in closed form, with coefficient 3/2 = 1 + 1/2 (one for the Erdős baseline, one half for the Laplace envelope around the saddle). Third, in the log-log scale chart the equalizer is exactly the Jeffreys prior on the scale-of-scales. The Erdős-Kolmogorov integral test is the criterion that selects it. The two-stage finite-time LIL proof, the Howard-Ramdas mixture and stitching constructions, and betting confidence sequences all read as instances of this equalizer principle. A companion empirical evaluation confirms the central identities and locates the Erdős threshold at the predicted value.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Productionized Fairness Measurement Under Privacy Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27558)
*Osonde A. Osoba,Yuzi He,Saikrishna Badrinarayanan,Varun Mithal,Sakshi Jain,Natesh S. Pillai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PPRE方法，结合安全两方计算、差分隐私和加法同态加密等技术，在保护隐私的前提下利用贝叶斯改进姓氏地理编码估计器和稀疏黄金调查集两种信号源，实现对LinkedIn美国会员按种族/族裔进行公平性测量，并提供了可迁移的隐私保护测量基础设施框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 种族和族裔信号在公平性评估中至关重要，但这类人口统计信号受法律约束且文化敏感，难以合规地收集和使用。现有方法在隐私保护和公平性测量之间缺乏有效的平衡方案。

Method: PPRE方法在两种种族/族裔人口统计信号源（贝叶斯改进姓氏地理编码估计器BISG和稀疏的自我报告人口统计调查集）之上，叠加了三种隐私保护技术：安全两方计算、差分隐私和加法同态加密，构建隐私保护公平性测量方案。

Result: 详细阐述了PPRE的隐私保证，并在候选人和观众两侧的公平性测量中展示了该方法的实际应用效果。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个可迁移的隐私保护测量基础设施框架，可供其他有类似需求的机构参考实施。

Abstract: Fairness measurements in the form of disaggregated evaluations often rely on demographic signals that are legally constrained or culturally sensitive. Race and ethnicity signals are among the more difficult signals to curate and use for this task. This paper presents Privacy-Preserving Probabilistic Race/Ethnicity Estimation (PPRE) as a method for enabling fairness measurements with respect to race/ethnicity for U.S.\ LinkedIn members in a privacy-preserving manner. PPRE applies privacy technologies (specifically: secure two-party computation, differential privacy, and additive homomorphic encryption) on top of two race/ethnicity demographic signal sources (the Bayesian Improved Surname Geocoding estimator and a sparse golden survey set of self-reported demographics) to power a fairness measurement solution with respect to US-based race/ethnicity demographics. We detail its privacy guarantees and demonstrate its application on candidate- and viewer-side fairness measurements. We close with a transferable framework for institutions seeking to implement similar privacy-preserving measurement infrastructure.

</details>


### [135] [Halt Fast! Early Stopping for Certified Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27694)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于元学习的任意有效认证鲁棒性框架，通过轻量级元学习器预测图像特定先验，实现20倍样本复杂度降低，并支持自适应计算资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准随机平滑需要大量模型评估（数万次/输入）且需预先固定样本量，计算成本极高，限制了实际部署。

Method: 采用元学习框架，使用轻量级元学习器为顺序E过程预测图像特定的先验分布，实现自适应计算资源分配。

Result: 相比传统方法实现了20倍样本复杂度降低，同时保持严格的统计保证和相当的认证性能。

Conclusion: 该方法支持基于应用风险阈值的自适应计算分配，实现了传统认证框架无法做到的资源分诊，为实时安全关键认证部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Randomized Smoothing (RS) provides rigorous robustness guarantees for neural networks without architectural constraints, yet its adoption is limited by extreme computational costs. Standard RS requires tens of thousands of model evaluations per input and forces practitioners to commit to fixed sample sizes a priori. In this work, we present a novel meta-learning framework for anytime-valid certified robustness that adaptively deploys computational resources. By using a lightweight meta-learner to predict image-specific priors for a sequential E-process, we achieve a 20-fold reduction in sample complexity compared to traditional methods while maintaining rigorous statistical guarantees. Beyond raw efficiency, we demonstrate how anytime-validity enables adaptively allocating compute based upon application-specific risk thresholds, a form of resource triage impossible under classic certification frameworks. That this is achievable while also providing similar certification performance demonstrates that our approach provides a pathway for real-time, safety-critical certification deployments.

</details>


### [136] [What Was That Again? Certified Robustness for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27698)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Neil Marchant,Jiani Xie,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于认证机制的诊断方法，通过双门控流程（双边原子审计+排名锦标赛）在无真实转录先验知识的情况下，显著降低ASR系统的词错误率（最高55%），并提升召回率与声学安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统对对抗性及良性扰动高度敏感，但在实际部署中缺乏真实转录作为参照，使得检测此类行为极为困难。

Method: 提出双门控诊断流程：1）双边原子审计（Two-Sided Atomic Audit），通过积累统计证据来认证token存在性与对抗性排除；2）排名锦标赛（Rank-Based Tournament），选择获胜序列。

Result: 在四种不同架构上，词错误率相对降低最高达55%，同时实现词级与句级粒度认证，增强了声学安全性。

Conclusion: 认证启发的机制能够在无真实转录先验知识的情况下，有效提升ASR系统对扰动的鲁棒性，并提供可解释的认证保障。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition systems are notoriously both sensitive to adversarial and benign perturbations. While this has been repeatedly demonstrated using reference datasets, detecting such behaviors in deployed systems is incredibly challenging, due to the absence of oracle knowledge of the true transcription. We demonstrate that employing a certification-inspired mechanism can significantly decrease WER, increase recall, and decrease the Spearman correlation between confidence and WER. We achieve this through a dual-gate diagnostic pipeline: a Two-Sided Atomic Audit that accumulates statistical wealth to certify both token existence and adversarial exclusion, and a Rank-Based Tournament that selects the winning sequence. Our evaluations across four diverse architectures demonstrate up to a 55% relative reduction in Word Error Rate, while also providing granular word- and sentence-level certifications to enhance acoustic security.

</details>


### [137] [OverFlowLight: Real-Time Gridlock Prevention and Traffic Signal Optimization for Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27381)
*Mingyuan Li,Boyang Huang,Tianqi Jiang,Chenpu Li,Chunyu Liu,Yang Li,Ruimin Li,Qiang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OverFlowLight是一个实时交通信号控制框架，通过多模态感知检测队列溢出并动态插入专用相位，结合基于规则和强化学习的混合控制策略，在实际部署中将溢出事件减少60.4%，网络吞吐量提升18.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制算法主要优化吞吐量，但在高峰时段无法有效应对队列溢出问题，导致上游交通阻塞并引发级联拥堵，且缺乏实时检测和主动预防溢出的实用框架。

Method: 提出OverFlowLight框架：(1) 利用摄像头和雷达的多模态感知实现实时溢出检测；(2) 动态生成并插入专用溢出相位清除阻塞队列；(3) 混合控制设计，结合快速基于规则的溢出干预和面向长期效率的强化学习后端。

Result: 在三个主要城市的43个交叉口实际部署，溢出事件减少60.4%，网络吞吐量提升18.2%，可无缝集成现有基于强化学习的TSC智能体，并显著减少人工干预需求。

Conclusion: 本文提出了首个实用、可扩展、数据驱动的主动预防交通死锁框架，为构建韧性高效的城市交通系统提供了关键组件。

Abstract: Queue overflow, a severe consequence of urban traffic congestion, occurs when vehicle queues exceed intersection capacity, obstructing upstream traffic and triggering cascading gridlocks. Prevailing traffic signal control (TSC) algorithms, primarily optimized for throughput, often fail to address overflow during peak hours, exacerbating congestion and creating safety hazards. We propose OverFlowLight, a real-time framework designed to preemptively resolve overflow and enhance overall TSC performance. It first introduces a mechanism to accurately detect overflow in real-time by leveraging multi-modal sensing from cameras and radars. Upon detection, it dynamically generates and inserts dedicated overflow phases into the signal cycle to clear the blocking queues. This is orchestrated by a hybrid control design that combines rapid rule-based overflow intervention with controller back ends such as reinforcement learning (RL) for longer-horizon efficiency. We conducted extensive real-world deployments of OverFlowLight across 43 intersections in three major cities. The framework demonstrates seamless integration with existing RL-based TSC agents, highlighting its modularity and practical applicability. Empirical results show that OverFlowLight reduces overflow incidents by 60.4% and increases network throughput by 18.2% compared to deployed baselines. Furthermore, it substantially diminishes the need for manual intervention common with expert-tuned signal plans. This work presents the first practical, scalable, and data-driven framework for actively preventing traffic gridlock, offering a crucial component for building resilient and efficient urban transportation systems. Our demonstration videos, codes and datasets are available at the anonymous URL, https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OverFlowLight-FBF9.

</details>


### [138] [hia-gat: A Heterogeneous Interaction-Aware Graph Attention Network For Frame-Level Traffic Conflict Risk Prediction On Freeways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27577)
*Mahshid Malazizi,Seyedmehdi Khaleghian,Mina Sartipi,Toru Hirano,Yunfei Xu,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将帧级高速公路风险评估建模为多智能体场景图级的二分类问题，提出HIA-GAT—一种双流异构图注意力网络，通过专用注意力路径分别处理纵向和横向交互，并利用基于SSM冲突归因的事件级门控监督机制融合两者。在NGSIM数据集上，HIA-GAT在多种TTC和PET阈值下达到最优风险排序性能（I-80 AUC 0.835，US-101 AUC 0.867），且门控机制提供了可解释的每车冲突类型归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高速公路风险评估方法多基于轨迹级或片段级分析，缺乏对帧级细粒度风险的建模；同时，现有方法未能充分建模车辆间纵向（同车道）和横向（相邻车道）交互的异构性，尤其是横向冲突（如换道）对图结构信息的依赖未被系统研究。

Method: 构建每帧的关系感知图（车辆为节点，同车道和相邻车道为两种边类型），融入基于物理的边特征（分别对应追尾和换道冲突机制）；提出HIA-GAT双流异构图注意力网络，包含纵向和横向两个独立注意力通路，通过冲突类型感知的门控机制融合，门控受SSM冲突归因的事件级监督。

Result: 在NGSIM I-80和US-101数据集上，HIA-GAT在9种TTC和PET阈值配置下平均AUC最优（I-80: 0.835, US-101: 0.867），在纯PET（换道）设置上增益最大；图结构对横向冲突风险建模至关重要，纵向风险可由非关系模型捕捉。

Conclusion: 图结构对横向冲突风险建模不可或缺，而纵向风险可用非关系聚合方法近似；HIA-GAT的门控机制提供了可解释的每车冲突类型归因，支撑实时可操作的高速公路安全监控。

Abstract: This paper formulates frame-level freeway risk assessment as a multi-agent scene graph-level binary classification problem, where each video or trajectory frame is labeled risky if any TTC- or PET-based conflict violates a specified severity threshold. We construct a relation-aware graph per frame with vehicles as nodes and two interaction types as edges: same-lane (longitudinal) and adjacent-lane (lateral), augmented with physics-informed edge features aligned to rear-end and lane-change conflict mechanisms. Building on a structured benchmarking suite of non-graph models and graph baselines, we propose HIA-GAT, a dual-stream heterogeneous graph attention network that processes longitudinal and lateral interactions through dedicated attention pathways and fuses them via a conflict-type-aware gating mechanism with event-level gate supervision derived from SSM conflict attribution. Experiments on the NGSIM I-80 and US-101 freeway datasets across nine TTC and PET threshold configurations show that HIA-GAT achieves the best average risk-ranking performance (AUC 0.835 on I-80 and 0.867 on US-101), with the largest gains on PET-only (lane-change) settings where relational structure is essential. Beyond accuracy, the learned gate provides interpretable per-vehicle attribution of dominant conflict type, supporting actionable, real-time freeway safety monitoring. We show that graph structure is critical for modeling lateral conflict risk, while longitudinal risk can often be captured by non-relational aggregation.

</details>


### [139] [PEBS: Per-rater Empirical-Bayes Shrinkage for RLHF Reward-Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27578)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEBS是一种面向RLHF奖励模型中标注者特异性仿射校准的闭式后验估计器，通过对每个标注者拟合独立校准器并应用经验贝叶斯收缩，在PRISM和PluriHarms数据集上分别实现8.58%和9.66%的RMSE降低，且无需重新训练奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RLHF奖励模型将所有标注者的偏好数据合并，拟合一个全局仿射校准器，忽略了标注者之间在评分尺度和偏移上的系统性差异，导致平均拟合无法匹配任何单个标注者。

Method: PEBS为每个标注者拟合独立的仿射校准器，并在保留集上应用Morris-James-Stein经验贝叶斯收缩向总体均值靠拢，整个过程以闭式完成，无需重新训练奖励模型。

Result: 在PRISM数据集上，PEBS将用户内持有集RMSE相比全局基线降低8.58%；在PluriHarms危害评分上（Qwen-2.5基座模型）降低9.66%。

Conclusion: PEBS是一种有效的标注者特异性仿射校准方法，能在不改变奖励基础模型的前提下，仅通过推断时使用的标注者级映射来提升RLHF奖励建模的个体拟合质量。

Abstract: Reward models for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pool preferences across thousands of annotators and fit one global affine calibrator, collapsing raters with systematically different rating-scale offsets and slopes into a single average-rater fit that does not match any individual annotator. PEBS is a per-rater empirical-Bayes shrinkage estimator: it fits per-rater affine calibrators on a held-out slice of each annotator's ratings and applies Morris-James-Stein empirical-Bayes shrinkage toward the population mean, in closed form and without retraining the reward model. On PRISM, PEBS reduces within-user held-out RMSE by 8.58% over the pooled population-slope baseline. The procedure replicates on PluriHarms harm ratings (Qwen-2.5 base, in-family) with a +9.66% RMSE reduction over the same population-slope baseline. PEBS is a closed-form post-hoc estimator for annotator-specific affine calibration in RLHF reward modeling; it leaves the reward base model unchanged and estimates only the rater-level map used at inference time for new ratings.

</details>


### [140] [Retroactive Advantage Correction: Closed-Form V-Trace Bias Correction for Delay-Aware RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27580)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出回溯优势修正（RAC）方法，解决RLHF中奖励信号异步（如代码执行验证器、评委集成、人工审核等延迟反馈）导致标准PPO失效的问题。RAC通过非负核函数对延迟完成的奖励进行排队、老化，并作为裁剪残差注入下一优化步的优势估计中。理论证明其无偏性条件与偏差界，在表格MDP实验中相比等待策略降低策略偏差最高47.9倍，且仅需两行代码即可集成到PPO/GRPO中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际生产环境中的RLHF经常面临奖励信号异步问题——代码执行验证器、慢速评委集成或排队的人工审核等延迟反馈会破坏标准PPO的同步奖励假设，导致策略优化出现偏差。

Method: 提出RAC方法：将每个待处理的慢速完成结果（奖励）放入队列，通过非负核函数进行老化，然后作为裁剪后的残差注入到下一优化步的优势估计中。理论证明在无偏裁剪重要性比率下，当有效延迟核注入全部质量时累积RAC修正完全无偏，否则偏差与未注入质量呈线性关系；在无延迟恒等核下退化为V-trace。

Result: 在表格MDP概念验证中，RAC在双慢通道配置下将闭式策略偏差降低高达47.9倍，同时比等待策略（wait-for-slow）具有更低的墙钟成本。

Conclusion: RAC有效解决了异步奖励信号下的RLHF问题，理论性质良好，实现简单（仅需两行代码补丁即可集成PPO/GRPO），在偏差-效率权衡上显著优于等待策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) in production does not always have a synchronous reward signal. Code-execution verifiers, slow judge ensembles, and queued human review can return several gradient steps after the rollout that produced them, breaking the synchronous-reward assumption underlying standard PPO. We address this gap with Retroactive Advantage Correction (RAC): each pending slow completion is queued, aged through a non-negative kernel, and reinjected as a clipped residual into the next optimiser step's advantage. We prove that under an unbiased clipped importance ratio, the cumulative RAC correction is exactly unbiased when the effective delay kernel reinjects all of its mass, and carries a bias linear in the unreinjected fraction otherwise; at the no-delay identity kernel it reduces to V-trace. On a tabular Markov decision process (MDP) proof-of-concept, RAC reduces the closed-form policy bias by up to 47.9x at the two-slow-channel configuration, beating wait-for-slow at lower wall-clock cost. RAC integrates with PPO and GRPO through a two-line reward-manager patch.

</details>


### [141] [Global Explanations for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Models via $K$-Order Markov Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27599)
*Amadeo Tunyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KARMA方法，通过构建马尔可夫替代模型来解释时间序列预测器，捕捉时间依赖关系，并在合成和真实数据上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法大多假设时间戳特征独立，忽略了时间序列数据的时间依赖性，导致解释结果违背数据的时序和因果结构。

Method: KARMA包含三个核心步骤：(1) 识别预测所需的最小历史长度K；(2) 从离散化的历史空间中估计最优的K阶马尔可夫转移核；(3) 从马尔可夫转移核中推导出五级全局解释层次结构。

Result: 在合成数据上，KARMA能够恢复模型学习的因果结构，并且在识别时间依赖性方面优于TimeSHAP等归因方法；在真实北京PM2.5数据上展示了五级解释层次。

Conclusion: KARMA通过显式建模时序依赖性，为时间序列预测模型提供了忠实且可解释的解释框架，优于忽视时序结构的传统归因方法。

Abstract: While many explainable AI (XAI) methods have been proposed, most are not designed for time-series forecasting models and often rely on the implicit assumption that timestamp features are independent. This assumption ignores the fundamental property of temporal dependence and can lead to explanations that violate the sequential and causal structure of the data. We introduce \textsc{KARMA}, a method for explaining time-series predictors by constructing a Markov surrogate model that captures the temporal dependencies learned by the predictor. Our approach revolves around three main aspects: identifying the minimal history length $K$ that is predictively sufficient for the model, estimating the best-fitting $K$-order Markov transition kernel from the discretized history space, and a five-level global explanation hierarchy that can be derived from the Markov transition kernel, which we illustrate using real-world weather data (Beijing PM 2.5). We also certify using complex synthetic data with known true causal edges that KARMA (i) recovers the data causal structure as learned by the model via a controlled experiment and (ii) identifies temporal dependencies better than established attribution methods such as TimeSHAP.

</details>


### [142] [HybridCodec: Modeling Discrete and Continuous Representations for Efficient Speech Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27627)
*Artem Ploujnikov,Francesco Verdini,Samir Sadok,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合离散-连续音频表示方法，通过结合时间压缩的离散令牌和降维的连续残差，减少离散化导致的信息损失，在保留说话人特征的同时减少自回归步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散音频表示在多模态文本-音频系统和LLM集成中广泛应用，但离散化过程会导致信息损失，造成下游任务性能下降。

Method: 提出混合离散-连续焦点调制编解码器（focal modulation codec）和混合Transformer架构。该架构在离散域进行自回归推理，同时结合非自回归预测和连续残差上采样。

Result: 相比纯离散方法，该方法显著提升了说话人特征的保留能力，同时减少了所需的自回归步数。

Conclusion: 混合离散-连续表示策略能有效缓解离散化带来的信息损失，在效率和性能之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Discrete audio representations have become increasingly popular for building multimodal text-audio systems and integrating audio capabilities into Large Language Models (LLMs). However, numerous studies report performance degradation on various downstream tasks due to information loss during discretization. To address this, we propose a novel approach combining temporally compressed discrete tokens with dimensionality-reduced continuous residuals. Our framework consists of a hybridized discrete-continuous focal modulation codec and a hybrid Transformer. This architecture performs autoregressive inference in the discrete domain, coupled with non-autoregressive prediction and continuous residual upsampling. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the retention of speaker characteristics compared to discrete-only methods, while simultaneously reducing the number of required autoregressive steps.

</details>


### [143] [CBD: API-Only LLM Black-Box Unlearning through Controlled Behavioral Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27683)
*Zhiqiang Xie,Yijing Lin,Zhipeng Gao,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CBD(Controlled Behavioral Divergence)的API黑盒机器遗忘框架，通过两个辅助模型在保留输入和遗忘目标输入之间创建可控行为差异，并将其转化为遗忘相关性分数，将遗忘相关提示路由远离目标LLM，实现在仅能API访问的情况下有效移除不需要数据的影响，同时保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备通过API调用大语言模型时，边缘生成的数据可能包含敏感、版权、有害或过时信息。机器遗忘能避免重新训练，但现有方法面临两个挑战：1) 仅能通过API黑盒访问，无法获取模型参数和内部logits；2) 当遗忘数据和保留数据具有高度相似的提示结构或语义模式时，难以保持保留效用。

Method: CBD框架使用两个辅助模型创建保留输入与遗忘目标输入之间的可控行为差异，将差异转化为遗忘相关性分数，据此将遗忘相关提示路由远离目标LLM。为应对高相似度下判别精度问题，通过估计经验Fisher矩阵并求解正则化广义特征值问题，构建基于梯度统计的判别基，引导遗忘信号聚焦于目标特定信息而非共享提示结构。

Result: 与11种白盒和灰盒遗忘基线相比，CBD实现了更优的遗忘-效用权衡且性能稳定。在ToFU forget10上，遗忘集表现接近重训练参考，模型效用达74.90（比第二优基线高约15%）。在WMDP上，将有害知识准确率降至25.68（接近随机猜测），同时MMLU准确率保持52.67。

Conclusion: CBD是首个仅需API黑盒访问即可实现有效机器遗忘的框架，通过可控行为差异和梯度统计判别基，解决了黑盒访问和高相似度数据两大挑战，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Edge devices increasingly invoke large language models (LLMs) through API services for context aware edge intelligence, while edge generated data may be collected to improve LLMs and may introduce sensitive, copyrighted, harmful, or outdated information into model behavior. Machine unlearning offers a practical way to remove the influence of undesired data without retraining LLMs. However, existing methods still face two gaps. The first is API only black box access, where target model parameters and internal logits are unavailable. The second is how to preserve retained utility when unlearning target data and retained data share highly similar prompt structures or semantic patterns. To address these challenges, we propose Controlled Behavioral Divergence (CBD), an API only black box unlearning framework. CBD uses two auxiliary models to create controlled behavioral divergence between retained inputs and unlearning target inputs, converts this divergence into an unlearning relevance score, and routes unlearning related prompts away from the target LLM. To improve discrimination accuracy under high similarity between target and retained data, CBD constructs a gradient statistics based discriminative basis by estimating empirical Fisher matrices and solving a regularized generalized eigenvalue problem, guiding the unlearning signal toward target specific information rather than shared prompt structures. Compared with eleven white box and gray box unlearning baselines, CBD achieves a better unlearning utility trade off and its performance varies little across settings. On ToFU forget10, CBD approaches the retrained reference on the forget set while raising model utility to 74.90, about 15% above the second best baseline. On WMDP, it lowers hazardous knowledge accuracy to 25.68, near random guessing, while preserving MMLU accuracy of 52.67. Code is at https://github.com/DGL-codes/CBD.

</details>


### [144] [Deployment-Side Adaptiveness in Multi-Horizon Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27688)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多步波动率预测中，相同训练好的多输出（MIMO）模型可通过不同推理时滚动规则产生不同精度的预测族，验证集选择策略能以低成本提升性能，但最优策略对度量标准和架构敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融预测中，预测性能不仅取决于模型训练方式，还取决于训练后模型的部署方式。现有研究对推理时部署策略的关注不足，尤其是多步波动率预测中MIMO模型的部署灵活性未被充分探索。

Method: 以训练好的MIMO预测器为基础，通过改变推理时滚动规则生成一系列不同精度和成本的预测族；在20个股指波动率序列、3个预测范围上，对线性模型到PatchTST等多种架构进行实验；使用验证集选择最佳部署策略，并对比MSE和QLIKE两种损失函数下的策略表现。

Result: 非默认滚动规则通常优于标准MIMO部署，但最佳固定规则因架构和预测范围而异；验证集选择的单例策略能以低成本提升性能；小规则子集能以较低推理成本获得大集成的大部分收益；MSE选择的策略在QLIKE损失下表现不佳。

Conclusion: 推理时部署是金融预测中一个重要的自适应来源；评估训练好的波动率预测器时，不仅应关注其架构，还应关注其部署策略。

Abstract: In financial forecasting, predictive performance depends not only on which model is trained, but also on how the trained model is deployed. We study this issue in multi-horizon volatility forecasting. Our starting point is that a trained multi-output (MIMO) forecaster does not define a single deployable predictor: by changing the inference-time rollout rule, the same trained model induces a family of forecasts with different accuracy and cost profiles. Across 20 stock-volatility series, three forecast horizons, and architectures ranging from linear models to PatchTST, we find that non-default rollout rules often improve over standard MIMO deployment. However, the best fixed rule varies substantially across architectures and horizons, making any single static replacement unreliable. We therefore evaluate validation-based deployment policies over the induced rule family. Under the primary MSE objective, validation-selected singletons provide a low-cost improvement over default MIMO, while small rule subsets recover much of the benefit of larger ensembles at substantially lower inference cost. We also find that policy rankings are metric-sensitive: MSE-selected policies do not transfer uniformly to QLIKE, a finance-standard volatility loss. These results show that inference-time deployment is a meaningful source of adaptiveness in financial forecasting, and that trained volatility forecasters should be evaluated not only by their architecture, but also by their deployment policy.

</details>


### [145] [Class-frequency Guided Noise Schedule for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27696)
*Jiequan Cui,Beier Zhu,Qingshan Xu,Xiaojuan Qi,Bei Yu,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了扩散模型中类别频率与多尺度噪声调度之间的相关性，发现低频类别因低密度区域更大导致分数估计不准确，而高频类别主导分数空间，为此提出了基于类别频率引导的噪声调度方法CFRG，在不平衡数据集上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中低密度区域会导致分数估计不准确，影响生成质量。尽管多尺度噪声调度可以缓解此问题，但低频类别因其更大的低密度区域而面临更严重的分数估计不准确问题；同时高频类别主导分数空间，导致生成样本向高频类别收敛，使得低频类别的生成样本质量和多样性较差。

Method: 提出Class-frequency Guided (CFRG)噪声调度，核心思想是为低频类别分配更大尺度的噪声，从而改善低频类别的生成质量。

Result: 在不平衡数据集CIFAR-100-LT和ImageNet-LT上，CFRG方法在图像生成、图像分类和文本到图像生成任务中均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 频率统计信息在噪声调度设计中具有关键作用，CFRG方法通过为低频类别分配更大噪声有效提升了扩散模型在不平衡数据上的生成性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we are the first to examine the correlations between class frequency and the multi-scale noise schedule within diffusion models. For score-based generative models, low-density regions often lead to inaccurately estimated scores, thereby compromising the generation quality. Although the multi-scale noise schedule can alleviate this issue during the diffusion process, low-frequency classes still face the challenge of large low-density regions, resulting in more inaccurate estimated scores than high-frequency classes. Furthermore, high-frequency classes tend to dominate the score space, causing a convergence of most data points towards generating samples from these classes. Consequently, samples generated within low-frequency classes exhibit suboptimal quality and limited diversity. To address this challenge, we propose the \textit{Class-frequency Guided (CFRG)} noise schedule, leveraging the insight that low-frequency classes should be endowed with larger-scale noises. To illustrate the effectiveness of our method, we conduct experiments on various tasks, including image generation, image classification, and text-to-image generation, using imbalanced datasets, \textit{i.e.}, CIFAR-100-LT, and ImageNet-LT. By employing the CFRG noise schedule, we achieve substantial improvements over baselines, manifesting the crucial role of frequency statistics in noise schedule design.

</details>


### [146] [RANSAC Scoring Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27385)
*James Pritts,Felix Seegräber,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需内点尺度参数的RANSAC评分函数，通过共轭逆伽马先验解析边缘化内点尺度，在近7万图像对基准测试中超越现有方法，在阈值误校准、少量验证数据等场景下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RANSAC变体需要用户提供依赖于内点尺度的阈值参数，而该尺度本身需从污染数据中估计，这形成了一个循环依赖问题。本文旨在消除这一参数依赖。

Method: 逆转传统推断顺序：先为固定内点划分设定共轭逆伽马先验，解析闭式边缘化内点尺度，再优化划分。评分函数涵盖非信息性Jeffreys极限和经验贝叶斯先验，通过排序-扫描算法实现O(N log N)计算。

Result: 在近7万图像对上，新评分在阈值误校准下保持平坦性能（基线方法显著下降），仅需2个验证对即可达到近最优精度（基线方法需约百倍数据），且数据稀缺时先验正则化自动收紧。

Conclusion: 首次实现真正意义上无内点尺度参数的RANSAC评分，在不同数据丰裕度下自适应，具有更强的鲁棒性和数据效率。

Abstract: The most widely used RANSAC variants score candidate models by counting inliers or summing per-point scores that saturate beyond a residual threshold. Every such score requires a user-supplied parameter that is a function of the inlier scale, which must itself be estimated from contaminated data. We remove this dependence by reversing the usual order of inference: rather than estimating the scale and then scoring against it, we marginalize the inlier scale analytically in closed form under a conjugate Inverse-Gamma prior for a fixed inlier partition, then optimize over partitions. A single closed-form expression spans the non-informative Jeffreys limit and informative empirical-Bayes priors, so the same score adapts across data-rich and data-scarce regimes without any change to the algorithm. The proposed RANSAC score is the first in which the inlier scale is genuinely absent from the formula. The score admits O(N log N ) computation via sort-and-sweep. On a benchmark of nearly 70 000 image pairs spanning different two-view estimation problems and both engineered and learned feature pipelines, the proposed score exceeds the state of the art (RANSAC, MSAC, GaU, MAGSAC): it stays nearly flat under threshold miscalibration where baselines degrade, reaches near-optimal accuracy from as few as two validation pairs where baselines need ont he order of 100 times more,. and tightens its prior regularization as validation data grows scarce.

</details>


### [147] [Unified Zero-Shot Time Series Forecasting: A Darts Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27438)
*Zhihao Dai,Dennis Bader,Alain Gysi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Darts库从2020年发布以来已成为广泛使用的时间序列分析工具。针对近期涌现的多种基础模型（如Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5、TiRex、PatchTST-FM）存在接口碎片化、互操作性差的问题，Darts开发了统一的FoundationModel类集合，提供标准化、全周期的预测接口，使现有Darts管道仅需更改模型名称即可使用基础模型进行零样本或微调预测、不确定性估计和回测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在零样本预测方面展现出精度优势，但这些模型通常作为独立包发布，接口碎片化、与常用工具互操作性差，难以在完整管道中进行联合评估和集成。

Method: 开发了统一的FoundationModel类集合，为Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5、TiRex、PatchTST-FM等基础模型提供标准化、全周期的预测接口，最小化外部依赖，将其集成到Darts生态系统中。

Result: 现有Darts管道仅需更改模型名称即可使用基础模型；新管道可进行零样本或微调预测、不确定性估计和回测，结合数据处理和评估工具，均在统一框架内完成。

Conclusion: 统一的基础模型接口降低了使用门槛，促进了从训练自定义模型向利用预训练通用预测器的范式转变，同时保持了与现有工具链的兼容性。

Abstract: Since its initial release in 2020, Darts has become a widely used open-source Python library for time series analysis. A series of foundation models have recently claimed accuracy improvements in zero-shot forecasting, promising a paradigm shift from training custom models to harnessing pre-trained general-purpose forecasters. Foundation models, however, are often released as isolated packages with fragmented interfaces and limited interoperability with common tooling, making joint evaluation and integration within complete pipelines difficult. In Darts, we developed a unified $\texttt{FoundationModel}$ class collection (Chronos-2, TimesFM 2.5, TiRex, PatchTST-FM) that provides standardized, full-cycle forecasting interfaces with minimal external dependencies for integrating foundation models into the ecosystem. Existing Darts pipelines can now use foundation models with only a name change; new pipelines can use them for zero-shot or fine-tuned forecasting, uncertainty estimation, and backtesting, combined with data processing and evaluation tooling, all within a unified framework.

</details>


### [148] [The Simulacrum: Decision-Theoretic Pretraining for Near-Optimal Time-Series Forecasting and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27711)
*Pablo Montero-Manso,Marcel Scharth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为"决策理论预训练"的神经网络框架，通过生成式世界和决策目标的联合建模，训练神经网络逼近最优决策规则，从而在未见时间序列上实现零样本推理。实验表明，该方法在有限样本偏差校正、预测组合难题以及真实世界基准预测中优于传统统计方法和大型预训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决时间序列估计中分析上难以处理或计算上过于复杂的难题，如有限样本偏差、校准不足和预测组合难题。传统方法（如MLE、AICc）在这些问题上表现有限，且缺乏灵活控制决策理论权衡的能力。

Method: 构建一个生成式世界（数据生成过程的分布）和目标决策目标，在分层模拟数据上训练神经网络，使其逼近最优决策规则。该神经估计器可直接近似过程级的有限样本性质（如风险、偏差、 minimax 性能和均匀校准）。

Result: 神经估计器在相同结构模型类上优于MLE和AICc等传统基线；在仅使用结构模型模拟数据训练的情况下，在多个真实世界预测基准上达到与统计模型、神经网络或大型预训练模型相当甚至更优的预测精度；成功解决了AR(p)模型的有限样本偏差与校准问题以及预测组合难题。

Conclusion: 该框架通过显式控制决策理论权衡，为分析人员提供高度定制化的高效估计工具，能够近似求解传统方法难以处理的复杂时间序列问题，包括结构方程和最优性准则。

Abstract: We introduce a neural network-based framework for learning time series estimators through a process we term decision-theoretic pretraining. Analysts specify a generative world, a distribution over data-generating processes, and a target decision objective. A neural network trained on stratified simulations from this world approximates the corresponding optimal decision rule, yielding a neural estimator that provides forecasts, parameter estimates, predictive intervals, or model-selection for zero-shot inference on previously unseen time series.
  The joint specification of the generative world and objective enables the estimators to directly approximate process-level, finite-sample properties: near-optimal risk, bias control, minimax performance, and uniform calibration. Our experiments demonstrate that these neural estimators can outperform traditional baselines such as maximum likelihood estimation and model selection via AICc, for the same model structural model classes. Furthermore, even when trained purely on simulations of structural models, they achieve competitive or state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy on major real-world benchmarks, compared with statistical, neural or large pre-trained models.
  We illustrate the framework by addressing two longstanding challenges: finite-sample bias and miscalibration in AR(p) models, and the forecast combination puzzle. These applications highlight the approach's main advantage: its ability to approximate solutions to analytically intractable or computationally prohibitive time series problems, including complex structural equations or optimality criteria. Ultimately, by enabling explicit control over decision-theoretic trade-offs, the framework equips analysts with highly efficient estimation tools tailored to their specific analytical needs.

</details>


### [149] [PairSAE: Mechanistic Interpretability from Pair Representations in Protein Co-Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27440)
*Giosue Migliorini,Aristofanis Rontogiannis,Grigori Guitchounts,Nicholas Franklin,Axel Elaldi,Olivia Viessmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairSAE通过N-mode SVD压缩成对张量，结合稀疏自编码器学习共享的token级特征，实现对结构生物学基础模型（如Boltz-2）可解释性的提升，避免传统SAE在pairformer架构中的二次爆炸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构生物学基础模型（如pairformer架构）的内部特征难以解释，标准稀疏自编码器（SAE）在处理成对表示时存在特征二次爆炸、难以捕捉序列与成对表示间分布式概念的问题。

Method: PairSAE首先通过N-mode SVD将成对张量压缩为token级交互角色，再使用稀疏自编码器学习一组共享的token级特征，这些特征可同时解码为序列表示和成对表示。

Result: 在Boltz-2对PLINDER蛋白-配体复合物的激活上，PairSAE学到了可解释的特征，这些特征与UniProt注释对齐，并能预测Boltz-2的亲和力值。

Conclusion: PairSAE成功将结构生物学基础模型的潜在空间与可解释的结构概念联系起来，阐明了模型“知道”什么，同时避免了传统SAE在pairformer架构中的局限。

Abstract: Foundation models for structural biology have achieved remarkable performance in predicting biomolecular structure and show promise for the design of proteins and small molecules. Yet understanding which internal features drive their outputs remains challenging. Standard sparse autoencoders (SAEs), effective on transformer-style sequence embeddings, do not transfer cleanly to pairformer-like architectures: naively operating on pairwise representations yields a quadratic blow-up of features and obscures concepts distributed jointly across sequence and pair representations.
  We introduce PairSAE, which summarizes pairwise tensors via an N-mode SVD into token-wise interaction roles, then uses a sparse autoencoder to learn a shared set of token-level features that decode into both sequence and pair representations. Evaluated on Boltz-2 activations for PLINDER protein-ligand complexes, PairSAE yields interpretable features that align with UniProt annotations and predict Boltz-2 affinity values. These results indicate that PairSAE links the latent space of foundation models for structural biology to interpretable structural concepts, clarifying what the model "knows" while avoiding pairformer-induced pitfalls that limit conventional SAEs.

</details>


### [150] [Flexformer: Flexible Linear Transformer with Learnable Attention Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27748)
*Haoran Zhang,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flexformer提出了一种基于可学习随机傅里叶特征的线性注意力机制，通过将频谱频率作为可训练参数，实现全数据驱动的注意力核学习，在保持线性复杂度的同时提升了表达能力，并在语言建模和序列分类任务上取得优于基线模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的注意力机制具有二次复杂度，难以扩展到长序列；而现有的基于核的线性注意力方法通常使用固定或弱可学习的核函数，限制了模型的表达能力和性能。因此，作者希望设计一种完全数据驱动的、可学习的线性注意力核，以兼顾效率与表现力。

Method: Flexformer基于随机傅里叶特征（RFF）的线性注意力框架，将频谱频率（spectral frequencies）作为可训练参数，使得模型能够以数据驱动的方式学习广泛的注意力核函数。同时，作者分别提出了平稳（stationary）和非平稳（nonstationary）两种变体，其中非平稳变体具有更强的表达能力。

Result: 在语言建模和序列分类任务上的实验表明，Flexformer一致性地优于基线模型。此外，Flexformer可以从预训练的Transformer中有效地蒸馏，恢复softmax注意力的性能，并展现出跨领域的核迁移能力，在长序列任务上实现了高效率与有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: Flexformer通过可学习的核函数设计，成功兼顾了线性注意力的效率与softmax注意力的表现力，具备从预训练模型蒸馏和跨域迁移的能力，为长序列建模提供了一种高效且灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformer models rely on attention mechanism to capture long-range dependencies but suffer from quadratic complexity, limiting their scalability to long sequences. Kernel-based linear attention reduces this complexity but typically relies on fixed or weakly learnable kernels, restricting expressiveness and performance. In this work, we propose Flexformer, a flexible linear Transformer that learns attention kernels in a fully data-driven manner. Flexformer builds on random Fourier feature-based linear attention and treats spectral frequencies as trainable parameters, enabling the model to learn a broad family of attention kernels.
  We develop both stationary and nonstationary variants, with the latter offering strictly greater expressiveness.
  Extensive experiments on language modeling and sequence classification demonstrate that Flexformer consistently outperforms baselines. Moreover, Flexformer can be effectively distilled from pretrained Transformers to recover softmax attention and exhibits strong kernel transferability across domains, achieving both high efficiency and competitive performance on long-sequence tasks.

</details>


### [151] [Learning in Markovian bandits with non-observable states and constrained decision epochs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27448)
*Thomas Hira,Victor Boone,Urtzi Ayesta,Ina Maria Verloop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有非可观状态和约束决策时点的马尔可夫强盗问题，提出自降解马尔可夫强盗模型，并设计了UCB-NOM算法，在无需状态数依赖的情况下实现近乎对数级的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有马尔可夫强盗研究多假设状态可观测或决策时点灵活，但在许多实际场景中状态不可观测且决策受约束，因此需要研究这类更具挑战性的设定下的遗憾最小化问题。

Method: 提出自降解马尔可夫强盗模型，证明纯策略渐近最优性；设计基于UCB思想的乐观算法UCB-NOM；通过偏置函数界这一先验知识实现O(log T)遗憾；理论和实验验证算法性能。

Result: 无先验知识时，稀疏切换算法的遗憾必然为ω(log T)；UCB-NOM在无先验知识时实现近乎对数遗憾，有先验知识时达到O(log T)遗憾和O(√(T log T))最坏情况遗憾界，且不依赖于状态数。

Conclusion: 状态非可观性在自降解马尔可夫强盗中仅造成轻微不便，对数遗憾虽不可普遍达到但可通过优化算法逼近；先验知识可恢复对数遗憾界。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of regret minimization in Markovian bandits with \emph{non-observable states} and possibly \emph{constrained} decision epochs. The focus is restricted to a ``pure'' regret benchmark, that compares the performance of the learning algorithm to the best \emph{pure policy} which -- akin to optimal policies of stochastic bandits -- picks the optimal arm from start to finish without ever switching. We introduce a generalization of rested Markovian bandits, \emph{self-degrading Markovian bandits}, for which pure policies are always asymptotically optimal.We show that without prior knowledge on the underlying bandit, the regret of algorithms that switch arms rarely necessarily scales super-logarithmically for every bandit, i.e., as $ω(\log(T))$, where $T$ is the learning horizon. Despite the unreachability of the logarithmic regime, we design UCB-NOM, an optimistic algorithm inspired by UCB, of which the regret is nearly logarithmic. Lastly, we show that given prior knowledge on the Markovian bandit in the form of a bound on the bias functions of its arm, a proper instantiation of UCB-NOM achieves $O(\log(T))$ regret. We further show that this prior knowledge allows for a $O(\sqrt{T \log(T)})$ worst-case regret bound for UCB-NOM. Notably, our regret bounds do not depend on the number of states of the underlying Markov chains. Our findings suggest that the non-observability of states is a mild inconvenience in self-degrading Markovian bandits.

</details>


### [152] [RS-Diffuser: Risk-Sensitive Diffusion Planning with Distributional Value Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27766)
*Shiqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RS-Diffuser提出了一种风险敏感的离线扩散规划框架，通过结合扩散轨迹生成与分布式价值评论家，利用条件风险价值等尾部感知目标指导去噪过程，实现单模型灵活调控风险偏好，在风险敏感D4RL和机器人导航任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散规划器通常是风险中性的，可能忽视实际部署中罕见但灾难性的后果，无法满足安全关键应用对风险敏感决策的需求。

Method: 1) 学习扩散规划器生成未来状态轨迹；2) 构建独立的逆动力学模型进行动作解码；3) 通过分位数回归训练蒙特卡洛分布式评论家，估计完整回报分布；4) 采样时利用条件风险价值等尾部感知目标计算梯度，作为风险敏感引导信号注入去噪过程。

Result: 在风险敏感D4RL和机器人导航基准上，RS-Diffuser在总体回报、最差情况鲁棒性和安全性违规减少方面均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 单模型通过仅改变推理时风险参数即可灵活生成风险规避、风险中性或风险寻求行为，验证了风险敏感扩散规划框架在安全关键离线强化学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning enables policy learning from fixed datasets without additional environment interaction, making it appealing for safety-critical applications where online exploration is costly or unsafe. Diffusion-based decision-making methods have recently achieved strong performance in offline RL by modeling rich, multimodal trajectory distributions. However, existing diffusion planners are typically risk-neutral and therefore may overlook rare but catastrophic outcomes that are crucial in real-world deployment. In this work, we propose RS-Diffuser, a risk-sensitive offline diffusion planning framework that combines diffusion-based trajectory generation with distributional value critics. RS-Diffuser learns a diffusion planner over future state trajectories, a separate inverse dynamics model for action decoding, and a Monte Carlo distributional critic that estimates the full return distribution of candidate plans through quantile regression. At sampling time, we incorporate a risk-sensitive guidance signal into the denoising process, using gradients computed from tail-aware objectives such as Conditional Value at Risk to steer generation toward desired risk profiles. As a result, a single trained model can flexibly produce risk-averse, risk-neutral, or risk-seeking behaviors by changing only the inference-time risk parameter. Extensive experiments on risk-sensitive D4RL and risky robot navigation benchmarks demonstrate that RS-Diffuser achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving both overall return and worst-case robustness while reducing safety violations.

</details>


### [153] [Prism Transformer: Progressive Head Schedules for Hierarchical Attention Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27449)
*Shubham Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prism Transformer通过在各层单调增加注意力头数量（早期层用少而宽的头部，深层用多而窄的头部）替代传统均匀分配方案，在不增加参数和计算量的前提下，建立了局部到全局的表示层级，在多个模型规模上持续优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多头注意力每层均匀分配隐藏维度（dh = dmodel/h），导致早期层头部维度受限，无法忠实捕获复杂的高维上下文模式。这种均匀分配被识别为一种结构性瓶颈。

Method: 提出Prism Transformer，用渐进式头部调度替代静态均匀头部配置：在各层单调增加头部数量，早期层用少量宽头捕获局部组合模式，深层用大量窄头分解为专门化的语言特征。该方法参数中性、计算中性，零训练/推理开销。

Result: 在124M、354M和757M三个模型规模上，Prism Transformer一致优于均匀基线，验证损失持续降低，在PIQA、HellaSwag、ARC-Easy、WinoGrande等零样本基准上持续提升。

Conclusion: 非均匀的子空间分配能够释放标准Transformer预算内的潜在容量，更有效地利用模型容量。

Abstract: Multi-head attention conventionally partitions the hidden dimension equally across all heads at every layer, enforcing an identical representational subspace dimension (dh = dmodel/h) throughout the models depth. In this work, we identify this uniform allocation as a fundamental structural bottleneck: due to their restricted dimensional space, early-layer heads are unable to faithfully capture complex, high-dimensional contextual patterns. To resolve this, we introduce the Prism Transformer, a novel architectural paradigm that replaces the static, uniform head configuration with a progressive head schedule. By monotonically increasing the head count across layers, the Prism Transformer naturally establishes a local-to-global representational hierarchy: early layers leverage fewer, exceptionally wide heads to capture complex, local compositional patterns, while deep layers deploy many, narrow heads to decompose these patterns into specialized linguistic features. Crucially, this structural shift is parameter-neutral, compute-neutral, and introduces zero training or inference overhead, preserving identical weight matrices and FLOP budgets as the standard Transformer. Across three model scales (124M, 354M, and 757M), the Prism Transformer consistently outperforms uniform baselines, achieving consistent reductions in validation loss alongside consistent gains on downstream zero-shot benchmarks (including PIQA, HellaSwag, ARC-Easy, and WinoGrande). Our findings demonstrate that non-uniform subspace allocation unlocks latent capacity within the standard Transformer budget, enabling more effective use of model capacity.

</details>


### [154] [Operator Learning for Cubic Nonlinear Schrödinger Equation on Periodic Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27459)
*Emmanuel E. Oguadimma,Victory C. Obieke,Xueying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何条件傅里叶神经算子（FNO），用于学习不同纵横比二维环面上三次非线性薛定谔方程的解算子，能够捕捉有理数与无理数几何下不同的Sobolev范数增长行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维环面的纵横比决定了傅里叶共振结构，有理数与无理数几何表现出不同的高频级联行为，现有数值方法难以同时捕捉这两种几何下的动力学差异。

Method: 以解的实部、虚部及纵横比参数ω²作为输入，构建几何条件傅里叶神经算子，训练逼近单步解算子，采用傅里叶伪谱法生成随机相位初始数据的轨迹进行测试。

Result: 学习到的算子能够再现两种几何下不同的Sobolev范数行为：有理环面上H²增长更强，无理环面上更受约束。消融实验表明，显式加入ω²可提升长时预测精度，尤其对有理几何效果显著。

Conclusion: 几何感知的神经算子能够有效学习非线性色散偏微分方程中的谱转移现象，加入纵横比参数可提升模型泛化能力。

Abstract: We consider the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger (NLS) equation on two-dimensional flat tori with varying aspect ratios. In this formulation, the choice of aspect ratio governs the Fourier resonance structure, so rational and irrational geometries can exhibit different high-frequency cascade behaviors. We present a geometry-conditioned Fourier neural operator (FNO) for the cubic defocusing NLS equation, where the input consists of the real and imaginary parts of the solution together with the aspect-ratio parameter \(ω^2\). The model is trained to approximate the one-step solution operator and is evaluated on unseen trajectories generated from random-phase initial data using Fourier pseudospectral method. Our numerical experiments show that the learned operator captures the main solution dynamics on both tori and reproduces the distinct Sobolev norm behavior of the two geometries, with stronger \(H^2\)-growth on the rational torus and more constrained behavior on the irrational torus, consistent with the findings of \cite{hrabski2021energy}. We perform ablation studies to examine the roles of retained Fourier modes, activation functions, Fourier-layer depth, and explicit geometry conditioning. The results indicate that including $ω^2$ improves long-time predictive accuracy, especially for the rational geometry, and supports the use of geometry-aware neural operators for learning spectral-transfer phenomena in nonlinear dispersive partial differential equations.

</details>


### [155] [Pepti-drift: Toxicity-Repulsive Drifting for Antigen-Conditioned Discrete Peptide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27824)
*Takashi Fujiwara,Hikaru Shindo,Kaushalya Madhawa,Jun Jin Choong,Keisuke Ozawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pepti-drift是一个毒性感知的潜在空间精炼框架，通过单步抗原条件漂移生成肽候选物，在吸引抗原结合肽的同时排斥毒性相关区域，并采用预热策略稳定吸引与排斥的竞争目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计抗原特异性结合肽同时避免毒性是治疗性肽发现的主要挑战，因为促进结合的物理化学特征与毒性相关特征在表征空间中常重叠。

Method: 提出Pepti-drift框架，在肽嵌入空间中通过单步抗原条件漂移，学习吸引生成肽潜在向量趋向抗原匹配的结合肽，同时远离毒性区域，并采用预热策略先学习结合导向吸引，再增加毒性排斥以稳定竞争目标。

Result: 实现了抗原特异性结合肽的生成同时考虑了毒性规避，有效处理了结合促进特征与毒性特征重叠的难题。

Conclusion: Pepti-drift通过潜在空间精炼和预热策略，在治疗性肽发现中实现了结合特异性和毒性规避的平衡。

Abstract: Peptides are a promising therapeutic modality that combine the chemical tunability of small molecules with the target specificity of macromolecular therapeutics. However, designing antigen-specific binding peptides while avoiding toxicity remains a major challenge for therapeutic peptide discovery. Here, we present Pepti-drift, a toxicity-aware latent refinement framework that generates peptide candidates through a single antigen-conditioned drift step. In a peptide embedding space, Pepti-drift learns to attract generated peptide latents toward antigen-matched binding peptides while repelling them from toxicity-associated regions. This is challenging because binding-promoting physicochemical features often overlap with toxicity-associated features in peptide representation space. To address this, we introduce a warm-up strategy to stabilize this competing objective by first learning binding-oriented attraction and then increasing toxicity repulsion.

</details>


### [156] [The Curse of Multiple Mediators: Hidden Interaction Effects in Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27510)
*Sankaran Vaidyanathan,David Arbour,Aaron Mueller,Scott Niekum,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新推导了激活修补（Activation Patching）的因果估计量，发现其自然间接效应（NIE）不仅捕获了特定组件的因果效应，还包含交互效应（INT）。INT无法通过调整估计量或分析单位消除，且其存在会导致因果重要性估计偏差和忠实度分数不稳定。论文证明INT随激活距离缩放，在局部仿射模型中可忽略，并具有组合分解结构。INT应被视为诊断工具而非噪声，其大小和符号可提示因果结论对提示的依赖性以及贪婪NIE排序的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活修补方法在归因因果责任时忽略了组件间的交互效应（INT），导致对模型行为的因果理解不准确。需要重新审视NIE估计量的性质，理解INT的来源、性质及其对可解释性研究的影响。

Method: 从因果中介分析出发重新推导激活修补的NIE估计量，识别出其包含的交互效应分量；通过GPT-2 IOI电路实验展示INT导致的失效模式；理论分析INT的缩放性质、局部仿射条件下的消失条件以及组合分解结构。

Result: INT是NIE的固有组成部分，无法通过调整估计量或分析单位消除；在GPT-2 IOI电路中，因果重要性条件于其他组件状态的组件要么不可见要么被人为放大；INT方差解释了忠实度分数的不稳定性；INT随干净激活与修补激活之间的距离缩放，在局部仿射模型中可忽略，可分解为成对及高阶组交互。

Conclusion: INT不是需要消除的干扰项，而是可解释性研究的有用诊断工具：其大小和符号可指示因果结论对提示的依赖性，并揭示基于贪婪NIE的组件排序无法发现的、需要通过组合搜索才能发现的机制。

Abstract: Activation patching is the primary tool in mechanistic interpretability. It attributes causal responsibility for a model behavior to each of its individual components by estimating its natural indirect effect (NIE). Re-deriving the activation patching estimand from causal mediation analysis, we find that the NIE does not solely capture the causal effect through the specific component. It also contains interaction effects (INT) that measure how much the component's causal effect itself depends on the state of other components in the model. A natural response may be to try to eliminate INT by adjusting the estimator or unit of analysis, but each of these potential remedies has predictable failure modes. We demonstrate these failure modes in the GPT-2 IOI circuit; components whose causal importance is conditional on the state of other components are either invisible or artificially inflated, and INT variance explains the previously documented instability of faithfulness scores. We prove that INT scales with the distance between clean and patched component activations, is negligible when the model is locally affine, and decomposes combinatorially into pairwise and higher-order group interactions. Despite its inevitability, INT is not a nuisance to be eliminated, but rather a diagnostic for interpretability studies. Its individual and group-level magnitude and sign signal when causal conclusions are prompt-dependent, and when greedy NIE-based component ranking will miss mechanisms only discoverable through combinatorial search.

</details>


### [157] [Boundary condition fidelity for bottom-hole pressure and CO2 plume prediction in geological carbon storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27515)
*Romal Ramadhan,Seyyed A. Hosseini,Larry W. Lake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对比十种边界处理方法，发现保留角点孔隙体积是截断域模拟的关键，其中结合传导率修正的渐变修饰方法在均质和非均质储层中均表现最佳（BHP NRMSE低于3.7%，羽流IoU高于0.97）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地质碳封存中，底部压力（BHP）和CO2羽流迁移的准确预测至关重要，但实际模拟常使用截断域，人工边界会扭曲压力扩散和CO2饱和度分布，因此需要评估不同边界处理方法的保真度。

Method: 在均质和非均质储层中，比较十种截断域边界处理方法（包括均匀孔隙体积乘子、传导率修正、角点修正、分层修正、渐变修饰等）与全域参考模拟的差异，以BHP RMSE、NRMSE、峰值压力偏差和羽流交并比（IoU）为评价指标。

Result: 均匀处理方法忽略角点存储导致较大压力误差（BHP RMSE 250-382 psi）和羽流IoU约0.80-0.84；角点修正场景将压力误差大幅降低，羽流IoU提升至0.94以上；传导率修正并非普遍有益——均质储层中改善压力保真度，非均质储层中可能过度限制流动；渐变修饰+传导率修正表现最稳定，BHP NRMSE低于3.7%，羽流IoU高于0.97。

Conclusion: 保留角点孔隙体积是截断域建模的最重要条件，渐变修饰结合传导率修正提供了最一致且准确的边界处理方法。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of bottom-hole pressure (BHP) and CO2 plume migration is essential for safe geological carbon storage, yet practical simulations often rely on truncated domains where artificial boundaries distort pressure diffusion and CO2 saturation footprints. In this study, we evaluate how boundary-condition fidelity affects BHP and CO2 plume prediction by comparing ten reduced-domain boundary treatments against full-domain reference simulations in homogeneous and heterogeneous reservoirs. We test uniform pore-volume multipliers, transmissibility modifiers, corner-adjusted pore-volume corrections, layered corrections, and gradual modifiers using BHP RMSE, NRMSE, peak pressure deviation, and plume Intersection over Union (IoU) as performance metrics. Our results show that conserving corner pore volume is the most important requirement for truncated-domain modeling. We find that uniform treatments which neglect corner storage generate large pressure errors, with BHP RMSE of 362 to 382 psi in the homogeneous model and 250 to 304 psi in the heterogeneous model, and yield plume IoU values near 0.80 to 0.84, indicating roughly 16 to 20% of the combined plume area is misrepresented. Corner-adjusted scenarios substantially reduce pressure errors and raise plume IoU above 0.94, but we observe that transmissibility correction is not universally beneficial. In homogeneous reservoirs, uniform transmissibility adjustment improves pressure fidelity; in heterogeneous reservoirs, it can over-restrict flow across variable-permeability boundary faces, increasing BHP error and contracting the predicted plume. We find the gradual modifier with transmissibility correction provides the most consistent performance, achieving BHP NRMSE below 3.7% and plume IoU above 0.97 in both reservoir types.

</details>


### [158] [WattLayer: Get Layers Right to Estimate Inference Energy of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27841)
*Adrien Sardi,Marie-Line Alberi Morel,Sara Alouf,Frédéric Giroire,Joanna Moulierac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与任务无关、逐层分解的AI推理能耗估算模型，在超10万层、295种神经网络架构、3种任务和3种硬件平台上验证，中位误差19.6%，优于现有方法，且可泛化到新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI广泛应用导致能耗问题日益突出，但目前缺乏标准化的方法论来准确估算AI推理能耗，尤其是跨不同任务和架构的能耗。

Method: 提出一种与任务无关的逐层能量估算模型，通过将神经网络按层分解，独立估算每层的能耗，从而支持跨架构和跨任务的泛化。

Result: 在包含超过10万层、295种架构、3种任务和3种硬件平台的大规模数据集上，该方法中位误差为19.6%，优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 逐层分解方法可泛化到新任务而无需完全重新训练，为设计节能AI系统提供了工具、洞察和精确方法论。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Artificial Intelligence (AI) has led to increasing concerns about energy consumption, yet there is a lack of standardized methodologies to accurately estimate AI inference energy consumption, particularly across various tasks and architectures. In this study, we propose a task independent, layer-wise energy estimation model for AI architectures. Our model is evaluated on a large dataset of more than 100,000 layers for 295 neural network architectures across 3 widely-used tasks and 3 distinct hardware platforms. Our approach achieves a median error of 19.6%, outperforming state-of-the-art methods. We further show that layer-wise decomposition generalize to new tasks without complete retraining, by leveraging shared layers across architectures. It offer tools, insights and a precise methodology to empower stakeholders in designing energy-efficient AI systems.

</details>


### [159] [Applicability of memorization indicators for early spotting of overfitting while recalibrating sEMG-decoders on low sample sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27855)
*Stephan J. Lehmler,Tobias Glasmachers,Ioannis Iossifidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于激活统计信息的记忆指标，用于在低样本sEMG校准场景中早期检测过拟合，无需额外验证集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在sEMG解码中受益于个性化校准，但校准数据量极其有限，容易过拟合，而传统早停法需要额外验证集，在低样本场景中难以应用。

Method: 利用ReLU激活层的激活统计信息作为记忆指标，在基准sEMG数据集上进行迁移学习实验：先多受试者预训练CNN，再对个体用户用少量重复次数进行微调，监测最后一隐藏层的激活行为与解码性能。

Result: 测试精度下降伴随激活率的特征性变化，验证了激活记忆指标在低样本sEMG校准中早期检测学习失败的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于ReLU激活统计的记忆指标无需额外验证集即可有效检测低样本校准中的过拟合，是一种有前景的工具。

Abstract: Deep learning models for surface electromyography (sEMG) can benefit substantially from subject-specific (re-)calibration, since no sufficiently large and diverse datasets are available to train fully generic decoders. However, for user acceptance, the number of repetitions that can realistically be collected during calibration is severely limited, which increases the risk of overfitting and, in extreme cases, can even degrade performance compared to the uncalibrated model. Classical overfitting indicators such as validation performance and regularization with early stopping are difficult to apply in this low-sample regime, as they require additional held-out data that is rarely available in practical calibration scenarios. In this work, we investigate a recently proposed class of memorization indicators based solely on the activation statistics of rectified linear units (ReLU) in deep neural networks, which can be computed directly from training data without any extra validation set. We conduct a transferlearning experiment on a benchmark sEMG dataset, where a convolutional neural network is first pre-trained on multiple subjects and subsequently fine-tuned on individual users using only a small number of repetitions. During calibration, we monitor both decoding performance and the activation behaviour of the last hidden layer. Our results provide first evidence that decreases in test accuracy during fine-tuning are ac companied by characteristic changes in activation rates, indicating that activation-based memorization indicators are a promising tool for early spotting of unsuccessful learning in low-sample sEMG calibration settings.

</details>


### [160] [Quantum Generative Diffusion Model for Real-World Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27561)
*Jack Waller,Filippo Caruso,Dimitrios Makris,Rajagopal Nilavalan,Xing Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个量子生成扩散模型QDiffusion-TS用于时间序列合成，在IQM量子处理器上验证。通过用量子神经网络替换去噪Transformer中的前馈组件，大幅减少参数（约三个数量级），在金融时间序列上Wasserstein距离降低44%，下游预测任务RMSE提升高达71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典生成模型随规模扩大面临计算成本与效率挑战，量子机器学习有望以更紧凑、高表达力的模型表示复杂数据分布，从而提升效率与可扩展性。

Method: 提出QDiffusion-TS混合量子框架：保留经典扩散架构，将去噪Transformer中的前馈神经网络组件替换为量子神经网络，形成混合量子Transformer，大幅减少可训练参数。

Result: 在Apple和Amazon金融时间序列上：生成数据Wasserstein距离较经典模型降低约44%；下游预测任务中，数据增强使RMSE较仅用真实数据训练的基线提升高达71%。

Conclusion: 量子增强架构能以显著更少的参数一致达到甚至超越经典模型性能，为高效、可扩展的数据驱动生成建模提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Generative models have achieved remarkable success in data synthesis, though recent advances driven by increasing model scale have introduced challenges in computational cost and efficiency. Quantum machine learning offers a promising alternative, representing complex data distributions using compact, highly expressive models. Here, we propose QDiffusion-TS, the first quantum generative diffusion model for time series synthesis, and validate it on the IQM quantum processor. The framework extends a classical diffusion architecture by replacing feed-forward components within the denoising transformer with quantum neural networks, yielding a hybrid quantum transformer that reduces the number of trainable parameters in each replaced component by nearly three orders of magnitude. Evaluated on financial time series from Apple and Amazon, the model generates synthetic data that more accurately reproduces the real distributions, reducing Wasserstein distance by approximately 44% relative to its classical counterpart across both datasets. In a downstream forecasting task, augmentation with the generated data improves predictive performance by up to 71% in RMSE over a baseline trained solely on real data. These results show that quantum enhanced architectures can consistently match and frequently surpass classical performance with substantially fewer parameters, establishing a practical framework towards more efficient and scalable data-driven generative modelling.

</details>


### [161] [Benchmarking on Tasks That Matter: Dataset Selection for Preserving Model Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27997)
*Rostislav Gusev,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，用于从机器学习基准测试中选择代表性数据子集，并评估不同选择策略对全局模型排名保持的效果。实验表明，在时间序列分类（112个数据集）和NLP基准（57个任务）中，多种策略优于随机选择；而在推荐系统（30个数据集）中改进不显著。最优策略仅用5个数据集即可达到0.95的Spearman相关系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基准测试涉及大量数据集，评估成本高昂。现有子集选择多依赖启发式方法，缺乏对模型排名鲁棒性的系统分析。

Method: 提出框架包含bootstrap聚合以提供有效的置信区间。考虑聚类、设计准则（A/D-最优性）、随机基线和贪心最远优先（FAFI）等策略，并推导FAFI在排名误差方面的选择质量上界。

Result: 在TSC（112数据集）和NLP（57任务）上，多种选择策略优于随机；推荐系统（30数据集）上改进不显著且统计上不显著。TSC中最优策略仅用5个数据集达到Spearman相关系数0.95。选择策略有效性依赖于数据集表示质量和基准测试规模。

Conclusion: 数据集子集选择能有效降低评估成本同时保持模型排名一致性，但效果因领域和基准规模而异，且依赖于数据集表示质量。

Abstract: Benchmarks of machine learning models often include many datasets, making evaluation expensive. For efficiency, it is preferable to perform evaluations on small, representative datasets instead. The selection of such subsets typically relies on heuristics and is rarely analyzed for the robustness of the resulting model rankings.
  We introduce a framework to perform the task of selecting datasets subsets with an evaluation of how different selection strategies preserve the global model rankings. Our framework includes bootstrap aggregation, which provides valid confidence intervals, allowing a principled comparison of selection strategies. We consider clustering, design criteria (A/D-optimality), random baselines, and greedy farthest-first (FAFI). For the latter, we derive upper bounds on selection quality in terms of ranking errors as a function of the number of selected datasets.
  Empirically, in time series classification (TSC, 112 datasets) and in a supplementary natural language processing benchmark derived from MTEB (57 tasks), several selection strategies improve rank preservation compared with random subsets, including simple FAFI. In contrast, in recommender systems (30 datasets), the improvement of strategies over random selection is small and typically statistically insignificant. For TSC, our best-performing strategy achieves a Spearman correlation of 0.95 with the full benchmark model rankings using only five selected datasets. Additional experiments indicate that the effectiveness of selection approaches depends on both the quality of dataset representations and the scale of the benchmarking regime.

</details>


### [162] [GNBAN: Graph Neural Basis Attention Networks for Long-Horizon Forecasting over Large Entity Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27863)
*Janak M. Patel,Anirudh Deodhar,Dagnachew Birru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GNBAN是一种端到端架构，结合异构图表示学习和可解释的基分解头，在零售需求预测中相比图基线方法提升约4-5%的WRMSSE，并能直接分解出趋势、季节性和残差成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售层级底部的需求预测需要预测数万个相关长时程序列，现有方法存在扩展性差（经典统计方法需为每个序列建单独模型）、联合状态空间过大（深度自回归模型）、可解释性不足（图基预测器产生不透明的长时程预测）等问题。

Method: 提出GNBAN架构：1) 将零售数据表示为从关系模式导出的异构图，单一模型服务整个目录；2) 将每个预测分解为趋势、季节性和通用成分，而非直接预测整个时域；3) 核心创新为每基注意力机制——每个基函数拥有独立可学习的查询，从实体历史邻域独立检索信息，使不同基函数专门化到不同时间模式并保持可解释性。

Result: 在M5 Walmart和Favorita Grocery Sales两个大规模基准上，采用匹配协议评估，GNBAN在体积加权WRMSSE上相比匹配的图基线提升约4-5%。定性分析显示其学习到的分解无需事后解释方法即可揭示趋势、季节性和残差需求驱动因素。
