Conclusion: 可扩展的关系型预测与可解释的预测分解可以在统一的图基框架中同时实现。

Abstract: Demand forecasting at the bottom of a retail hierarchy requires predicting tens of thousands of correlated long-horizon series across products, stores, and regions. Modern systems must scale across massive catalogs, capture shared demand dynamics, and remain interpretable enough to be trusted. Classical statistical methods need a separate model per series and are hard to manage at scale; deep autoregressive models struggle as the joint state grows to tens of thousands of dimensions; and recent graph-based forecasters, while capturing cross-entity dependencies, often produce opaque long-horizon forecasts. We propose GNBAN (Graph Neural Basis Attention Network), an end-to-end architecture combining heterogeneous graph representation learning with an interpretable basis-decomposition head. Retail data are represented directly as a heterogeneous graph derived from the relational schema, so a single model serves the entire catalog. Rather than predicting the horizon directly, GNBAN decomposes each forecast into trend, seasonal, and generic components. Its key innovation is a per-basis attention mechanism: each basis function keeps its own learnable query and retrieves information independently from the entity's historical neighborhood, letting different bases specialize to distinct temporal patterns while preserving interpretability. On two large-scale benchmarks, M5 Walmart and Favorita Grocery Sales, evaluated under matched protocols, GNBAN improves volume-weighted WRMSSE by roughly 4-5% over a matched graph baseline. Qualitative analysis shows the learned decomposition exposes trend, seasonal, and residual demand drivers without post-hoc explanation methods. These results demonstrate that scalable relational forecasting and interpretable forecast decomposition can be achieved together in a unified graph-based framework.

</details>


### [163] [Dangerous Liaisons of Convex Learning and Non-Affine Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28123)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在凸学习中，梯度聚合规则的单调性保持当且仅当该规则是正仿射的；非仿射聚合（如自适应、隐私、鲁棒或公平约束）会破坏单调性，导致收敛不稳定和泛化性能下降，并提出了恢复单调性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习流水线中广泛使用非仿射梯度聚合规则（如自适应优化、隐私保护、鲁棒性、公平性约束），但这些规则可能破坏更新算子的单调性，从而影响收敛和泛化。作者希望回答一个开放问题：能否设计保持单调性的非仿射聚合规则？

Method: 理论分析：证明单调性保持的充要条件是聚合规则为正仿射；量化分析非仿射聚合对收敛稳定性和算法稳定性的负面影响；提出恢复单调性的充分条件。

Result: 单调性保持当且仅当聚合规则为正仿射；非仿射聚合会显著破坏收敛稳定性和算法稳定性；给出了可恢复单调性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 为非仿射聚合（如自适应、隐私、鲁棒、公平）在凸学习中的失败模式提供了统一的理论解释，并为设计理论上保证收敛和泛化的聚合规则指明了方向。

Abstract: Last-iterate convergence and generalization guarantees in first-order convex learning hinge on the monotonicity of the update operator. While linear averaging preserves the monotonicity of gradient updates, this property is often violated when gradients are aggregated non-affinely, as in modern pipelines enforcing constraints like adaptivity, privacy, robustness or fairness. Whether it is possible to design non-affine aggregation rules that maintain monotonicity has remained an open question. We answer this question negatively: we prove that the monotonicity of aggregated gradients is preserved if and only if the aggregation rule is positively affine. Consequently, non-affine aggregation prevents steady convergence and substantially degrade algorithmic stability. We quantify these drawbacks and propose a path forward by identifying sufficient conditions under which monotonicity can be restored. Our results provide a unified theoretical framework explaining the disparate failure modes observed in modern learning systems.

</details>


### [164] [Disentangling Continuous-Time Latent Dynamics: Identifiability of Latent SDEs via Diffusion Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28228)
*Yuanyuan Wang,Wenjie Wang,Haoxuan Li,Mingming Gong,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在连续时间潜在随机微分方程（SDE）模型中，通过环境诱导的扩散协方差变化，可以实现潜在坐标的排列和缩放可识别性，并提出了一种两阶段估计器用于潜在解纠缠和因果图恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间潜在因果模型已建立强可识别性结果，但连续时间潜在随机微分方程（SDE）模型的可识别性仍未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究加性噪声潜在SDE模型，通过未知非线性微分同胚观测，共享漂移但环境特定扩散协方差。证明两个具有逐坐标方差比成对不同的对角扩散机制可识别潜在坐标（排列和缩放），无需漂移稀疏性假设。先从线性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck系统证明，再推广至一般加性噪声潜在SDE。提出两阶段估计器用于潜在解纠缠和可选的图恢复。

Result: 在温和光滑性条件下，瞬时漂移-雅可比因果图可识别至相同排列；合成系统实验验证了理论可识别性边界；Hardanger Bridge监测数据展示了实际传感器轨迹上的应用效果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了连续时间潜在SDE模型中基于环境扩散协方差变化的结构可识别性理论，为连续时间序列的因果表示学习提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Causal representation learning for time series has developed strong identifiability results in discrete-time latent causal models, but identifiability in continuous-time latent stochastic differential equation (SDE) models remains largely open. We address this gap using environment-induced shifts in diffusion covariance. We study additive-noise latent SDEs observed through an unknown nonlinear diffeomorphism, with shared drift but environment-specific diffusion covariance. We show that two diagonal diffusion regimes with pairwise distinct coordinate-wise variance ratios identify the latent coordinates up to permutation and scaling, without any sparsity assumption on the drift. We first prove this result for linear Ornstein--Uhlenbeck systems and then extend it to general additive-noise latent SDEs. Under mild smoothness, the instantaneous drift-Jacobian causal graph is identifiable up to the same permutation. We propose a two-stage estimator for latent disentanglement and optional graph recovery; experiments on synthetic systems confirm the predicted identifiability boundary, and an application to Hardanger Bridge monitoring data illustrates the approach on real sensor trajectories.

</details>


### [165] [How Width and Data Shape Generalization Scaling Laws in Quadratic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28242)
*Julius Girardin,Emanuele Troiani,Yizhou Xu,Vittorio Erba,Florent Krzakala,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了特征学习模型中泛化误差随模型参数数量和样本数量的联合缩放规律，通过分析二次两层网络在有限样本设置下的正则化经验误差最小化，揭示了受数据谱结构控制的相图和幂律缩放行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解现代机器学习中模型性能如何随模型大小和数据规模共同缩放是一个核心问题，但现有理论工作通常局限于固定特征、无限宽度或在线SGD等场景，缺乏对特征学习模型中参数数量与样本数量联合缩放规律的理论刻画。

Method: 对二次两层网络在结构化数据下的有限样本设置进行理论分析，研究ℓ2正则化经验测试误差最小化，显式刻画泛化误差作为样本数、模型宽度和正则化参数的函数。

Result: 揭示了参数数量变化时具有不同缩放 regimes 的相图；泛化误差遵循由目标谱结构控制的数据依赖幂律；刻画了不同 regime 之间的转变，包括插值开始的转变及其对泛化的影响。

Conclusion: 发现了特征学习模型中参数数量与样本数量联合缩放的新规律，为理解现代深度学习的缩放行为提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Understanding how performance scales jointly with model size and data is a central problem in modern machine learning. Existing theoretical works on scaling laws typically describe generalization as a function of data or compute, often in fixed-feature or infinite-width regimes and for online SGD. Here, we instead study how generalization scales with the number of trainable parameters and the number of samples in a feature-learning model. We analyze $\ell_2$-regularized empirical test error minimization in a quadratic two-layer network in a finite-sample setting with structured data. This setting allows for an explicit characterization of the generalization error as a function of the number of samples, model width, and regularization. Our results reveal a phase diagram with distinct scaling regimes as the number of parameters varies. In particular, the generalization error follows data-dependent power laws controlled by the spectral structure of the target. We further characterize the transitions between regimes, including the onset of interpolation, and their impact on generalization.

</details>


### [166] [VGB for Masked Diffusion Model: Efficient Test-time Scaling for Reward Satisfaction and Sample Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28301)
*Kijung Jeon,Thuy-Duong Vuong,Molei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MDM-VGB是一种增强掩码扩散模型（MDM）的离散扩散采样器，通过在去掩码生成过程中引入理论上合理的奖励引导重掩码机制（灵感来自Jerrum-Sinclair回溯马尔可夫链），实现了在满足结构约束的同时优化下游奖励，理论上具有二次复杂度且对过程验证器噪声鲁棒，在Sudoku和QM9等基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）是改进生成模型的有前景范式，尤其当输出需满足结构约束或优化下游奖励时。现有方法如best-of-N存在误差累积导致指数复杂度的问题，需要更高效且理论上合理的采样策略。

Method: 将Jerrum-Sinclair回溯马尔可夫链从固定前缀树扩展到掩码状态图，允许任意位置上token的去掩码和重掩码。通过奖励引导的重掩码操作，偏好产生更高价值部分配置的移动，实现高效高奖励生成和低奖励样本的有效修复。

Result: 理论证明MDM-VGB对过程验证器噪声具有鲁棒性，且实现二次复杂度（quadratic complexity）；而best-of-N等启发式方法因误差累积导致指数复杂度。在Sudoku和QM9等约束满足与科学基准上取得强实证性能。

Conclusion: MDM-VGB提供了一种理论上严谨、计算高效的推理时扩展方法，通过奖励引导的重掩码机制有效提升了离散扩散模型在结构化生成任务上的表现，且鲁棒性优于常见测试时启发式方法。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling is a promising paradigm to improve generative models, especially when outputs must satisfy structural constraints or optimize downstream rewards. We consider Masked Diffusion Model (MDM) and introduce MDM-VGB, a discrete diffusion sampler that augments unmasking generation with theoretically principled reward-guided remasking. Inspired by the recent success of the classical Jerrum-Sinclair backtracking Markov chain in reward-tilted generation, MDM-VGB extends the backtracking random walk from a fixed prefix tree to a masked-state graph, allowing tokens to be unmasked and remasked at arbitrary positions. The resulting sampler favors unmasking and remasking moves that lead to higher-value partial configurations, enabling both effective high-reward generation and efficient repair of low-reward samples. We prove that MDM-VGB is robust to process-verifier noise and achieves quadratic complexity, while popular test-time heuristics such as best-of-$N$ can incur exponential complexity due to error accumulation. Our theoretical findings are corroborated by strong empirical performance, particularly on popular constraint-satisfaction and scientific benchmarks such as Sudoku and QM9.

</details>


### [167] [Training Observable Control Policies to Expose Agent State Through Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27609)
*Andres Enriquez Fernandez,John J. Bird*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在通信受限条件下，利用智能体的可观测动作来估计其状态，通过强化学习训练策略提高可观测性，并在飞行器跟踪问题中验证了该方法在提升可观测性的同时几乎不影响任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理或操作约束常导致通信受限，使得多智能体协调或监控变得困难。即使没有强通信，仍可能存在部分信息，但智能体状态需要重构估计。本文旨在利用智能体与环境交互时产生的动作（无需显式通信）来估计其状态。

Method: 采用强化学习方法，通过在训练奖励中鼓励策略可观测性（policy observability），使学得的策略更有利于状态估计问题。通过对训练后的智能体进行仿真分析来评估可观测性。

Result: 在飞行器跟踪问题中，成功找到了一个具有增强可观测性的策略，且该策略对名义任务性能的影响极小。

Conclusion: 在通信受限场景下，通过强化学习设计可观测策略，可在不显著牺牲任务性能的前提下有效提升状态估计能力，为低通信条件下的智能体监控与协调提供可行方案。

Abstract: Physical or operational constraints often impose communications limitations on autonomous agents. Such limitations complicate monitoring or multiagent coordination. Even when strong communications are absent, some information may still be available. The remainder of the relevant agent state may be reconstructed via estimation. The actions taken by an agent are a potential source of information -- as the agent interacts with the environment, these actions may be observed even in the absence of explicit communication. We investigate using actions to estimate the state of an agent, using reinforcement learning to develop policies which make the estimation problem more tractable. Policy observability is encouraged through the training reward and is analyzed using simulation of the trained agent. In an aircraft tracking problem a policy with enhanced observability is found that has minimal impact on nominal task performance.

</details>


### [168] [COOPA: A Modular LLM Agent Architecture for Operations Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27611)
*Chuanhao Li,Xiaoan Xu,Dirk Bergemann,Ethan X. Fang,Yehua Wei,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COOPA是一个模块化的LLM智能体架构，用于可解释、可扩展的运筹学决策支持，通过迭代置信度建模、元素级溯源解释和多求解器路由三个组件，在八个大语言模型骨干和四种基线对比中，于六个骨干上取得最佳宏平均准确率，最高超过最强基线6.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运筹学建模需要大量领域知识、数学抽象和求解器专业知识，现有基于LLM的系统在复杂问题上准确率低、输出不透明且求解器支持范围窄。

Method: COOPA包含三个组件：1) 迭代置信度建模——生成多个候选公式化，自评估并采用最大-最小置信度准则选择；2) 元素级溯源与置信度解释——将变量、参数、约束和目标与引用源文本链接，提供审计轨迹；3) 多求解器路由——将问题路由到专门的优化智能体。

Result: 在三个OR基准、八个LLM骨干和四种基线对比中，COOPA在六个骨干上取得最佳宏平均准确率，比最强基线提升最多6.7个百分点。消融实验验证了迭代置信度建模的贡献，案例研究展示了源追踪和多求解器调度的价值。

Conclusion: COOPA通过模块化设计有效提升了LLM在运筹学建模中的准确率、可解释性和求解器兼容性，为自动化OR决策支持提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Operations Research (OR) provides a rigorous framework for high-stakes decision-making, but effective OR modeling requires substantial domain knowledge, mathematical abstraction, and solver expertise. Recent LLM-based systems automate parts of this pipeline, yet remain limited by low accuracy on complex problems, opaque outputs, and narrow solver support. We propose COOPA (COoperative OPerations Agent), a modular LLM-agent architecture for interpretable and scalable OR decision support. It combines three components: iterative confidence-based modeling, which generates multiple candidate formulations, self-evaluates them across modeling dimensions, and selects one using a max-min confidence criterion; element-level provenance and confidence explanations, which link variables, parameters, constraints, and objectives to quoted source text and provide an audit trail for human verification; and multi-solver routing to specialized optimizer agents for different OR problem classes. Across three OR benchmarks, eight LLM backbones, and four baselines under identical conditions, COOPA achieves the best macro-average accuracy on six of eight backbones and improves over the strongest baseline by up to 6.7 percentage points. A within-system ablation isolates the contribution of iterative confidence-based modeling, while additional analyses and case studies illustrate the value of source traceability and multi-solver dispatch.

</details>


### [169] [Two-Stage Fine-Tuning for Protein Sequence Generation with Targeted Amino-Acid Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27939)
*Violeta Basten-Romero,Rubén Muñoz-Tafalla,Anna María Díaz-Rovira,Bertran Miquel-Oliver,Isaac Filella-Merce,Víctor Guallar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段蛋白生成管线：首先领域自适应微调（FT）使序列接近目标氨基酸组成，再通过强化学习（RL）进行迭代奖励加权微调以强制执行FT无法满足的序列约束。实验验证该方法能有效对齐氨基酸组成而不降低序列质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型虽常用于生物序列生成，但如何引导它们满足明确的分布设计目标（如氨基酸组成约束）仍缺乏研究。具体应用场景是合成饲料蛋白设计，其中膳食蛋白的氨基酸组成直接决定营养价值。

Method: 两阶段管线：(1) 领域自适应微调（FT），在领域内蛋白数据集上微调预训练语言模型；(2) 迭代奖励加权微调，通过强化学习（RL）以FT模型为冻结参考，使用组成奖励项约束序列，使氨基酸组成对齐目标分布。

Result: FT使平均组成接近目标；后续RL能进一步强制执行FT无法单独满足的序列约束；与两个基线和消融变体对比验证了奖励项设计的有效性；分离了各训练阶段的贡献；证实氨基酸组成对齐未降低序列质量。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段FT+RL管线能有效实现蛋白序列的氨基酸组成约束生成，在保持序列合理性和多样性的同时满足目标组成分布，为合成饲料蛋白设计等应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Protein language models are standard priors for biological sequence generation, but steering them toward explicit distributional design targets remains largely unexplored. We study a constrained protein generation problem in which sequences must match a desired amino-acid (AA) composition profile while preserving plausible sequence statistics and diversity. The motivating application is synthetic feed protein design, where the AA composition of dietary proteins directly determines their nutritional value. We propose a two-stage pipeline in which domain-adaptive fine-tuning (FT) on an in-domain protein dataset is followed by iterative reward-weighted FT via reinforcement learning (RL) anchored against the FT model as a frozen reference. We evaluate the pipeline on two AA compositions and find that FT brings the average composition close to the target, while the subsequent RL enforces specific sequence constraints that FT alone cannot satisfy. We additionally evaluate the design choices of the proposed composition reward term against two baselines and an ablated variant, isolate the contribution of each training stage, and verify that AA composition alignment is achieved without degrading sequence quality.

</details>


### [170] [FoggyTrust: Robust Federated Learning with Hierarchical Trust Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27622)
*Emmanuel Rassou,Tomas Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOGGYTRUST 提出了一种层次化扩展的 FLTrust 框架，通过将信任计算本地化到雾节点，在保持鲁棒性的同时处理全局数据异构性，并结合局部信任聚合与异构感知优化器（如 FedAdam 和 SCAFFOLD），在 CIFAR-10 的 Krum 和 Trim 攻击下相比 FLTrust 提升超过 50%，并在真实野生动物监测数据集上展现出应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法（如 FLTrust）在全局数据异构场景下存在信任估计的分布不匹配和客户端漂移问题，需要一种能够同时处理数据异构性与拜占庭攻击的框架。

Method: 提出 FOGGYTRUST，层次化扩展 FLTrust：将信任计算下沉到雾节点，在局部同质客户端组内进行信任评分聚合，并结合异构感知全局优化器（FedAdam、SCAFFOLD）缓解组间客户端漂移。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 的 Krum 和 Trim 攻击下相比 FLTrust 提升超过 50%；在实际野生动物监测数据集上也验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 层次化信任网络能够有效兼顾拜占庭鲁棒性与数据异构性，适用于安全关键的社会影响力场景（如分布式野生动物监测）。

Abstract: Byzantine-robust federated learning seeks to protect distributed model training from malicious or corrupted clients without requiring access to their private data. FLTrust addresses this challenge by introducing a trusted server-side root dataset that assigns trust scores to client updates for more robust aggregation. In this work, we propose FOGGYTRUST, a hierarchical extension of FLTrust that localizes trust computation to fog nodes, allowing the framework to better handle globally heterogeneous data while preserving robustness within locally homogeneous client groups. We further show that this two-level architecture can simultaneously address distribution mismatch in trust estimation and client drift across groups by combining local trust-based aggregation with heterogeneity-aware global optimizers such as FedAdam and SCAFFOLD. Across benchmark datasets, FOGGYTRUST achieves its strongest gains on more challenging heterogeneous settings, particularly on CIFAR-10 under Krum and Trim attacks, where it achieves an over 50% improvement over FLTrust. We also test FOGGYTRUST in a real-world safari dataset to show the promise of hierarchical trust networks for robust federated learning in socially impactful, safety-critical settings such as distributed wildlife monitoring.

</details>


### [171] [Continual Learning for Sequential Personalization of Small Language Models: A Stability Monitoring Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27634)
*Thomas S. Paula,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了小型语言模型（SLMs）在边缘设备上进行顺序LoRA个性化微调时的灾难性遗忘问题，提出通过检查点级别的协议监控任务性能、遗忘情况和参考集漂移，并展示轻量级参考集分布诊断能够揭示模型特有的不稳定性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLMs正被部署到边缘设备以实现个性化应用，但个性化需要模型在持续学习环境中适应不断变化的数据，这带来了灾难性遗忘的风险——学习新知识会损害先前任务或一般能力的表现。现有研究如TRACE已显示持续微调会显著降低对齐大语言模型的能力，因此需要针对SLMs的个性化场景进行专门的稳定性研究。

Method: 采用顺序LoRA个性化微调方案，在每个适应阶段后保存模型检查点，并在当前任务、先前任务和固定参考集上评估模型。通过检查点级别的协议监控任务性能、遗忘情况和参考集漂移，并利用轻量级参考集分布诊断方法分析模型的不稳定性模式。

Result: 轻量级参考集分布诊断能够揭示SLMs在顺序LoRA个性化过程中特有的模型不稳定性模式，包括那些任务级指标无法捕捉到但实际已造成有害适应的情形。

Conclusion: 本文提出的监控方法为持续学习环境下SLMs的稳定性监测提供了新的研究方向，有助于发现传统任务指标无法反映的模型退化问题。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly being considered for deployment on edge devices such as laptops, enabling private, low-latency, and locally personalized applications. However, personalization requires models to adapt over time to evolving user- or task-specific data, placing them in a continual learning setting. This creates the risk of catastrophic forgetting, where learning new information degrades performance on previously learned tasks or broader model capabilities. Recent benchmarks such as TRACE have shown that continual fine-tuning can significantly degrade the general abilities of aligned large language models. In this work, we present a study for sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs. We save model checkpoints after each adaptation stage and evaluate them on current tasks, previously seen tasks, and a fixed reference set. This checkpoint-level protocol enables us to monitor task performance, forgetting, and reference set drift over time. We show that lightweight reference set distributional diagnostics can reveal model-specific instability patterns during sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs, including cases where task-level metrics alone hide harmful adaptation. We hope this can highlight new research avenues for monitoring stability of SLMs in a continual learning setting.

</details>


### [172] [TeRoR: Decoupled Temporal Rotation with Relational Circular Region for Temporal Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27651)
*Peijia Xie,Yike Liu,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TeRoR是一种改进的时序知识图谱嵌入方法，通过将实体嵌入的时间演化进行分割、对头尾实体进行独立旋转变换以增强时序建模能力，并引入半径约束来捕捉关系的一对多、多对一、多对多映射特性，在四个数据集上取得了竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeRo方法在建模多种关系的映射特性（如一对多、多对一、多对多）方面表现不佳，同时在时序信息的表达能力上也存在局限性。因此提出改进方法TeRoR以解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 将实体嵌入的时间演化进行分割；2) 在复数向量空间中对头实体和尾实体进行独立的旋转变换，以增强时序信息建模能力；3) 针对关系特性，训练一个半径参数，将旋转和平移后的头实体约束在以尾实体为中心的圆形区域内，从而有效捕捉关系的多种映射特性。

Result: 在四个不同的TKG数据集上，TeRoR相比现有最先进模型取得了具有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: TeRoR通过改进时序信息表达和关系映射特性建模，有效提升了时序知识图谱嵌入的质量，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: In recent years, with the emergence of Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGs), research on learning entity and relation representations in TKGs has attracted increasing attention, giving rise to a large number of TKG embedding methods. TeRo is a simple and efficient temporal knowledge graph embedding approach. However, TeRo does not do well in modeling the mapping properties of various relations, such as one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many. Meanwhile, it also has limitations in the expression of temporal information. To address these issues, we propose a novel TKG embedding method named TeRoR. This method divides the temporal evolution of entity embeddings, and conducts independent rotation transformations on head and tail entities in the complex vector space to strengthen temporal information modeling capacity. In terms of relational characteristics, we train a radius to constrain the rotated and translated head entities within a circular region centered on the tail entity, which effectively captures the diverse mapping properties of relations. Experimental results demonstrate that TeRoR achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models on four distinct TKG datasets.

</details>


### [173] [Are Time-Series Foundation Models Ready for E-Nose Data? An Empirical Assessment of Their Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27672)
*Taeyeong Choi,Mohammed Kamruzzaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在电子鼻（E-Nose）气体传感数据上的表现，发现微调是必要步骤，且将TSFM嵌入与专用预测模型表示融合可进一步提升性能，揭示了TSFMs在气体传感应用中的潜力与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管TSFMs在多个时间序列任务和领域展现出强大的泛化能力，但其在气体传感数据上的效用尚未被探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 系统评估代表性TSFMs（如Chronos-2和MOMENT）在电子鼻数据上的表现，通过微调实验以及将TSFM嵌入与专用预测模型表示融合的方式，检验其在气体识别和浓度预测任务中的有效性。

Result: 微调是实现满意性能的必要条件；融合TSFM嵌入与专用模型表示能进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 当前TSFMs在气体传感应用中既展现出潜力，也存在局限性，未来需要进一步改进以适应此类数据。

Abstract: Inspired by advances in natural language processing and computer vision, "time-series foundation models" (TSFMs) have recently been introduced with the promise of strong generalization across diverse time-series tasks, including forecasting, classification, and anomaly detection, as well as across domains such as healthcare, climate science, and manufacturing. However, their utility for gas-sensing data remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, this paper systematically evaluates recent TSFMs on electronic nose (E-Nose) data. In particular, we investigate whether embeddings produced by representative TSFMs, including Chronos-2 and MOMENT, provide effective representations for gas identification and concentration prediction. Specifically, we show that fine-tuning is necessary to achieve satisfactory performance on E-Nose data, and fusing TSFM embeddings with representations learned by specialized predictive models can further improve the performance, suggesting both the potential and limitations of current TSFMs for gas-sensing applications.

</details>


### [174] [Textual Belief States for World Models: Identifiable Representation Learning Under Strict Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27681)
*Xiang Gao,Kaiwen Dong,Yuguang Yao,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Kamalika Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在文本部分可观测环境中实现严格潜在状态中介的方法，通过离散、可解释、可变长度的文本潜在状态和因子化GRPO训练方法，在保持一步预测准确性的同时，显著提升了表示质量和 rollout 性能（最高提升98%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于LLM的部分可观测环境中，由于"历史旁路"问题，预测性能无法反映表示质量，潜在状态变得不可识别。严格潜在状态中介虽能解决这一问题，但在文本环境下实施面临挑战：文本潜在状态离散不可微，且LLM解码器容易绕过瓶颈。

Method: 1) 形式化证明了严格中介的必要性，展示了严格中介使表示质量可经验性测试，而历史泄漏架构会破坏这种联系；2) 引入离散、可解释、可变长度的文本潜在状态；3) 提出因子化GRPO (fGRPO)，一种树结构强化学习方法，在训练中强制实施严格中介。

Result: 在TextWorld和ScienceWorld实验中，在保持一步预测准确性的同时，表示质量提升高达57%，rollout性能提升高达98%，且收益随任务复杂度和时间跨度增加而增加。

Conclusion: 严格潜在状态中介在文本领域可以有效实现，能够显著提升表示质量和长期推理性能，尤其适用于复杂和长时域任务。

Abstract: World models in partially observed environments rely on latent representations that summarize interaction history, but in many modern LLM-based architectures predictive performance fails to reflect representation quality due to history bypass, rendering the latent state unidentifiable. Strict latent state mediation, requiring predictions to depend only on the latent state and action, is a classical principle that resolves this, but enforcing it in text-based settings is an open challenge: textual latent states are discrete and non-differentiable, precluding variational training, and expressive LLM decoders readily ignore the bottleneck. We show how to make strict mediation work in the text domain. We formalize why it is necessary, showing that strict mediation makes representation quality empirically testable while history-leaky architectures break this connection. We then introduce textual latent states, which are discrete, interpretable, and variable-length, and factorized GRPO (fGRPO), a tree-structured reinforcement learning method that enforces strict mediation during training. Experiments on TextWorld and ScienceWorld show preserved one-step prediction accuracy alongside up to 57\% gains in representation quality and 98\% improvements in rollout performance, increasing with task complexity and horizon.

</details>


### [175] [OperatorSHAP: Fast and Accurate Shapley Value Estimation for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28065)
*Joshua Stiller,Santo M. A. R. Thies,Felix Czaja,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OperatorSHAP，一种网格无关的归因方法，使得可以像FastSHAP一样为神经算子训练解释器，在函数空间建立归因理论框架，其解释在不同分辨率下与离散Shapley值一致，且无需重新训练即可跨网格大小迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法（如Shapley值）计算成本高，而当前摊销解释器（如FastSHAP）局限于同质输入，无法处理物理应用中常见的不规则网格和几何结构数据。

Method: 提出OperatorSHAP，一种网格无关的归因方法和训练流程，用于训练神经算子的FastSHAP式解释器，并在函数空间建立归因理论框架，将其与Aumann-Shapley值联系起来。

Result: OperatorSHAP的解释与最先进的离散Shapley值在不同分辨率下一致，且无需重新训练即可跨网格大小迁移。

Conclusion: OperatorSHAP解决了现有摊销归因方法无法处理不规则网格和几何结构的问题，为物理应用中的安全关键决策提供了高效且一致的模型可解释性。

Abstract: Understanding model predictions is essential for physical applications, where outputs often inform safety-critical decisions, such as structural load assessment, weather warnings, and clinical diagnosis. Shapley values satisfy many desirable properties as an attribution method, but their computational cost during inference hinders their practical use. Current amortized explainers, such as FastSHAP, are limited to homogeneous inputs, which is problematic for physical applications where data often comes from irregular grids and geometries. We introduce OperatorSHAP, a grid-agnostic attribution method and training procedure that allows us to train FastSHAP-like explainers for neural operators. We establish a theoretical framework for attributions in function space, connecting to Aumann-Shapley values. We further show that OperatorSHAP's explanations are consistent with state-of-the-art discrete Shapley values across resolutions and transfer across grid sizes without retraining.

</details>


### [176] [Beyond Sparse Supervision: Diffusion-Guided Learning for Few-Shot Graph Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28134)
*Liming Liu,Chao Hu,Mingfei Lu,Yiwei Ge,Xingle Li,Heyuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADC-GNN是一个结合扩散引导特征增强、对比表示学习和多跳谱注意力机制的图神经网络框架，用于解决图欺诈检测中的稀疏不平衡监督和表示稀释问题，在公开基准和私有数据集上取得了一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界欺诈图存在两个耦合挑战：1）稀疏且不平衡的监督——已验证的欺诈标签稀缺且严重偏向良性账户；2）表示稀释——空间消息传递可能过度平滑伪装异常，而谱滤波器可能抑制欺诈相关的中高频不规则性。

Method: ADC-GNN包含三个核心模块：1）扩散引导特征增强——在余弦调度下构建噪声扰动的节点特征视图，非全拓扑生成的图扩散模型；2）对比表示学习——通过扰动间对比学习稳定节点表示；3）多跳谱注意力——自适应强调欺诈相关的跳级和关系级线索。

Result: 在1%训练设置下，ADC-GNN在三个公开基准上相较于原始图欺诈基线和四个近期图异常/欺诈基线取得一致改进；额外在约6万条记录的私有真实世界电信交易数据集上进行了案例研究。

Conclusion: ADC-GNN通过扩散引导对比学习与多跳谱注意力的统一框架，有效应对了图欺诈检测中的标签稀疏和表示稀释问题，其有效性通过消融实验、训练比率分析等多维度评估得到验证。

Abstract: Graph-based fraud detection is essential for safeguarding large-scale transaction systems, where undetected anomalies may lead to substantial financial losses and security risks. Real-world fraud graphs pose two coupled challenges: sparse and imbalanced supervision, where verified fraudulent labels are scarce and heavily skewed toward benign accounts, and representation dilution, where spatial message passing may oversmooth camouflaged anomalies while spectral filters may suppress fraud-relevant mid- and high-frequency irregularities. To address these challenges, we propose ADC-GNN, short for Attention-guided Diffusion-Contrastive Graph Neural Network, a unified framework that combines diffusion-guided feature augmentation, contrastive representation learning, and multi-hop spectral attention for few-shot graph fraud detection. The diffusion component is formulated as a feature-space denoising augmentation mechanism rather than a full topology-generative graph diffusion model: it constructs noise-perturbed node-feature views under a cosine schedule and uses contrastive learning to stabilize node representations across perturbations. The spectral attention module further adaptively emphasizes fraud-relevant hop-level and relation-level cues. We evaluate ADC-GNN primarily on three public benchmarks and additionally report a proprietary real-world telecom transaction dataset with approximately 60,000 records as a private case study. Under the 1% training setting, ADC-GNN achieves consistent improvements over original graph fraud baselines and four protocol-consistent recent graph anomaly/fraud baselines on the public benchmarks. Additional analyses on split stability, training ratios, oversampling alternatives, module-level ablations, diffusion schedules, and runtime and memory-consumption comparisons further characterize the effective operating regime of ADC-GNN.

</details>


### [177] [Aurora: A Leverage-Aware Spectral Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27715)
*Alec Dewulf,Dhruv Pai,Li Yang,Ashley Zhang,Ben Keigwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Aurora优化器，通过行归一化解决了Muon优化器在矩阵参数更新中行范数不均匀导致神经元失效的问题，同时保持了Muon的极因子几何结构。实验表明Aurora优于Muon，且在宽MLP层上效果更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在处理高瘦矩阵（如MLP层中的投影矩阵）时，更新量的行范数会变得不均匀，导致部分神经元持续接收微小更新而最终失效。现有方法虽添加行归一化，但破坏了Muon的极因子几何结构。

Method: 提出Aurora优化器，在保持Muon的极因子几何特性的前提下，对矩阵参数更新施加行均匀性约束，通过额外的行归一化步骤实现。

Result: Aurora在预训练实验中优于Muon；结合现有方法后，在modded-nanoGPT速度跑优化器赛道中达到谱优化器最先进水平；且Aurora相对于Muon的收益随MLP扩展因子增大而提升。

Conclusion: Aurora是一种更优的优化器，能有效解决Muon的行范数不均匀问题，并可能在超宽MLP层训练中发挥更大作用。

Abstract: We show that for tall matrix parameters, like projection matrices in the MLP layers, the Muon update can have row norms that are arbitrarily non-uniform. This can lead to a self-reinforcing feedback loop whereby neurons receive persistently small updates and eventually do not contribute meaningfully to network outputs. This problem is effectively mitigated by an additional row normalization step, but current methods do this in a way that moves the Muon update geometry away from the polar factor of the momentum matrix, which we find is undesirable. We propose Aurora, an optimizer that enforces row-uniformity of matrix parameter updates while respecting Muon's polar factor geometry. Aurora outperforms Muon in our pre-training experiments and, when combined with existing methods, achieves state-of-the-art performance among spectral optimizers on the optimizer track of the modded-nanoGPT speedrun. Additionally, we find that Aurora's empirical gains over Muon scale with the MLP expansion factor, suggesting that Aurora may allow for effective training of very wide MLP layers.

</details>


### [178] [Learning to Reason with Curriculum II: Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27721)
*Nived Rajaraman,Audrey Huang,Miroslav Dudik,Robert Schapire,Dylan Foster,Akshay Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过半自动机模拟问题，从理论上证明了一种基于自动课程学习的分解组合方法，相比直接学习，在监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RLVR）两种设置下均能显著降低统计复杂度，在SFT中突破Ω(T)的token壁垒，在RLVR中将参考模型的全序列覆盖要求放宽到短块覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解组合泛化的理论基础：为何将问题分解为子问题比直接求解更高效？

Method: 以半自动机模拟（状态跟踪、正则语言识别、模算术）为规范问题，采用自动课程学习框架递归分解长序列为短子问题，分别学习求解再组合。

Result: (1) SFT设置：仅需2^{O(√log T)}个监督token（亚多项式），突破Ω(T)壁垒；(2) RLVR设置：将参考模型要求从全序列长度T覆盖降为短块长度B覆盖，条件指数级减弱。

Conclusion: 分解组合方法在理论上能显著提升学习效率，为链式思维推理和组合泛化提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Compositional generalization, the ability to solve complex problems by combining solutions to simpler sub-problems, is a fundamental capability of both natural and artificial intelligence, and a key mechanism underlying chain-of-thought reasoning. However, the theoretical underpinnings of compositional generalization remain poorly understood: when and why does decomposing a problem into parts yield more efficient learning than solving it directly? We study this question through the canonical problem of learning to simulate semiautomata (predicting the outcome of $T$ steps of sequential computation), a model that captures state tracking, regular language recognition, and modular arithmetic. We show that an autocurriculum-based approach building on Part I of this series, recursively decomposing longer sequences into shorter sub-problems, learning to solve them, and composing the solutions, achieves dramatically better statistical complexity than direct methods. (i) For a setting inspired by supervised fine-tuning (SFT) where the learner receives interactive feedback on intermediate states of the computation, curriculum facilitates learning from only $2^{\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log T})}$ tokens of supervision; i.e., subpolynomial in the sequence length $T$, overcoming the $Ω(T)$ token barrier required by direct simulation. (ii) For a setting inspired by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where the learner improves a pre-trained reference model using an outcome verifier, we show that curriculum reduces the requirement on the reference model from coverage at the full sequence length $T$ to coverage at a shorter block length $B \ll T$, an exponentially weaker condition.

</details>


### [179] [CPAgents: Agentic Composite Phenotype Generation for Cardiac Disease Association](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28179)
*Zuoou Li,Wenlong Zhao,Kelly Yu,Weitong Zhang,Paul M. Matthews,Wenjia Bai,Bernhard Kainz,Mengyun Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CPAgents框架，通过三个智能体（分析者、提议者、验证者）自动构建可解释的复合心脏影像表型，在群体规模队列中显著提升了疾病判别性能（72个分类器-疾病-指标组合中56个排名第一），实现了超越专家驱动特征选择的更强表型-疾病关联发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表型组关联研究依赖预定义的单变量表型或专家手工特征，无法捕捉有临床意义的非线性效应和跨表型交互作用，限制了大规模心血管研究中表型-疾病关联的发现能力。

Method: 提出CPAgents框架，包含三个智能体：Analyst识别统计病理并提出候选变换；Proposer在数值安全约束下生成医学和统计学驱动的表达式；Verifier通过多阶段标准评估候选表型并生成透明的证据链。最终从基础影像特征中自动构建多项式、比值和交互形式等可解释复合表型。

Result: 在群体规模心脏影像队列中，复合表型显著改善疾病判别能力：72个分类器-疾病-指标组合中56个排名第一（基线仅18个），所有九类临床疾病类别均观察到性能提升。

Conclusion: 该框架能产生简洁、临床可解释的表型公式和透明证据链，实现超越专家驱动特征选择的规模化更强表型-疾病关联发现。

Abstract: Identifying robust associations between cardiac imaging phenotypes and clinical diseases is fundamental to population-scale cardiovascular research and reliable risk stratification. However, current phenome-wide association studies rely on pre-defined, single-variable phenotypes or expert-crafted features, which limits their ability to capture clinically meaningful non-linear effects and cross-phenotype interactions. To address this, we propose CPAgents, an iterative phenotype-Composition framework for cardiovascular Phenome-wide association study (PheWAS) that automatically constructs and validates interpretable composite phenotypes (e.g., polynomial, ratio, and interaction forms) from base imaging features. Specifically, our system coordinates three agents: (i) an Analyst that identifies statistical pathologies and nominates candidate transformations; (ii) a Proposer that generates constrained, medically and statistically motivated expressions under numerical safety rules; and (iii) a Verifier that evaluates candidates using multi-stage criteria and produces transparent evidence trails for accepted phenotypes. Evaluated on a population-scale cardiac imaging cohort, the discovered composite phenotypes markedly improve disease discrimination: across 72 classifier-disease-metric combinations, our variants achieve the top rank in 56 cases versus 18 for baselines, with gains observed across all nine clinical disease categories. Our framework yields compact, clinically interpretable phenotype formulas with transparent evidence trails, enabling scalable discovery of stronger phenotype-disease associations beyond expert-driven feature selection.

</details>


### [180] [Reduction of Probabilistic Chemical Reaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27737)
*Mauricio Montes,Gregoire Sergeant-Perthuis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将因子图约简技术迁移至Napp-Adams编译的化学反应网络（CRNs）中，在保持信念传播不动点的前提下，显著减小CRN规模，解决概率生化计算中网络过大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在细胞层面编程自适应行为需要实现概率计算，但现有基于CRN的隐马尔可夫模型、因子图等实现需要规模过大的反应网络，且经典CRN约简技术不适用。

Method: 恢复Napp-Adams编译CRN中编码的因子图结构，将因子图约简结果迁移至其化学实现，在保持信念传播不动点条件下减少CRN规模。

Result: 获得了显著更小的CRN，同时保留了幸存变量上的信念传播不动点。

Conclusion: 通过因子图约简可有效压缩概率生化计算中的CRN规模，为细胞级自适应行为编程提供更可行的方案。

Abstract: Programming adaptive behaviors at the cellular level is a long-standing goal that raises the question of how probabilistic computation can be implemented in biochemical systems. Chemical reaction networks (CRNs) provide such a substrate and have been shown to realize probabilistic models, including hidden Markov models and factor graphs, with dynamics reproducing Bayesian inference and belief propagation. However, encoding these algorithms typically requires prohibitively large reaction networks, and classical CRN reduction techniques do not directly apply. By recovering the factor graph structure encoded in Napp--Adams-compiled CRNs, we transport recent factor-graph reduction results to their chemical implementations, obtaining significantly smaller CRNs while preserving the belief-propagation fixed points on surviving variables.

</details>


### [181] [LLawCo: Learning Laws of Cooperation for Modeling Embodied Multi-Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28182)
*Qinhong Zhou,Chuang Gan,Anoop Cherian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLawCo框架，通过让具身智能体反思失败经验提取高层行为法则（如“必要时交谈”、“等待伙伴”），并利用监督微调将这些法则融入思维链，从而在部分可观测的去中心化环境中实现智能体之间及与任务目标的对齐，在PARTNR-Dialog和TDW-MAT基准上分别提升4.5%和6.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的具身智能体在去中心化、部分可观测环境中常表现出与伙伴行为不一致或与环境状态不匹配的行为，导致合作效率低下、任务成功率差。

Method: 提出LLawCo框架：1) 智能体通过反思失败经验提取行为模式；2) 从模式中推导高层行为法则；3) 通过监督微调将法则显式融入思维链，使推理与任务要求和伙伴行为对齐。

Result: 在PARTNR-Dialog和TDW-MAT基准上，基于四种骨干LLM，平均成功率分别提升4.5%和6.8%，超越现有开源通信式智能体框架。

Conclusion: LLawCo通过将行为法则纳入思维链，有效改善了具身智能体在多智能体协作任务中的对齐能力和任务成功率。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating in decentralized and partially observable environments have attracted growing attention in recent years. However, existing large language model (LLM)-based agents often exhibit behaviors that are misaligned with their partners or inconsistent with the environment state, leading to inefficient cooperation and poor task success. To address this challenge, we propose a novel framework, Learning Laws of Cooperation (LLawCo), that enables embodied agents to autonomously align with both their partners and task objectives. Our framework allows agents to reflect on past failures to extract misaligned behavioral patterns, which are used to derive high-level behavioral laws, such as "Talk when necessary" and "Wait for partner." These laws are explicitly incorporated into the agents' chains of thought via supervised fine-tuning, aligning their reasoning with task requirements and the behavior of other agents. To evaluate our approach, we introduce PARTNR-Dialog, a large-scale multi-agent communicative and cooperative planning benchmark built on the PARTNR environment. Experiments on existing tasks and our new benchmark demonstrate significant improvements in cooperative efficiency and task success rates. Across four backbone LLMs, our method achieves average success rate improvements of 4.5% on the PARTNR-Dialog benchmark and 6.8% on the TDW-MAT benchmark over state-of-the-art open-source communicative agent frameworks. See the LLawCo project page for details: https://www.merl.com/research/highlights/LLawCo

</details>


### [182] [The Weakest Link Tells It All: Outcome-Supervised Process Reward Modeling via Learnable Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27739)
*Tianyu Jia,Yue Fang,Hongxin Ding,Rihong Qiu,Zhibang Yang,Zhijing Wu,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LCA框架，通过可学习的信用分配机制和弱链分配原则，将结果监督的PRM建模为多实例学习问题，采用Softmax-Weighted-Sum池化技术，在无需步骤级标注的情况下实现细粒度过程奖励建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结果监督PRM存在信用分配难题，即无法准确将最终结果归因到正确的推理步骤。均匀分配和因果分配都无法锚定步骤正确性，阻碍了过程错误识别。需要一种既能利用结果监督的可扩展性，又能准确分配信用到各推理步骤的方法。

Method: 1) 提出弱链分配原则（Weakest Link Assignment）：推理链的强度取决于其最弱环节；2) 将结果监督PRM形式化为多实例学习（MIL）问题；3) 引入Softmax-Weighted-Sum (SWS)池化技术，处理推理状态间的强依赖性和冗余性；4) 联合学习信用分配和奖励建模。

Result: 在多个任务和骨干网络上，LCA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art outcome-supervised PRMs。算法在温和假设下满足贝叶斯一致性。

Conclusion: LCA框架有效解决了结果监督PRM中的信用分配问题，通过将问题建模为MIL并引入SWS池化，实现了无需步骤级标注的高效过程奖励建模，为PRM训练提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) enhance the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) by providing fine-grained feedback, yet training PRMs typically requires expensive stepwise annotations. Outcome-supervised PRMs offer a scalable alternative by learning from final-answer correctness alone, but this introduces a fundamental *credit assignment* challenge, i.e., attributing outcomes to responsible reasoning steps. Existing approaches rely on either uniform or causal assignment, both of which fail to anchor credit in step correctness and thus hinder process error identification.
  In this work, we propose Outcome-Supervised Process Reward Modeling via **L**earnable **C**redit **A**ssignment (**LCA**), an outcome-supervised PRM framework that jointly learns credit assignment and reward modeling under the principle of *Weakest Link Assignment: a reasoning chain is as strong as its weakest link*. To address mutual dependence between credit assignment and reward modeling, we formalize outcome-supervised PRM as a Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) problem and introduce Softmax-Weighted-Sum (SWS) pooling, an MIL pooling technique tailored for strong dependence and redundancy among reasoning states. We prove Bayes consistency of our algorithm under mild assumptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that **LCA** consistently outperforms state-of-the-art outcome-supervised PRMs across multiple tasks and backbones. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LCA.

</details>


### [183] [The Remittance Blueprint: Data-driven Intelligence for Sri Lanka](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28190)
*Dhinanjaya Fernando,Dinura Ginige,Kalana Lakshan,Chanupa Gurusinghe,Lasana Pahanga,Subavarshana Arumugam,Sandeepa Weerasekara,Sandareka Wickramanayake,Nisansa de Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于32年（1994-2025）斯里兰卡月度数据，运用时间序列分析与机器学习方法，发现汇款流入主要由外部宏观经济变量（汇率动态和全球油价）驱动，而非国内指标。Ridge回归比SARIMA提升73.8%预测精度（年化RMSE：4.948亿美元），预测2026年汇款达90.01亿美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯里兰卡作为依赖海外汇款的经济体，需系统性识别汇款驱动因素并提升预测精度，以制定有效的汇率政策、技能移民策略和正规金融渠道，增强长期经济韧性。

Method: 使用384个月度协调数据集，综合运用探索性数据分析、平稳性校正时间序列建模（ADF检验、Johansen协整、VAR/VECM）及监督学习（Ridge回归、SARIMA等），并通过脉冲响应分析检验汇率贬值和油价冲击的非对称影响。

Result: 汇款主要由汇率和全球油价等外部变量驱动；Ridge回归相较SARIMA实现73.8%的精度提升（年化RMSE 4.948亿美元）；预测2026年汇款在稳定条件下达90.01亿美元。

Conclusion: 斯里兰卡汇款高度依赖全球宏观经济，需加强汇率政策管理、推动技能型劳动力迁移、扩大正规汇款渠道以增强经济韧性。

Abstract: This study analyzes Sri Lankan migration and remittances over 32 years (1994-2025). Using a 384-month harmonized dataset, we apply exploratory data analysis, stationarity corrected time-series modeling (ADF, Johansen, VAR/VECM), and supervised learning. Results reveal remittance inflows are primarily driven by external macroeconomic variables, specifically exchange rate dynamics and global oil prices, rather than domestic indicators. Impulse response analysis confirms the asymmetric impact of currency depreciation and oil price shocks. Predictively, multivariate machine learning models outperform traditional univariate approaches; Ridge Regression achieves a 73.8% accuracy improvement over SARIMA (Annualized RMSE: USD 494.8 Mn). The optimized framework projects 2026 remittances at USD 9,001 million under stable conditions. These findings highlight the structural dependence of remittances on global economies, emphasizing the need for robust exchange rate policies, skilled migration, and formal financial channels to enhance long-term economic resilience.

</details>


### [184] [PerturbCellRL: Verifier-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Single-Cell Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27752)
*Dongxia Wu,Mingyu Li,Yuhui Zhang,Anurendra Kumar,Emma Lundberg,Serena Yeung-Levy,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PerturbCellRL框架，利用强化学习对预训练的单细胞转录组生成器进行后训练，通过细胞级验证器（Pearson top-k相似度、RMSE top-k邻近度、DE Spearman、通路活性）作为奖励，确保生成的单细胞在生物学上具有一致性，在多个遗传和化学扰动基准上优于预训练模型，且与最先进方法在群体层面指标上具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞扰动生成模型虽能改善群体层面的预测，但生成的单个细胞未被显式验证其生物学一致性，缺乏细胞级别的质量保证。

Method: 采用强化学习框架，对预训练的单细胞转录组生成器进行后训练，使用四类细胞级验证器作为奖励信号：Pearson top-k相似度、RMSE top-k邻近度、DE Spearman以及通路活性验证器（确保通路响应与已知扰动生物学一致）。

Result: 在多个遗传和化学扰动基准上，PerturbCellRL在奖励对齐评估指标和保留评估指标上均优于预训练的流匹配生成器，同时在群体层面指标上与最先进方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 将可信赖的单细胞预测问题重新定义为验证器引导的生成对齐过程，超越单纯匹配表达分布，显式检查单细胞扰动效应的生物学一致性。

Abstract: Single-cell perturbation models can reduce costly wet-lab screening by predicting how cells respond transcriptionally to interventions. While recent generative models improve population-level prediction, individual generated cells are not explicitly checked for biological consistency. We introduce PerturbCellRL, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that post-trains a pretrained single-cell transcriptomic generator using a suite of cell-level verifiers as rewards. These verifiers define four rewards: Pearson top-k similarity, RMSE top-k proximity, DE Spearman, and Pathway activity. The Pathway activity verifier rewards cells whose pathway responses match known perturbation biology. We evaluate PerturbCellRL on multiple genetic and chemical perturbation benchmarks. Across these benchmarks, PerturbCellRL improves over the pretrained flow-matching generator on reward-aligned evaluation metrics and a held-out evaluation metric. Moreover, PerturbCellRL remains competitive with state-of-the-art methods on population-level metrics. Together, these results frame trustworthy single-cell prediction as verifier-guided generative alignment, moving beyond matching expression distributions toward predictions whose single-cell perturbation effects are explicitly checked for biological consistency.

</details>


### [185] [Towards Value-Constrained Credit Assignment in Fully Delegated AI Cooperatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28217)
*Young Yoon,Jimin Kim,Soyeon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在完全委托的AI合作社中基于价值约束的奖励分配框架，利用遍历学习（TL）实现细粒度的梯度过滤与贡献归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在AI合作社中，多个委托人（人类）通过代理贡献数据并参与模型更新，但委托人之间存在异质性价值约束，现有方法（如FedAvg式的联邦学习）无法精细地根据每个委托人的价值约束来筛选更新并分配奖励的问题。

Method: 提出价值条件梯度过滤（value-conditioned gradient filtering），在线边际贡献信号（online marginal contribution signals）和累计收益结算（cumulative revenue settlement）三个机制，基于遍历学习（TL）实现。TL能够在不牺牲质量的前提下进行去中心化反向传播，保留显式的遍历和梯度路径，提供比FedAvg更精细的归因基础。

Result: 框架在数据估值、联邦贡献估计、个性化联邦学习和多元对齐等多个维度进行了定位和分析，展示了其与现有方法的区别与优势。

Conclusion: 该框架为存在异质性价值约束的完全委托AI合作社提供了更精细的奖励分配机制，TL作为归因基座优于传统聚合中心的分布式学习方法。

Abstract: We propose a framework for reward allocation in fully delegated AI cooperatives where humans are represented by agents that contribute data and participate in model updates under heterogeneous value constraints. The key idea is to credit only those updates that remain admissible after screening them against each principal's value profile. We formulate value-conditioned gradient filtering, online marginal contribution signals, and cumulative revenue settlement within a traversal learning (TL) substrate. TL is especially attractive here because it performs decentralized backpropagation without the quality loss associated with aggregation-centric distributed learning and, we argue, offers a finer attribution substrate than FedAvg-style federated learning by preserving explicit traversal and gradient paths. The framework is positioned against data valuation, federated contribution estimation, personalized federated learning, and pluralistic alignment.

</details>


### [186] [Layerwise Progressive Freezing: A Training Scaffold for Depth-Scalable Binary Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27759)
*Evan Gibson Smith,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StoMPP提出随机掩码部分渐进二值化方法，通过按层从输入到输出逐步将裁剪权重和激活替换为硬二值版本，实现无STE（直通估计器）的二值神经网络训练，在深度网络中显著提升精度；与STE组合使用时效果更佳，并发现前向逐层渐进顺序对防止深度坍塌至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的直通估计器（STE）在训练二值神经网络时存在前向/反向传播不一致问题，导致网络加深时精度严重下降。本文探索一个正交维度：训练过程中何时何地施加二值化约束，旨在消除STE带来的精度退化。

Method: 提出StoMPP（随机掩码部分渐进二值化），按层从输入到输出逐步将裁剪后的权重和激活替换为硬二值版本，使用随机部分掩码和软刷新机制。提供两个变体：1）完全无STE的训练流程；2）与STE组合，仅对冻结项应用STE。

Result: 无STE模式下，ResNet-50 BNN在CIFAR-10/100/ImageNet上分别提升+18.0/+13.5/+3.8；与STE组合模式下，分别提升+27.1/+19.8/+17.7。该模式在ResNet-18/34/50、MobileNetV2及BERT微调中均有效。发现前向逐层渐进顺序决定性能，反向渐进几乎退化为随机猜测。

Conclusion: 训练二值神经网络的渐进顺序是关键因素：前向逐层渐进可防止深度坍塌，反向渐进则导致性能崩溃。该现象源于激活引起的梯度封锁——已二值化的激活会切断上游梯度流，而渐进顺序控制这些封锁的形成时机。消融实验在无STE环境下进行，确保结论反映渐进机制本身而非与STE的交互作用。

Abstract: Training binary neural networks (BNNs) from scratch is dominated by the straight-through estimator (STE), whose forward/backward mismatch produces severe accuracy degradation as networks deepen. We study an orthogonal axis: when and where binarization is enforced during training. We introduce StoMPP (Stochastic Masked Partial Progressive Binarization), which gradually replaces clipped weights and activations with their hard binary counterparts layer by layer from input to output, using stochastic partial masks with soft refresh. StoMPP delivers two complementary benefits. As a standalone training rule, it provides a fully STE-free procedure that improves over vanilla STE with gains that grow with depth (ResNet-50 BNN: +18.0/+13.5/+3.8 on CIFAR-10/100/ImageNet), and the pattern holds across ResNet-18/34/50, MobileNetV2, and BERT fine-tuning. Composed with surrogate gradients by applying STE only to frozen entries, it reaches +27.1/+19.8/+17.7 over vanilla STE on the same setting. Underlying both regimes is a single mechanistic finding: progression order is decisive. Forward layerwise progression prevents depth collapse, reverse progression collapses to near-chance, and binary-weight networks (without binary activations) are insensitive to order. We trace this asymmetry to activation-induced gradient blockades: a committed binary activation severs gradient flow upstream, and ordering controls when these blockades form. To isolate the progression's contribution from any benefit conferred by STE, we conduct all ablations in the STE-free regime; the resulting characterization (schedule, refresh, ordering, dynamics) thus reflects the progression itself rather than its interaction with surrogate gradients.

</details>


### [187] [Difference of Convex Programming in the Wasserstein Space with Applications to MMD Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27767)
*Clément Bonet,Pierre-Cyril Aubin-Frankowski,Youssef Mroueh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将经典凸凹过程（CCCP）提升到Wasserstein空间，用于处理具有差分之凸（DC）分解结构的概率测度泛函优化问题。针对最大均值差异（MMD）和能量距离（ED）泛函，推导了显式的Wasserstein DC分解，并在光滑性和强凸性假设下证明了迭代的几乎平稳性。实验表明，精心选择的DC分解比Wasserstein梯度下降收敛更快更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein空间上的许多目标泛函（如MMD）沿Wasserstein测地线是非凸的，导致标准一阶方法（如Wasserstein梯度下降）的分析和收敛面临挑战。现有方法缺乏处理这类非凸结构的通用框架。

Method: 将经典凸凹过程（CCCP）推广到Wasserstein空间，针对具有差分之凸（DC）分解的目标泛函设计迭代算法。在凸分量的光滑性和强凸性假设下分析算法的几乎平稳性。特别针对MMD和ED泛函构造了显式的Wasserstein DC分解。

Result: 在理论层面，证明了算法迭代的几乎平稳性；在实验层面，验证了所提DC分解相比Wasserstein梯度下降在MMD目标上收敛更快且更稳定。

Conclusion: 将CCCP提升至Wasserstein空间为处理非凸概率测度优化问题提供了新途径，合理利用DC结构可以显著提升优化性能。

Abstract: Optimizing functionals over the space of probability measures is now ubiquitous in machine learning. A widely used approach is to perform the optimization directly over the Wasserstein space, but many objective functionals of practical interest are non-convex along Wasserstein geodesics, making the analysis of standard first-order methods challenging. In this work, we study a class of objectives over the Wasserstein space that admit a difference-of-convex (DC) decomposition and we lift the classical convex-concave procedure (CCCP) to this setting. Under smoothness and strong convexity assumptions on the convex components of the decomposition, we prove almost stationarity along the iterates of the resulting algorithm. Our main focus is on the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) and the Energy Distance (ED) functionals, for which we develop explicit Wasserstein DC decompositions, and establish local convergence of the scheme under mild assumptions. Empirically, we show that well-chosen DC decompositions yield faster and more stable convergence than Wasserstein gradient descent on these MMD objectives.

</details>


### [188] [NormGuard: Reward-Preserving Norm Constraints in Flow-Matching Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27771)
*Tianlin Pan,Lianyu Pang,Cheng Da,Huan Yang,Changqian Yu,Kun Gai,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RL后训练会导致流式生成模型的每步速度范数膨胀5%-15%，降低感知质量。推理时重归一化无效（因膨胀已与模型权重协同适应），而抑制范数膨胀无明确奖励信号。提出\methodname铰链惩罚项，仅在速度范数超过参考值时激活，可加性组合任意速度局部基损失，在多个设置下提升图像质量和逼真度同时保持奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练虽能提升流式生成模型的奖励对齐，但常导致感知质量退化，且这种退化无法被奖励代理捕获。现有推理时修正方法（如CFG中的速度重缩放）在RL场景下失效，亟需有效的训练时干预策略。

Method: 提出\methodname铰链惩罚项，仅在当前速度范数超过参考模型速度范数时激活，以加性方式与任意速度局部基损失组合，在训练时抑制速度范数膨胀。

Result: 在2个基模型、3种后训练方法（NFT、AWM、DPO）、2个奖励代理上，\methodname一致提升了MLLM评判的图像质量和法医级逼真度，同时保持奖励，且增益在少步推理下更显著，不能被早停解释。

Conclusion: 速度范数膨胀是RL后训练中感知质量退化的结构性信号，训练时通过铰链惩罚抑制膨胀是比推理时修正更有效的策略，\methodname能在保持奖励的同时提升感知质量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reward alignment of flow-based generators, but often degrades perceptual quality in ways that are not captured by the reward proxy. We identify a simple structural signature of this drift: across three post-training methods (NFT, AWM, DPO), RL fine-tuning inflates the per-step velocity norm $\|v_θ\|$ by $5\%$ to $15\%$ relative to the reference. A form of norm inflation has been studied in classifier-free guidance (CFG), where rescaling the velocity back to a reference norm at inference time can mitigate the resulting artifacts. However, this inference-time correction does not transfer cleanly to RL: rescaling $v_θ$ to match $\|v_{\text{ref}}\|$ at inference time neither improves reward nor fixes the quality degradation, because the inflation is co-adapted into the model weights. Furthermore, an adjoint sensitivity analysis shows that velocity magnitude rescaling carries no coherent first-order reward signal at the batch level, indicating that suppressing norm inflation is unlikely to remove a consistently reward-carrying component. Since inference-time renormalization fails while norm suppression carries no reward cost, training-time intervention is the appropriate strategy. Together, these findings motivate \methodname, a hinge penalty that activates only when $\|v_θ\|$ exceeds $\|v_{\text{ref}}\|$ and composes additively with any velocity-local base loss. Across two base models, three post-training methods, and two reward proxies, \methodname consistently improves MLLM-judged image quality and forensic realism while preserving reward, with gains that amplify under few-step inference and are not explained by early stopping.

</details>


### [189] [Parameter Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT) for Network Traffic Prediction via Dynamic Urban Congestion Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28274)
*Abdolazim Rezaei,Mehdi Sookhak,Mahboobeh Haghparast*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出参数高效混合Transformer（PEHT），通过LoRA减少参数量并融合城市移动性与拥塞信息，在网络流量预测任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态城市蜂窝网络中，网络流量受复杂移动模式、拥塞动态和异构用户行为影响，现有预测方法难以兼顾准确性与参数效率。

Method: 基于Transformer架构，将主要网络通信特征与次要城市移动性特征分离；在编码器中引入LoRA减少可训练参数；采用多模态融合策略将外部移动性与拥塞特征注入解码器。

Result: 在Telecom Italia Milan数据集和多个合成拥塞场景下，PEHT在RMSE、MAE和R²指标上均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: PEHT通过参数高效的设计有效融合多模态信息，实现了高精度的网络流量预测，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate network traffic prediction is a critical element for efficient resource allocation in dynamic urban cellular networks. However, prediction remains challenging because network demand is influenced by complex mobility patterns, congestion dynamics, and heterogeneous user behavior. This paper introduces the Parameter-Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT), a network traffic prediction framework that integrates urban mobility and congestion information into a Transformer-based architecture. PEHT separates primary network communication features from secondary urban mobility features and incorporates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) into the Transformer encoder to reduce the number of trainable parameters while maintaining high predictive accuracy. A multimodal fusion strategy then injects external mobility and congestion features into the decoder to improve traffic forecasting. Experiments on the Telecom Italia Milan dataset and multiple synthetic congestion scenarios show that PEHT outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of RMSE, MAE, and $R^2$. The implementation is available in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [190] [Accelerating Hierarchical Sparse Predictive Coding with Hybrid Amortized Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27802)
*Kazuhisa Fujita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了层次稀疏预测编码模型中不同推断方法的效率，比较了四种方案：经典ISTA迭代推断、加速MFISTA、基于LISTA的自顶向下摊销推断，以及混合方法（快速摊销初始化+少量修正迭代）。结果表明，混合方法在重建质量、稀疏性和速度之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次稀疏预测编码模型虽具有计算与神经科学上的优势，但其迭代隐含推断成本高昂，且随层次加深而加剧，限制了实际应用。

Method: 固定层次稀疏能量目标函数，比较四种推断方案：ISTA（经典迭代）、MFISTA（加速迭代）、LISTA式自底向上摊销编码器（结构感知的推断）、混合方法（摊销初始化+少量能量修正迭代）。

Result: 混合方法（浅层LISTA初始化+短修正循环）在重建质量、稀疏性和延迟上优于纯摊销方法，且比长迭代推断快得多。

Conclusion: 快速摊销初始化配合少量能量修正迭代是高效实用的层次稀疏推断策略，能够在计算效率与表征质量之间取得良好折衷。

Abstract: Hierarchical predictive coding provides an interpretable framework for perception as error-driven inference in multi-layer generative models, while sparse coding imposes parsimonious latent representations through explicit sparsity constraints. Their combination yields hierarchical sparse predictive coding models with appealing computational and neuroscientific properties, but practical use is often limited by the cost of iterative latent inference. In such models, each input may require many recurrent refinement steps before a useful sparse representation is obtained, and this burden becomes more severe as the hierarchy deepens. We study this bottleneck by holding the hierarchical sparse energy fixed and varying the inference procedure. The comparison includes four schemes: classical iterative inference based on ISTA, an accelerated MFISTA reference, structurally informed amortized inference using a LISTA-style bottom-up encoder adapted to the hierarchical model, and a hybrid method in which this fast amortized initialization is followed by a small number of corrective energy-based refinement steps. Under this shared objective, we measure reconstruction quality, sparsity, latency, and stability on static image benchmarks. The results show that a shallow LISTA-style initializer plus short corrective recurrence improves over pure amortization while remaining much faster than long iterative inference.

</details>


### [191] [Towards Automating Scientific Review with Google's Paper Assistant Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28277)
*Rajesh Jayaram,Drew Tyler,David Woodruff,Corinna Cortes,Yossi Matias,Vahab Mirrokni,Vincent Cohen-Addad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI与人类在科学评估中协作的四个渐进层级分类体系，并介绍了论文辅助工具PAT——一个能够对科学手稿进行深度评审和验证的智能体AI框架，在数学错误检测上相比零样本方法提升34%召回率，并在STOC和ICML两大CS顶会作为预提交工具成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人类同行评审无法匹配AI辅助科学带来的论文产出增速，为了缓解这一系统性的评审瓶颈，需要部署AI来加速验证和评审过程本身。

Method: 提出AI-人类协作科学评估的四级分类体系（taxonomy）；构建了名为PAT的智能体AI框架，采用推理缩放技术（inference scaling techniques）进行深度科学评审，包括检查理论结果、验证实验、提出改进建议和识别潜在缺陷。

Result: 在SPOT基准上，PAT在数学错误检测上比零样本召回率提升34%；在STOC和ICML两大CS顶会的试点部署中，成功识别出关键错误并提出实质性改进建议。

Conclusion: PAT能在早期捕获错误，减轻审稿人的认知负担，同时保留审稿人对评审结果的控制权，是AI加速科学验证与评审过程的有效步骤。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is driving a revolution in scientific discovery, accelerating everything from hypothesis generation to mathematical theorem proving. However, this rapid acceleration is creating a systemic challenge: traditional human peer review cannot scale to match the influx of AI-assisted science. Ultimately, to resolve this tension, we must also deploy AI to accelerate the verification and review process itself. To frame the discussion around this transition, we propose a taxonomy consisting of four progressive levels of AI-human collaboration in scientific evaluation, and discuss various trade-offs involved with each.
  As a step toward this future, we introduce the Paper Assistant Tool (PAT), an agentic AI framework built for deep scientific review and verification. PAT ingests full scientific manuscripts and produces a comprehensive evaluation, checking theoretical results, validating experiments, suggesting improvements, and identifying potential flaws. By utilizing inference scaling techniques, PAT is able to identify deeper issues than a single model call alone, achieving a 34% improvement over zero-shot recall on mathematical errors in the SPOT benchmark. Pilot deployments of PAT as a pre-submission tool for authors at two major Computer Science conferences -- STOC and ICML -- demonstrate its ability to identify critical errors and suggest substantive improvements to research papers. By catching errors early, PAT eases the cognitive burden placed on referees, while preserving their control over the outcomes of the review process.

</details>


### [192] [USAD: Uncertainty-aware Statistical Adversarial Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27832)
*Zhijian Zhou,Xunye Tian,Jiacheng Zhang,Zesheng Ye,Yiyi Guo,Donghao Zhang,Liuhua Peng,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出USAD方法，通过方差差异（VD）和扰动协方差差异（PCD）两个新统计量分别捕获对抗样本的全局不确定性和局部不确定性，从而提升统计对抗检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MMD的统计对抗检测方法无法有效捕获对抗样本的特征分布异常（全局不确定性）和扰动不稳定性（局部不确定性），导致检测能力受限。

Method: 提出USAD框架，包含两个新统计量：(1) 方差差异（VD）衡量对抗样本与干净样本特征分布的全局不确定性差异；(2) 扰动协方差差异（PCD）通过高斯扰动下的特征协方差变化捕获局部不确定性差异。两者聚合进行检测。

Result: 在各种对抗攻击下，USAD相比基线方法取得了更优的检测性能。

Conclusion: 考虑对抗样本的特征性行为（全局与局部不确定性）对于有效进行统计对抗检测至关重要。

Abstract: Statistical adversarial detection (SAD) treats detection as a two-sample test. Given a reference set of clean examples (CEs) and a batch of queries, potentially containing an unknown mixture of CEs and adversarial examples (AEs), SAD decides whether the query distribution drifts away from the CE distribution while controlling the false-alarm rate. Existing SAD-based methods mainly use maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) to measure the distributional discrepancy. However, MMD's distributional properties limit its ability to capture characteristic uncertainty patterns of AEs that are crucial for detection: AEs typically exhibit abnormal feature spread (i.e., global uncertainty) and instability under perturbations (i.e., local uncertainty). To close the gap, we propose Uncertainty-aware Statistical Adversarial Detection (USAD), which explicitly captures these uncertainty patterns with two new statistics: (1) Variance Discrepancy (VD), which measures the difference in feature spread between AEs and CEs to capture global uncertainty differences. (2) Perturbation-based Covariance Discrepancy (PCD), which compares feature covariance under Gaussian perturbations to capture local uncertainty differences. By aggregating VD and PCD, USAD achieves superior detection performances over baseline methods against various adversarial attacks, highlighting the importance of considering characteristic behaviors of AEs for effective SAD. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/USAD.

</details>


### [193] [FlexMoE: One-for-All Nested Intra-Expert Pruning for MoE Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27866)
*Fan Mo,Yuxuan Han,Geng Zhang,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将预训练MoE大语言模型转化为嵌套可部署子网络家族的方法，通过专家通道重要性排序和离散动作剪枝，在单一训练过程中生成不同预算下的子网络，支持实时在线预算切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE压缩方法多为固定预算优化，无法适应不同设备、用户和工作负载下变化多样的部署预算限制，缺乏一个能从单一预训练模型生成多预算可部署子网络的灵活方案。

Method: 先对专家FFN通道进行重要性排序，然后让每个专家学习离散动作来剪枝其通道。通过逐渐增加成本压力，单次动作训练从高预算到低预算导出一系列动作掩码，每个掩码对应一个嵌套在排序基模型中的可靠小子网络。在中预算(40%)处进行一次恢复微调，将恢复后的模型迁移到其他未见预算。

Result: 在Qwen2-57B-A14B上，即使不微调，剪枝50%路由专家参数仍保留约99.8%的基础性能。剪枝子网络实现了真实内存减少和吞吐量提升，并支持通过内核级协同设计实现实时在线预算切换。

Conclusion: 该框架超越了近期MoE压缩基线，提供了一种灵活、多预算适配的MoE模型部署方案，显著降低了存储和服务开销。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models scale model ability with sparsely activated experts, making this architecture a standard recipe for modern large models. However, sparse activation does not remove the deployment burden of storing and serving all experts, and the available deployment budget can vary substantially across devices, users, and workloads. Existing MoE compression methods are still largely fixed-budget, typically optimizing one compressed endpoint at each chosen target budget. We study a different setting: converting a large pretrained MoE LLM into a nested family of deployable subnetworks across budgets. Our method first ranks expert FFN channels by their importance, then lets each expert learn a discrete action to prune its channels. By gradually increasing cost pressure, a single action-training run exports a series of action masks from high to low budgets, each of which identifies a reliable smaller subnetwork nested in the ranked base model. Moreover, we use a single recovery fine-tune at a mid pruning budget (40%) to recover degraded model quality and transfer the recovered model to other unseen budgets. Overall, our framework surpasses recent MoE compression baselines. Specifically, on Qwen2-57B-A14B, our method retains ~99.8% of base performance while pruning 50% of routed expert parameters even without fine-tuning. For deployment, our pruned subnetworks deliver real memory reduction and throughput gains, and further support realtime online budget switching with kernel-level co-design.

</details>


### [194] [A Comparison of Fusion Techniques for Multi-Modal Human Activity Recognition on the HARMES Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27886)
*Ahmed Mohamady,Robin Burchard,Kristof Van Laerhoven*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了七种多模态传感器融合方法在HARMES数据集上的表现，结果表明门控多模态融合方法（Gated Multi-modal Fusion）以0.82的宏F1分数超越了传统的拼接式后期融合基线（0.76），验证了其在人类活动识别任务中的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态深度学习在可穿戴传感器的人体活动识别中表现出优于单模态的性能，且已有多种融合范式被提出，但目前缺乏在统一基准数据集上对这些范式进行头对头系统比较的研究。

Method: 在包含61小时完全标注的IMU、音频和环境湿度数据的HARMES数据集上，将七种最新的传感器融合方法应用于一个先进的多模态模型架构，采用留一参与者交叉验证进行评估。

Result: 门控多模态融合（Gated Multi-modal Fusion）取得了最高的宏F1分数0.82，比基于拼接的后期融合基线（0.76）提升了6个百分点。

Conclusion: 门控多模态融合在ADL识别任务中显著优于其他融合方法，所有实验代码已公开。

Abstract: Recent advances in Human Activity Recognition (HAR) from wearable sensors have shown that multi-modal deep learning models consistently outperform their uni-modal counterparts. Modalities can include IMUs, RGB cameras, audio signals, and others. One important aspect of multi-modal deep learning is the sensor fusion approach we apply. Over recent years, multiple fusion paradigms have been proposed for multi-modal HAR. However, to the best of our knowledge, no head-to-head comparison of these paradigms exists on a common multi-modal HAR benchmark dataset. To address this research gap, we systematically compare seven state-of-the-art sensor fusion methods on the recently released HARMES dataset, which comprises 61 hours of fully labeled IMU, audio, and ambient humidity data. The chosen dataset focuses on 15 household and personal hygiene activities of daily living (ADLs). By applying the seven different fusion techniques to a state-of-the-art multi-modal model architecture, we show that Gated Multi-modal Fusion achieves the highest macro F1-score (0.82), surpassing the concatenation-based late fusion HARMES paper baseline of 0.76 by +6pp under leave-one-participant-out evaluation. All code used in our experiments is made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [195] [TA-SparseMG: Trend-Aware Sparse Forecasting via Multi-Scale Gating for Long-Term Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27908)
*Wenchao Liu,Hongbing Wang,Youji Zhu,Xiaodong Liu,Xiangguang Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TA-SparseMG，一种基于SparseTSF框架的轻量级跨周期时间序列预测模型，通过趋势感知归一化、尺度自适应门控去噪和多尺度门控注意力MLP三个模块，在多个长期预测基准上实现稳定优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期时间序列预测面临统计非平稳性、局部高频扰动和跨周期耦合依赖等挑战，轻量模型难以在参数效率和预测性能之间取得平衡。

Method: 在SparseTSF稀疏跨周期建模框架基础上，集成三个核心模块：(1) 趋势感知可逆实例归一化模块，捕捉输入窗口统计量并校准预测窗口分布；(2) 尺度自适应门控去噪模块，在周期重排前进行特征平滑和残差抑制；(3) 多尺度门控注意力MLP预测模块，通过条件门控和特征调制增强预测头的自适应表征能力。

Result: 在多个长期时间序列预测基准上，TA-SparseMG一致地实现了优越且稳定的性能表现。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实每个模块都能独立提升分布适应能力、输入鲁棒性和跨周期特征映射能力，表明三个模块均有效贡献于整体性能提升。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting finds extensive applications in domains such as power demand, traffic flow, meteorological observation, and renewable energy dispatch. Forecasting dynamically varying long-term time series poses inherent challenges, including statistical nonstationarity, local high-frequency disturbances, and coupled cross-period dependencies, which make it difficult for lightweight models to balance parameter efficiency and forecasting performance. To address this issue, this study presents TA-SparseMG, a lightweight cross-period forecasting model built on SparseTSF's sparse cross-period modeling framework. It incorporates three key modules: a trend-aware reversible instance normalization module, a scale-adaptive gated denoising module, and a multiscale gated-attention MLP forecasting module. The trend-aware normalization module captures input-window statistics and calibrates forecast-window distributions, effectively mitigating distribution shift. The scale-adaptive gated denoising module performs feature smoothing and residual suppression before period rearrangement, thereby reducing interference from high-frequency perturbations. The multiscale gated attention prediction module strengthens the prediction head's adaptive representational capacity via conditional gating and feature modulation. Extensive experiments across multiple LTSF benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed TA-SparseMG consistently achieves superior, stable performance. Ablation studies confirm that each module independently improves distribution adaptation, input robustness, and cross-period feature mapping capability.

</details>


### [196] [Graph Dimensionality Reduction for Contextual Bandits: Structure-Specific Regret Bounds under Approximate Smoothness and Noisy Eigenspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27917)
*Joyanta Jyoti Mondal,Ibne Farabi Shihab,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphDR-LinUCB算法，将图结构臂的特征投影到低频谱子空间，将维度依赖从d降至k，证明了首个月O(k√T)的遗憾界，并在多个真实数据集上将累积遗憾降低15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构臂的上下文赌博机问题中，标准降维方法忽略了图结构，导致探索成本膨胀d/k倍，需要一种能利用图结构的降维方法。

Method: 将臂特征投影到图的低频谱子空间，在该k维空间中运行线性UCB算法；通过扰动分析扩展到带噪图，并引入奖励平滑不匹配和图估计误差的显式惩罚项。

Result: 证明了首个月O(k√T)遗憾界；在合成基准和六个真实数据集上，累积遗憾比全维LinUCB降低15倍，在五个数据集上优于其他图感知方法。

Conclusion: 高频奖励成分的实际代价取决于其沿博弈路径的实现影响而非总能量，因此无需最坏情况下的线性T惩罚；谱子空间对齐度（Γ_k）可预测降维器的胜负。

Abstract: Contextual bandits with graph-structured arms arise in recommendation, citation retrieval, and social advertising, where arms connected on a graph tend to share reward signal. Standard dimensionality reduction ignores this structure, inflating exploration cost by a factor of $d/k$. We propose GraphDR-LinUCB, which projects arm features onto the graph's low-frequency spectral subspace and runs linear UCB in the resulting $k$-dimensional space. We prove the first $\wtO(k\sqrt{T})$ regret bound for spectral-projection-based contextual bandits, reducing dimension dependence from $d$ to $k$; a perturbation argument extends this to noisy graphs, with an explicit penalty for reward-smoothness mismatch and graph-estimation error. Our central theoretical finding is that the high-frequency reward component need not incur a worst-case linear-in-$T$ penalty: its actual cost depends on its realized impact along the played path, not on its total energy. A simple spectral comparison between subspaces ($Γ_k$) predicts which reducer wins on a given dataset, correctly calling five of six real-dataset outcomes without any fitted threshold. Across a synthetic benchmark and six real datasets (MovieLens, Amazon, LastFM, ogbn-arxiv, MIND), GraphDR-LinUCB reduces cumulative regret by $15\times$ over full-dimensional LinUCB and outperforms competing graph-aware methods on five of six; the single failure is precisely where the graph's spectral subspace is misaligned with the reward.

</details>


### [197] [RECAST: Model Reconstruction via Counterfactual-Aware Wasserstein Geometry under Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27948)
*Xuan Zhao,Lena Krieger,Zhuo Cao,Arya Bangun,Hanno Scharr,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RECAST是一种基于Wasserstein重心原型的替代模型方法，能在有限数据和受限访问条件下，通过反事实感知的Wasserstein优化实现黑箱模型的高保真重建，无需在线查询，并支持公平性审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反事实解释的黑箱模型重建方法存在决策边界偏移、过拟合以及需要在线查询访问等限制，亟需一种在有限数据和受限访问条件下仍能保持高保真度的替代模型方法。

Method: 提出RECAST框架，采用Wasserstein重心原型构建行为替代模型，将反事实样本作为信息性（尽管代表性较低）样本纳入两类，通过Wasserstein优化处理决策边界偏移问题，无需在线查询即可完成重建。

Result: 在真实世界数据集和多种设置下的实验表明，RECAST在高保真度、查询效率方面表现优异，即使在访问受限和存在噪声的情况下也能保持稳定的结果。

Conclusion: RECAST有效解决了低样本和受限访问条件下的黑箱模型重建问题，并具备系统性的群体公平性诊断能力，为第三方审计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CFs) help understand machine learning models by identifying minimal input changes that would lead to alternative model outcomes. Recent work demonstrates their utility for reconstructing black-box models, enabling third-party auditing of opaque decision systems for fairness and accountability. Still, CF-based reconstruction may suffer from decision boundary shifts, overfitting, and restrictive assumptions requiring online query access to target platforms. We propose REconstruction via Counterfactual-Aware waSserstein opTimization (RECAST) under limited data and restricted access, a behavioral surrogate model based on Wasserstein barycentric prototypes. Our approach addresses decision boundary shifts by incorporating CFs as informative, though less representative, samples for both classes, maintaining high surrogate fidelity in low-sample regimes without requiring online access during reconstruction. To enhance fairness auditing, our method enables systematic group fairness diagnostics. Experiments on real-world datasets and various setups show that RECAST effectively achieves high fidelity and query efficiency, as well as stable results even when the access is limited and noisy.

</details>


### [198] [Dual-Learning based Penalized Multi-Align Clustering for Multi-View Incomplete and Disorderly Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27984)
*Liang Zhao,Shubin Ma,Bo Xu,Qingchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态数据融合中存在的模态缺失、时间不同步及类别数据量差异大等问题，提出了一种基于双学习的惩罚性多对齐聚类模型（DLPMAC），通过双学习机制保持跨模态语义一致性和结构相似性，并利用惩罚性多对齐模块实现样本级的多对多数据对齐，有效提升了数据对齐与融合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合方法在对齐聚类中心后无法保证样本级别的精确对齐，且未解决不同类别数据量差异显著的问题，影响后续融合性能。

Method: 提出DLPMAC模型，包含双学习机制（从各模态学习语义和结构先验知识）和惩罚性多对齐模块（通过惩罚机制实现样本级多对多数据对齐，防止数据聚合）。

Result: 实验结果表明DLPMAC在从采样和聚类两个角度解决数据对齐与融合挑战方面具有有效性。

Conclusion: DLPMAC通过双学习机制保持跨模态语义一致性和结构相似性，并利用惩罚性多对齐模块提升了数据对对齐精度，有效解决了多模态数据对齐与融合问题。

Abstract: Multimodal feature fusion can effectively capture complex patterns in real-world data by integrating complementary information from different modalities. However, in many applications, such as boiler combustion monitoring, equipment failure, inconsistent sensor sampling frequencies, and network delays often cause missing modalities and temporal asynchrony. These issues lead to incomplete and disorderly multimodal data. To address them, previous studies have proposed several data fusion methods that align cluster centers before fusion. However, these methods have two key limitations. First, they cannot guarantee accurate sample-level alignment of data pairs. Second, they do not address significant discrepancies in data sizes across different classes, which may affect subsequent fusion performance.
  To address these problems, we propose a dual-learning based penalized multi-align clustering model, named DLPMAC. The dual-learning mechanism enables the model to learn prior knowledge from each modality, including semantic and structural information. This helps preserve semantic consistency and structural similarity across modalities at both local and global levels. In addition, the penalized multi-align module performs multi-to-multi data alignment through a penalty mechanism. It allows one sample to form data pairs with different samples from other modalities, thereby improving data-pair alignment accuracy. The penalty mechanism also prevents data aggregation, avoiding the case where excessive samples are linked to a single sample. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of DLPMAC in addressing data alignment and fusion challenges from both sampling and clustering perspectives.

</details>


### [199] [Fair Classification with Efficient and Post-hoc Controllable Fairness-Accuracy Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28097)
*Maaya Sakata,Kazuto Fukuchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新型公平分类算法，通过梯度优化学习有效特征表示，实现后处理公平分类器的权衡效率，无需重训即可达到与处理中方法相当或更优的公平-准确权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后处理方法具有事后可控性但准确率下降严重，而处理中方法权衡效率高但每次调整权衡比都需要昂贵的重训。需要兼顾事后可控性和高效权衡。

Method: 提出一种基于梯度优化的公平分类算法，通过学习有效的特征表示来提升后处理公平分类器的权衡效率。

Result: 在真实数据集上，该方法实现了与处理中方法相当甚至更优的权衡效率，且无需任何重训。

Conclusion: 该方法同时实现了事后可控性和高效权衡，弥补了现有后处理方法和处理中方法各自的不足。

Abstract: Post-hoc controllability of fair machine learning models, the ability to control the trade-off between fairness and accuracy after training, is valuable for practical deployment. Existing post-processing methods provide such post-hoc controllability but often suffer from significant accuracy degradation, whereas in-processing methods achieve efficient trade-offs but require computationally expensive retraining for each change in trade-off ratio. To achieve both post-hoc controllability and efficient trade-offs, we propose a novel fair classification algorithm that learns effective feature representations to improve the trade-off efficiency of post-processing fair classifiers, by a gradient-based optimization approach. Experimental results on real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves trade-off efficiency comparable to, or even surpassing, in-processing methods, without requiring any retraining.

</details>


### [200] [When One Adapter Speaks for Many: Discovering Low-Rank Redundancy in Continual Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28117)
*Tanguy Dieudonné,Giulia Lanzillotta,Enis Simsar,Louis Barinka,Thomas Hofmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiteLoRA提出一种门控机制，在持续学习中动态决定是重用已有低秩适配器还是创建新适配器，将活跃适配器数量减少20-70%的同时保持或超越现有性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习（CL）中使用LoRA时默认每个新任务都需要独立适配器，但作者发现不同任务的LoRA适配器之间存在显著的低秩冗余，即不同任务学习的子空间高度重叠，早期适配器已能很好表示后续任务。

Method: 提出LiteLoRA，一种即插即用的门控机制，在训练时学习决定是激活新的LoRA适配器还是复用已有的低秩表示，通过选择性学习实现稳定性与可塑性的平衡。

Result: 在标准CL基准上，活跃适配器数量减少20-70%，同时性能匹配或超越现有最先进方法，验证了结构性冗余的普遍性。
